## 1420590
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…私は多くの血を流し過ぎた。<line-break>運命と戦うために全てを犠牲にした…。<line-break>だが…お前は私とは違う。<line-break>お前は救うために戦ってきたはずだ。<line-break>そんな、お前の力を必要としている人が<line-break>いる。<end>"
EN: "I have spilled far too much blood. I sacrificed everything to fight fate. But you are not like me. You have fought to save people. And there are people who need your power.<end>"
KO: "…나는 너무 많은 피를 흘렸다. 운명과 싸우기 위해 모든 것을 희생했지…. 하지만… 넌 나와 달라. 넌 구하기 위해 싸워 왔을 것이다. 그런 네 힘을 필요로 하는 사람이 있다.<end>"

## 1420591
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前はそれでも行くのか。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Knowing that, will you still go, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그래도 갈 셈인가, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1420592
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意外と度胸がないのね。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break> <line-break>自分の力が平和を乱すかもしれない。<line-break>それが恐ろしいのかしら…<end>"
EN: "You have less nerve than I expected, <value:$28>. Are you afraid your power might disturb the peace?<end>"
KO: "의외로 배짱이 없네, <value:$28>. 자신의 힘이 평화를 깨뜨릴지도 모른다는 게 무서운 걸까….<end>"

## 1420593
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>あなたの力は大きいわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Your power is immense.<end>"
KO: "그래. 네 힘은 커.<end>"

## 1420594
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたがその気になれば…<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>この大陸に再び戦渦を引き起こすことも<line-break>できるのでしょうね…。<end>"
EN: "If you chose to, you could likely plunge this continent into war once more.<end>"
KO: "당신이 마음만 먹는다면… 이 대륙에 다시 전쟁을 일으킬 수도 있겠죠….<end>"

## 1420595
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お前には仲間がいる。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>それはお前が正しいことに力を<line-break>振るってきた証であり、<line-break>お前の力を押さえる枷でもある。<end>"
EN: "But you have companions. Their presence proves that you have wielded your power for what is right, and it is also the chain that holds that power in check.<end>"
KO: "하지만 네게는 동료가 있다. 그것은 네가 옳은 일에 힘을 써 왔다는 증거이자, 네 힘을 억누르는 족쇄이기도 하지.<end>"

## 1420596
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこに友がいる限り、その力は破滅を<line-break>招くことはない。<line-break>自分と仲間を信じるがいい。<end>"
EN: "So long as your friends are beside you, that power will not bring ruin. Trust in yourself and your companions.<end>"
KO: "그곳에 친구가 있는 한 그 힘이 파멸을 부르는 일은 없다. 자신과 동료를 믿어라.<end>"

## 1420597
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その人たちに何も告げずに行くなんて、<line-break>許されないわ。<line-break> <line-break>残される方にも、それぞれの思いが<line-break>あるんだから。<end>"
EN: "You can't leave without saying anything to them. Those left behind have feelings of their own.<end>"
KO: "그 사람들에게 아무 말도 하지 않고 가는 건 용납할 수 없어. 남겨지는 쪽에도 저마다의 마음이 있으니까.<end>"

## 1420598
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その辺にしておきなさい。<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>ここから先は<line-break><value:$28>自身が<line-break>決めることです。<end>"
EN: "That is enough. What happens from here is for <value:$28> to decide.<end>"
KO: "그쯤 해 두세요. 여기서부터는 <value:$28> 본인이 결정할 일입니다.<end>"

## 1420599
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々に口を挟む権利はありません。<end>"
EN: "We have no right to interfere.<end>"
KO: "우리에게 참견할 권리는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1420600
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、それでは、<line-break>そろそろあなたの望むところへ<line-break>お連れしましょう。<end>"
EN: "Come, then. It is time I took you wherever you wish to go.<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼 슬슬 당신이 원하는 곳으로 모시겠습니다.<end>"

## 1420601
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間の元に戻るのも、<line-break>新たな世界を求めるのも<line-break>それはあなたの自由なのですから…。<end>"
EN: "You are free to return to your companions or to seek a new world.<end>"
KO: "동료들에게 돌아가든 새로운 세계를 찾든 그건 당신의 자유니까요….<end>"

## 1420602
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さあ、どうぞ。<line-break>あなたが開いた未来への扉は<line-break>一つではありません。<end>"
EN: "Go on. There is more than one door into the future you have opened.<end>"
KO: "…자, 가세요. 당신이 연 미래의 문은 하나뿐이 아닙니다.<end>"

## 1420603
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界は再び、あなたに様々な顔を<line-break>見せてくれるでしょう。<line-break>自由な旅を！<end>"
EN: "The world will once again show you its many faces. Travel freely!<end>"
KO: "세계는 다시 당신에게 여러 모습을 보여 줄 겁니다. 자유로운 여행을!<end>"

## 1420604
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、ここで待っていれば…<end>"
EN: "I-if we wait here...<end>"
KO: "여, 여기서 기다리고 있으면…<end>"

## 1420606
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1420607
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん、なるほどね。<line-break>で、何にも知らない<line-break><value:$28>が通ったら、<line-break>みんなで一気に身柄を確保しようって<line-break>寸法、ねえ<end>"
EN: "Ah, I get it. When an unsuspecting <value:$28> comes by, we all rush in and take our quarry into custody. That's the plan, huh?<end>"
KO: "흠, 그렇군. 그래서 아무것도 모르는 <value:$28>이 지나가면 다 같이 단숨에 붙잡겠다는 셈이군.<end>"

## 1420608
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってるぅ！<line-break>そういうのって、<line-break>まちぶせ、って言うんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "I know this one! It's called an ambush!<end>"
KO: "알아아! 그런 걸 매복이라고 하는 거지.<end>"

## 1420609
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まっ、待ち伏せだなんて、<line-break>人聞きの悪い。<line-break> <line-break>アタクシを、そんな悪事の巻きぞえに<line-break>しないでくれるかしら？<end>"
EN: "A-an ambush? What an awful way to put it. Kindly leave me out of such villainy, would you?<end>"
KO: "매, 매복이라니 듣기 거북하네요. 저를 그런 나쁜 일에 끌어들이지 말아 주실래요?<end>"

## 1420610
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だったらちょっと、<line-break>せまいから、<line-break>どいてほしいんだけど<end>"
EN: "Then could you move over a little? It's cramped in here.<end>"
KO: "그럼 좀 비좁으니까 비켜줬으면 하는데.<end>"

## 1420611
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しっ、誰か来そうよ！<end>"
EN: "Shh! I think someone's coming!<end>"
KO: "쉿, 누가 올 것 같아!<end>"

## 1420614
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "안 오네.<end>"

## 1420615

## 1420616
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと！<line-break>大きな声出すんじゃないわよ、<line-break>馬の尻尾女！<line-break>それで<value:$28>が<line-break>気付いたらどうするのよ！<end>"
EN: "Hey! Keep your voice down, horse-tail woman! What if <value:$28> hears you?<end>"
KO: "잠깐! 큰 소리 내지 마, 말꼬리 여자! 그러다 <value:$28>이 눈치채면 어떡할 거야!<end>"

## 1420617
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぶち壊しってんでしょ？<line-break>あのねー、ぶち壊しも何も、こんなトコ、<line-break><value:$28>は通んないって言ってんの。<line-break>どこの誰？<line-break>こんなトコで張ろうって言ったのはあ<end>"
EN: "You mean I'll ruin it? There's nothing to ruin! I'm telling you, <value:$28> isn't coming through here. Now who was it that said we should lie in wait here?<end>"
KO: "판을 망쳤다는 거지? 있잖아, 망치고 말고도 없이 이런 데는,<value:$28>가 안 지나간다니까. 대체 누구야? 이런 데서 진을 치자고 한 건?<end>"

## 1420618
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティだよぉ。<end>"

KO: "페티야아.<end>"

## 1420619

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이, 이봐!<end>"

## 1420620
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーダメダメ。全くダメよ。<line-break><value:$28>の頭が<line-break>どれだけ切れるか知らないの？<line-break><value:$28>だったら、<line-break>もうとっくに敵…つまり、<line-break>あたしらの存在をかぎつけてるはずよん。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is hopeless. Completely hopeless. Don't you know how sharp <value:$28> is? By now, <value:$28> will have sniffed out the enemy―in other words, us.<end>"
KO: "아, 안 돼 안 돼. 전혀 안 돼. <value:$28> 머리가 얼마나 잘 돌아가는지 몰라? <value:$28>이라면 벌써 적… 그러니까 우리 존재를 눈치챘을 거야.<end>"

## 1420621
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そしておそらく、<line-break>裏をついて、可能な限り<line-break>戦闘を避けようとしてる。<end>"
EN: "And in all likelihood, our quarry is outmaneuvering us and avoiding a fight wherever possible.<end>"
KO: "그리고 아마 빈틈을 찔러 가능한 한 전투를 피하려는 거야.<end>"

## 1420622
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<value:$28>なら、<line-break>それくらいはやってきそう…。<line-break> <line-break>この森には、未開な部分が多いし、<line-break>そこをかいくぐられると…。<end>"
EN: "That does sound like something <value:$28> would do. Much of this forest is still unexplored. If our quarry slips through those parts...<end>"
KO: "그렇군. <value:$28>이라면 그 정도는 해낼 것 같아…. 이 숲은 미개척지가 많으니 그곳으로 빠져나가면….<end>"

## 1420623
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逃げられてしまいます！<end>"
EN: "Our quarry will get away!<end>"
KO: "도망쳐 버립니다!<end>"

## 1420624

## 1420625
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Then let's hurry and go look! Lulluantha wants to see <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그럼 빨리 찾으러 가자아. 루루안타, <value:$28>을 만나고 싶어어.<end>"

## 1420626
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、待って。<line-break>向こうには馬鹿でかい<line-break>蜂の巣があったわ。<line-break>あんなとこは、<line-break>通らないんじゃなくって？<end>"

KO: "하지만 잠깐. 저쪽엔 엄청 큰 벌집이 있었어. 그런 데는 지나가지 않는 거 아니야?<end>"

## 1420627
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へいへい、ご明察。<line-break>その通りでー。<end>"
EN: "Yes, yes, very perceptive. Exactly right.<end>"
KO: "그래그래, 정확하네. 그 말이 맞아.<end>"

## 1420628
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何よあんた！　態度でかいわね！すでに高貴な私と同じラインに<line-break>立っててよ！<end>"
EN: "W-what is with that attitude? You already act as if you stood on the same lofty plane as me!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야 너! 태도가 건방지네! 이미 고귀한 나와 같은 선에 서 있잖아!<end>"

## 1420629
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だってカルラちゃんも<line-break>高貴なんだもんさ！<line-break>しょーがなーいじゃん。<line-break>ねえ、アイリーン？<end>"
EN: "That's because dear Karula is noble too! Can't be helped! Right, Irene?<end>"
KO: "카를라도 귀한 신분이잖아! 어쩔 수 없지 뭐. 그렇지, 아이린?<end>"

## 1420630
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アイリーンは、何か、<line-break>まじめに考えてるよぉ。<end>"

KO: "아이린은 뭔가 진지하게 생각하고 있어.<end>"

## 1420631
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…一理ある。蜂の巣があって、<line-break>危なくて通れそうにない。<line-break> <line-break>しかし、相手がその一般的な認識すら<line-break>利用するとしたら…？<end>"
EN: "There is some sense in that. The beehive makes the route look too dangerous to use. But what if our quarry is exploiting that very assumption?<end>"
KO: "…일리가 있어. 벌집이 있어서 위험해 지나갈 수 없을 것 같아. 하지만 상대가 그 일반적인 인식까지 이용한다면…?<end>"

## 1420632
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえ、もし、すでに私たちの思惑が、<line-break>筒抜けだとしたら…！<end>"
EN: "No―what if our entire plan has already been exposed?<end>"
KO: "아니, 만약 이미 우리의 의도가 전부 새고 있다면…!<end>"

## 1420633
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml

## 1420634

## 1420635
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml

## 1420636

## 1420637

KO: "안 오네….<end>"

## 1420640
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（ぶじに通れてよかったね、<line-break><value:$28>。）<end>"
EN: "(I'm glad you made it through safely, <value:$28>.)<end>"
KO: "(무사히 지나가서 다행이네, <value:$28>.)<end>"

## 1420641
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんともまあ、<line-break>考えようによっては、<line-break>ぜいたくなことじゃな。<line-break>救世の英雄に、見送って<line-break>もらえるのじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "Well, depending on how you look at it, this is quite a luxury. The hero who saved the world is here to see us off.<end>"
KO: "…뭐랄까, 생각하기에 따라서는 호사스러운 일이군. 구세의 영웅에게 배웅받는 것이니.<end>"

## 1420642
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃんに見送ってもらえるのが、<line-break>ぜいたくか…。<line-break>なんか、わかんねえな。<end>"
EN: "Having Sis see us off is a luxury? I don't really get it.<end>"
KO: "누나가 배웅해 주는 게 사치인가…. 뭔가 잘 모르겠네.<end>"

## 1420643
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、<value:$28>、<line-break>これからどうすんだよ？<line-break> <line-break>ホントに俺んちに遊びに<line-break>来ねえのか？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, what are you gonna do now? You really won't come visit my place?<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>, 이제 어떻게 할 거야? 정말 우리 집에 놀러 안 올 거냐?<end>"

## 1420644
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァンはよほど自分の家に<line-break>自信があるんだな。<end>"
EN: "Van must be very proud of his home.<end>"
KO: "반은 자기 집에 꽤 자신이 있나 보군.<end>"

## 1420645
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いーや、その真逆だぜ！<line-break> <line-break> <line-break>最強の冒険者を、<line-break>親父に会わせてやりてえんだ。<line-break>な？　サ、イキョー！<end>"
EN: "No, just the opposite! I want my old man to meet the best adventurer there is. Well? Best be going!<end>"
KO: "아니, 정반대야! 최강의 모험자를 아버지께 보여 드리고 싶은 거라고. 그렇지? 최, 강!<end>"

## 1420646
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最強と、さあ行こう…を<line-break>かけてるんだ。<end>"
EN: "He's playing “the best” off “best be going.”<end>"
KO: "최강과 '자, 가자'를 엮은 말장난이야.<end>"

## 1420647
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…。<end>"
EN: "Hmph...<end>"
KO: "훗….<end>"

## 1420648
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっかましいわい！<end>"
EN: "Oh, shut up!<end>"
KO: "시끄럽다!<end>"

## 1420649
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐお！！<end>"
EN: "Gwah!!<end>"
KO: "크오!!<end>"

## 1420650
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフ…。<line-break>こうしていると、すぐに<line-break>日が暮れてしまいそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Heh... If we carry on like this, the sun will be down before we know it.<end>"
KO: "후후후…. 이러고 있으면 금세 날이 저물 것 같군.<end>"

## 1420651
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、名残惜しい。<line-break>いつかまた、この面々で<line-break>冒険ができるとよいのじゃがな。<end>"
EN: "It truly is hard to part. I hope this same company might adventure together again someday.<end>"
KO: "정말 아쉽군. 언젠가 다시 이 멤버로 모험할 수 있으면 좋겠네.<end>"

## 1420652
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "不可能だろうな。<end>"
EN: "That will probably be impossible.<end>"
KO: "불가능하겠지.<end>"

## 1420653
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラ…。<end>"
EN: "Sera...<end>"
KO: "세라….<end>"

## 1420654
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜ俺たちがここにいるか、<line-break>考えろ。<line-break> <line-break>俺たちは、それぞれが帰る理由と<line-break>場所を抱えているからこそ、<line-break>今、ここにいる。<end>"
EN: "Think about why we're here. Each of us has a reason to return and a place to return to. That is precisely why we're all here now.<end>"
KO: "우리가 왜 여기 있는지 생각해라. 우리는 각자 돌아갈 이유와 장소가 있기 때문에 지금 여기 있는 거다.<end>"

## 1420655
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃそうだけどよお…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I know, but...<end>"
KO: "그야 그렇긴 하지만….<end>"

## 1420656
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、そこは、天運の導きに<line-break>従うまでよ。<line-break> <line-break>考えてみれば、これだけの面々が<line-break>集ったのも、ほとんど偶然のような<line-break>もんじゃからな。<end>"
EN: "Then we can only follow where fortune leads. Come to think of it, this entire company was brought together almost by chance.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그건 천운의 이끄심을 따르면 될 일이지. 생각해 보면 이렇게 많은 사람들이 모인 것도 거의 우연 같은 일이니까.<end>"

## 1420657
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、<line-break>そう言われればそうだ。<line-break> <line-break>みなずいぶん方々から、<line-break><value:$28>に惹かれて<line-break>やってきたのじゃな。<end>"
EN: "True, now that you mention it. Everyone came from far and wide, drawn here by <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 듣고 보니 그래. 모두 꽤 먼 곳에서 <value:$28>에게 이끌려 온 거구나.<end>"

## 1420658
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、そろそろ<line-break>お別れとしようか。<line-break> <line-break>皆、それぞれの方向へと、<line-break>帰らねばならないのだからな。<end>"
EN: "Then I suppose it is time to say farewell. We must all go our separate ways.<end>"
KO: "그럼 슬슬 헤어질 때인가. 모두 각자의 길로, 돌아가야 하니까.<end>"

## 1420659
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、俺とナッジは<line-break>テラネだ。<end>"
EN: "Right. Nadge and I are headed for Terane.<end>"
KO: "그래, 나와 나지는 테라네야.<end>"

## 1420660
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>近いうち、遊びにきてよ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. Come visit soon, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "응. <value:$28>, 조만간 놀러 와.<end>"

## 1420661
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはアルノートゥンだ。<line-break>イズキヤルの墓を作ってやらねばな。<line-break> <line-break><value:$28>も、<line-break>墓参りに来てやってくれい。<end>"
EN: "I am bound for Alnortun. I must build Izkiyal a grave. Come and pay your respects as well, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나는 알노툰으로 간다. 이즈키얄의 무덤을 만들어 줘야지. <value:$28>도 성묘하러 와 주게.<end>"

## 1420662
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私とセラは、ともかく一度、<line-break>ミイスへ向かおうと思う。<end>"
EN: "Sera and I intend to make for Miis first, at any rate.<end>"
KO: "나와 세라는 일단 미이스로 향하려고 한다.<end>"

## 1420663
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "面倒だがな。<end>"
EN: "A nuisance, though.<end>"
KO: "귀찮긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1420664
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはもちろん、<line-break>ドワーフ王国じゃわい。<line-break> <line-break>訪ね来てくれるのであれば、<line-break>でかい炉があるからの、<line-break>いい仕事ができよう。<end>"
EN: "I am returning to the Dwarf Kingdom, of course. If you come to visit, there is a great forge there. We could do some fine work.<end>"
KO: "나는 물론 드워프 왕국이지. 찾아온다면 큰 화로가 있으니 좋은 일을 할 수 있을 게야.<end>"

## 1420665
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、俺はおっさんたちと<line-break>途中まで行くぜ。<line-break> <line-break>姉ちゃんがいつか帰ってくるまで<line-break>ノーブルを守るのは、俺だからな。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll travel part of the way with the old-timers. Until my sister comes home someday, I'm the one who'll protect Noble.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 아저씨들과 중간까지 같이 갈게. 누나가 언젠가 돌아올 때까지 노블을 지키는 건 나니까.<end>"

## 1420666
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出発だな。<end>"
EN: "Time to depart.<end>"
KO: "출발이군.<end>"

## 1420667
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？　行かないのか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Aren't you going?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 안 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1420668
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、わしが代表として言おう。<end>"
EN: "Well, let me speak for everyone.<end>"
KO: "아, 내가 대표로 말하지.<end>"

## 1420669
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>おぬしがいては、<line-break>解散できぬではないか！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, we cannot disband while you are still standing here!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 자네가 있으면 해산할 수가 없지 않나!<end>"

## 1420670
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの時、あなたは神殿で<line-break>こう言ってくれました。<line-break>新しい役目を探しに行こう、と。<line-break>…でも、結局それはどこにも<line-break>見つからなかった…。<end>"
EN: "That day in the temple, you said, “Let's go and find a new role.” But in the end, there was none to be found...<end>"
KO: "그때 당신은 신전에서 이렇게 말해줬죠. 새로운 역할을 찾으러 가자고. …하지만 결국 어디에서도 찾지 못했어요…<end>"

## 1420671
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、悔いなどありません。<line-break>今の私にはどうしても<line-break>欲しいものができたのですから。<end>"
EN: "But I have no regrets. Now there is something I want more than anything.<end>"
KO: "하지만 후회는 없습니다. 지금의 저에게는 반드시 갖고 싶은 것이 생겼으니까요.<end>"

## 1420672
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec142-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノエル用メッセージＶ４終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "노엘용 메시지 V4 끝<end>"

## 1430000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グフトク、Ｖｏｌ１メッセージ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "구프트크 볼륨1 메시지 시작<end>"

## 1430001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここまでたどり着けるとは、<line-break>少しは腕が立つらしいな。<end>"
EN: "You made it this far. Looks like you've got some skill after all.<end>"
KO: "여기까지 도달한 걸 보니 제법 실력이 있나 보군.<end>"

## 1430002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、どうだかな？            <line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>最近じゃ、仕事にあぶれて<line-break>にわか冒険者で食いつないでる奴も<line-break>多いって言うぜ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I don't know about that. I hear plenty of people are out of work these days and scraping by as would-be adventurers.<end>"
KO: "글쎄, 어떨까? 요즘은 일거리가 없어 임시 모험자로 먹고사는 녀석도 많다더군.<end>"

## 1430003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつらも、その口じゃねえのか？<line-break>運がいいだけのあぶれ者さ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Maybe that's all this lot is―jobless drifters who got lucky.<end>"
KO: "이 녀석들도 그런 부류 아닌가? 운만 좋은 떠돌이들이지.<end>"

## 1430004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、ひとつ<line-break>腕前を試させてもらおう。<end>"
EN: "Then let's see what you're made of.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 실력을 한번 시험해 보지.<end>"

## 1430005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりに暴れられる。<line-break>いくぜっ！<end>"
EN: "Been a while since I had a chance to cut loose. Let's go!<end>"
KO: "오랜만에 날뛸 수 있겠군. 간다!<end>"

## 1430006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なかなか、やるじゃないさ。<end>"
EN: "Not bad. Not bad at all.<end>"
KO: "제법 하는데.<end>"

## 1430007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、おかしら！<end>"
EN: "B-Boss!<end>"
KO: "두, 두목!<end>"

## 1430008

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I like you.<end>"
KO: "마음에 들었어.<end>"

KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2너 누구야? 대체 무슨 짓이야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3누구냐, 너는!? 이름을 밝혀라!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5뭐야, 너!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6누구야, 너!<end><if><value:$01><equal>7당신 누구야? 무슨 생각이지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>9너 누구야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11당신들 누구야? 무슨 생각이지!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…네놈, 뭐라도 된 줄 아나?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10뭐야, 너!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14누구냐, 너는!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17너, 뭐라도 된 줄 알아? 인간 주제에 그 오만한 태도! 절대로 용서 못 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…네놈들, 정체가 뭐냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23누구냐?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27누구냐?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26누구냐?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>24대체 무슨 짓입니까?!<end><if><value:$01><equal>25뭐야! 당신 누구야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>21무례한! 너희 누구냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192누구신가요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>193저기… 누구세요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194누구냐?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191당신 누구야? 무슨 용건이지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190너희는 누구냐? 이게 대체 무슨 짓이지!?<end>"

## 1430010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430028

## 1430032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "突然、襲いかかるなんて<line-break>卑怯なやり方だった。それは謝るわ。<line-break>でも、あんたの力を試したかったの。<line-break>あんた、<line-break>…思ったとおりのスゴ腕ね。<end>"
EN: "Attacking you out of nowhere was underhanded, and I apologize. But I wanted to test your strength. You're every bit as skilled as I expected.<end>"

## 1430033

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그 실력을 믿고 부탁 하나 할게.<end>"

## 1430034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワッシャー海賊といえば、<line-break>知る人ぞ知る海の冒険者。<line-break>洋上に恐れるものはなかったわ。<line-break>それが、近ごろじゃ<line-break>船を陸へ上げて<line-break>朝から晩まで砦にこもりっきり。<end>"
EN: "Those in the know once called the Washer Pirates adventurers of the sea. There was nothing we feared on the open water. But lately our ships have stayed ashore, and we've been holed up in the fort from dawn to dusk.<end>"
KO: "와샤 해적이라면 아는 사람은 아는 바다의 모험자들. 바다 위에선 두려울 게 없었지. 그런데 요즘은 배를 육지에 올려 두고 아침부터 밤까지 요새에 틀어박혀 있어.<end>"

## 1430035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どいつもこいつも<line-break>朝っぱらから飲んだくれて…<line-break>こいつらを見れば、わかるわね？<end>"
EN: "Every last one of them starts drinking first thing in the morning. One look at this lot should tell you enough.<end>"
KO: "다들 아침부터 술에 절어서는… 저 녀석들 보면 알겠지?<end>"

## 1430036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかしら、そりゃないっスよ。<line-break>俺たちだって、<line-break>好きでこうしてるわけじゃねえっス。<end>"
EN: "Aw, boss, that's not fair. It's not like we want to live this way.<end>"
KO: "대장, 그건 너무하잖아요. 우리도 좋아서 이러는 건 아니라고요.<end>"

## 1430038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>4うわっ、かなりお酒クサイかも。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>12海賊が陸に上がりっきりとはね。<line-break>何かわけありみたいだな。<line-break>いったい、何があったんだ？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>15海賊が陸に上がりっきり？<line-break>何があったんですか？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19ほんと、お酒くさ～い。<line-break>でも、どうして海へ出ないのぉ？ <end>わっ！　本当に、お酒くさい。<line-break>でも、どうして海へ出ないの？ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>4Whoa, it really reeks of booze.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12Pirates stranded ashore, eh? There must be a story behind that. It really reeks of booze. But why don't you go to sea?<end>"

## 1430039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカよ。<line-break>港近くにまで姿を現して、<line-break>船をかたっぱしから沈めてしまうの。<end>"
EN: "A giant squid. It's been appearing near the harbor and sinking every ship it finds.<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어야. 항구 근처까지 나타나서 배를 닥치는 대로 가라앉혀 버려.<end>"

## 1430040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここらの海じゃ、ワッシャー海賊に<line-break>かなう奴はいなかった。<line-break>俺たちゃ、まさしく洋上の王者だった。<end>"
EN: "No one in these waters could stand against the Washer Pirates. We truly were the kings of the open sea.<end>"
KO: "이 근처 바다에선 와셔 해적을 당할 자가 없었지. 우리는 말 그대로 바다 위의 왕자였어.<end>"

## 1430041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、今じゃあよぉ～、<line-break>イカにおびえて船も出せねえ。<line-break>…これが飲まずにいられるかって。<end>"
EN: "But now we're too scared of a squid even to launch a ship. How can you expect us not to drink?<end>"
KO: "그런데 지금은 말이야~ 오징어가 무서워 배도 못 띄워. …이래서 술을 안 마실 수 있겠냐고.<end>"

## 1430042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その大イカを<line-break>退治してもらいたいの。<end>"
EN: "I want you to kill that giant squid.<end>"
KO: "그 대왕오징어를 퇴치해 줬으면 해.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お金で払いたいのはやまやまだけど、<line-break>今言ったとおり、<line-break>大イカのせいで景気が悪くてね。<end>"
EN: "I'd gladly pay you in coin, but as I said, the giant squid has been bad for business.<end>"
KO: "돈으로 주고 싶은 마음은 굴뚝같지만 방금 말했듯이 큰 오징어 때문에 경기가 나빠서 말이야.<end>"

## 1430053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、どうかしら？<line-break>かわりに、私たちの足…<line-break>つまり船を自由に使えるというのは？<line-break>大イカを退治してくれたら、<line-break>普通の船じゃ立ち入れない、<line-break>危険な海を渡してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "So how about this instead? You may use our means of travel―our ships―as you please. Kill the giant squid, and we'll take you across dangerous waters where ordinary ships cannot go.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 어때? 대신 우리 발… 그러니까 배를 자유롭게 쓸 수 있게 해 주는 건? 대왕오징어를 퇴치해 주면 평범한 배로는 들어갈 수 없는 위험한 바다를 건너게 해 줄게.<end>"

## 1430054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しぶきの群島、闇の門の島、古城…。<line-break>ワッシャー海賊でなきゃ<line-break>渡れない島々へね。<line-break>冒険者にとっては、<line-break>悪い条件じゃないと思うけど？<end>"
EN: "The Spray Archipelago, the Island of the Dark Gate, the Old Castle... islands only the Washer Pirates can reach. Not a bad offer for an adventurer, wouldn't you say?<end>"
KO: "물보라 군도, 어둠의 문의 섬, 고성… 왓샤 해적이 아니면 건널 수 없는 섬들이지. 모험자에겐 나쁜 조건은 아니라고 생각하는데?<end>"

## 1430055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、<line-break>よく考えてみてちょうだい。<line-break>　<line-break>…大イカは手強いわ。<end>"
EN: "In any case, think it over carefully. The giant squid is a formidable foe.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 잘 생각해 봐. …대왕오징어는 만만치 않아.<end>"

## 1430056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行くよ！<end>"
EN: "Let's go!<end>"
KO: "간다!<end>"

## 1430057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へい、おかしら！<end>"
EN: "Right, boss!<end>"
KO: "예, 두목!<end>"

## 1430058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、大イカを退治する気になったら、<line-break>この先の砦に来てちょうだい。<line-break>　<line-break>待ってるわ。<end>"
EN: "If you decide to kill the giant squid, come to the fort up ahead. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어를 퇴치할 마음이 생기면 이 앞의 요새로 와. 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1430059

JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2大イカ退治か…。<line-break>引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せるわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>3大イカ退治のう…。<line-break>引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せるぞ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>6大イカ退治か…。<line-break>引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せるよ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>7引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せるわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>9大イカ退治ねぇ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、<value:$28>が<line-break>助けてやりたいって言うなら<line-break>付き合ってやってもいいけどね。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>11大イカ退治の依頼、<line-break>引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せるわ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>13大イカ退治だと…。<line-break>くだらん。<line-break>時間の無駄だ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14大イカ退治のう…。<line-break>どうしたものか…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>17なんなのよ、あの態度！<line-break>さぁ、帰るのよ、<value:$28>！<line-break>アタクシ、イカなんてまっぴらだわ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>20…どうするかは、<line-break>お前が決めろ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>21ふん、礼儀を知らぬ女だ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>23引き受けるかどうかは、<line-break><value:$28>に任せよう。<end><if><value:$01><equal>26大イカ退治…。<line-break>どうしたものか…。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24大イカ退治ですか…。<line-break>どうなさいますか？<line-break><value:$28>様<end><if><value:$01><equal>25大イカ退治ね…。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192大イカ退治ですか。<line-break>私は貴方の決定に従います。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193大イカ退治…。<line-break>どうするかは<line-break><value:$28>さんに任せます。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194下らない…。<line-break>どうするかは<line-break><value:$28>が決めてくれ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191大イカ退治ね。<line-break><value:$28>がやりたいなら<line-break>付き合ってもいいわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190どうするかは<line-break><value:$28>が決めてくれ。<end>なんか、気に入らねえな。<line-break>カッコつけやがって。 <end>"

## 1430061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Boss, what should we do with this one?<end>"

## 1430062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念ね。<line-break>少しは期待してたんだけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>あんたらに勝てないようじゃ、<line-break>話にならないね。<line-break>森の入り口に捨ててきな。<end>"
EN: "What a shame. I had such hopes, too. But anyone who can't beat the likes of you is no use to me. Dump this one at the entrance to the forest.<end>"
KO: "아쉽네. 조금은 기대했는데…. 너희한테도 못 이길 정도면 말이 안 되지. 숲 입구에 버리고 와.<end>"

EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2What was that about? Those creeps! Next time I see them, they'll pay.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3Who were those brigands? Is this forest their territory? I wonder who those men were.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20I'll make sure to settle this score.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21They gave us quite a beating. Without fail.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26That was dreadful... But who were they?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24Next time... without fail...<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Are you okay? That was awful. Who was that back there?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192Let us return, then.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193Still, those people... What in the world were they?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194I will make them pay for hurting <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Oh, what was that about?! Those people were awful. Next time we meet, I won't let them off!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2정말, 아까 녀석들 뭐야! 다음에 만나면 가만 안 둬.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3아까 그 도적들은 뭐냐? 이 숲이 놈들 영역인가? 하지만 이 빚은 갚아야겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>4어떻게든 <value:$28>도 무사한 것 같네. 숲 깊숙이까지 왔는데… 헛된 하루였을지도.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5젠장! 저 녀석들 뭐야! 다음에 만나면 가만 안 둬!<end><if><value:$01><equal>6너무해, 갑자기 덤벼들다니. 다음에 만나면 용서 안 해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7호되게 당했네. 다음에 만나면 가만 안 둬.<end><if><value:$01><equal>9이야, 완패네. 이 빚은 이자까지 붙여 갚아 줄 테니 각오해.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11당했네. 다음에 만나면 이 빚을 갚아야겠어.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12호되게 당했군. 완패야. 이 빚은 갚아야지. …그런데 놈들 정체가 뭐야?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…이 빚은 반드시 갚는다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10저 녀석들 뭐야. 다음에 만나면 가만 안 둬.<end><if><value:$01><equal>14아까 그 도적들은 뭐냐? 이대로 끝날 거라 생각하면 큰 착각이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15호되게 당했네. 그런데 저 사람들은 대체 누구였을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>17그러니까 이런 숲은 싫다고 했잖아. <value:$28>와 같이 있으면 정말 좋은 일이 없어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19있잖아, 괜찮아? 호되게 당했네. 아까 아저씨들은 누구였을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…이 빚은 반드시 갚아 주겠다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>21호되게 당했군. 하지만 다음엔 이렇지 않을 거다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>23당했군. 이 빚은 갚아야겠다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27…이 빚은 반드시 갚아야겠군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26호되게 당했군…. 그런데 정체가 뭐였지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>24다음엔… 반드시….<end><if><value:$01><equal>25괜찮았어? 호되게 당했네. 아까 그 사람… 누구였을까?<end><if><value:$01><equal>192그럼 돌아가죠.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193그런데 아까 사람들은… 대체 뭐였을까요?<end><if><value:$01><equal>194…<value:$28>를 다치게 한 대가, 반드시 치르게 해 주마.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191정말, 대체 뭐야!? 다음에 만나면 백 배로 갚아 주자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190큭, 호되게 당했군. 놈들은 대체 뭐였지? 언젠가 이 빚은 반드시 갚는다!<end>정말! 너무해, 그 사람들. 다음에 만나면 용서 안 해.<end>"

## 1430065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかしら、こいつどうします？<end>"
EN: "Boss, what should we do with this one?<end>"
KO: "두목, 이 녀석 어쩔까요?<end>"

## 1430067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残念ね。<line-break>少しは期待したんだけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、あんたたちに勝てないようじゃ、<line-break>話にならないわね。<line-break>森の入り口に捨ててきな。<end>"
EN: "What a shame. I had hoped for a little more... But if you can't beat the likes of them, you're no use at all. Dump this one at the entrance to the forest.<end>"
KO: "아쉽네. 조금은 기대했는데… 뭐, 너희가 이기지 못할 정도면 이야기가 안 되지. 숲 입구에 버리고 와.<end>"

## 1430068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来てくれると思っていたわ。<line-break>大イカを退治してくれるのね？<line-break>　<line-break>もう準備はできているのかしら？<end>"
EN: "I thought you'd come. You're going to take care of the giant squid, aren't you? Are you ready?<end>"
KO: "와 줄 줄 알았어. 대왕오징어를 퇴치해 줄 거지? 준비는 다 됐어?<end>"

## 1430069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう少し待って<end>"
EN: "I need a little more time.<end>"
KO: "조금만 더 기다려<end>"

## 1430070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もちろん<end>"
EN: "Of course.<end>"
KO: "물론이지<end>"

## 1430071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったわ。<line-break>準備を調えてから来てちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "All right. Come back when you're ready.<end>"
KO: "알겠어. 준비를 갖추고 다시 와.<end>"

## 1430072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカは手強いわ。<line-break>装備は抜かりないようにね。<end>"
EN: "The giant squid is a tough one. Make sure you're properly equipped.<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어는 강해. 장비를 단단히 챙겨.<end>"

## 1430073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、作戦は立ててあるの。<line-break>聞いて。<line-break>　<line-break>海の中で戦っても<line-break>こっちに勝ち目はないわ。<line-break>だから、大イカを港におびき寄せる。<end>"
EN: "I've already got a plan. Listen. We don't stand a chance fighting it in the water, so we'll lure the giant squid into the harbor.<end>"
KO: "이미 작전은 세워 뒀어. 들어. 바다에서 싸워서는 승산이 없어. 그러니 큰 오징어를 항구로 유인하는 거야.<end>"

## 1430074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "海面で音を立てれば、<line-break>すぐに寄ってくるはず。<line-break>そこまでは、私らでやるわ。<line-break>大イカが港に上がってきたら<line-break>あとは、あんたたちの仕事よ。<line-break>大イカの息の根を止めてちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "If we make enough noise on the surface, it should come straight to us. We'll handle that part. Once the giant squid comes into the harbor, the rest is up to you. Finish it off.<end>"
KO: "바다 표면에서 소리를 내면 금방 다가올 거야. 거기까지는 우리가 할게. 큰 오징어가 항구로 올라오면 그다음은 너희 일이야. 숨통을 끊어 줘.<end>"

## 1430075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お呼びっスか？　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "You called?<end>"
KO: "부르셨습니까?<end>"

## 1430076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "지금 당장 대왕오징어를 항구로 유인해.<end>"

## 1430077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいから、さっさと大イカを<line-break>港に誘い出すんだよ！<line-break>　<line-break>あんたひとりで無理なら、<line-break>他の飲んだくれどもを<line-break>たたき起こしな！<end>"
EN: "Enough! Get out there and lure that giant squid into the harbor! If you can't do it alone, go wake up the rest of those drunks!<end>"
KO: "됐으니까 빨리 대왕오징어를 항구로 유인해! 혼자 못 하겠으면 다른 술꾼들도 깨워!<end>"

## 1430081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へ、へい！　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Y-Yes!<end>"
KO: "예, 예!<end>"

## 1430082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワッシャー海賊の命運が<line-break>かかってるんだ。<line-break>失敗は許さないよ！<end>"
EN: "The fate of the Washer Pirates is riding on this. You'd better not fail!<end>"
KO: "왓샤 해적의 운명이 걸려 있어. 실패는 용납하지 않아!<end>"

## 1430083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫、あいつらだって海の男さ。<line-break>大イカをおびき出すくらいのことは<line-break>簡単にやってのけるよ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry. They're men of the sea. Luring out a giant squid will be easy for them.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, 녀석들도 바다 사나이들이야. 대왕오징어를 유인하는 정도는 쉽게 해낼 거야.<end>"

## 1430084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そういや、<line-break>まだ名前を聞いてなかったね。<line-break>教えてもらえる？<end>"
EN: "Say, I never got your name. Mind telling me?<end>"
KO: "…그러고 보니 아직 이름을 못 들었네. 알려 줄래?<end>"

## 1430085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遅いね…。<end>"

KO: "늦네….<end>"

## 1430087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1430088

## 1430089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "港で大イカがお待ちかねだぜ。<end>"

## 1430090

## 1430091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカを港におびき寄せました。<line-break>あとは頼みます。<end>"
EN: "The giant squid's been lured into the harbor. The rest is up to you.<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어를 항구로 유인했습니다. 나머지는 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1430092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカは港にいる。<line-break>早いとこ退治してくんな。<end>"
EN: "The giant squid's in the harbor. Hurry up and kill it, will ya?<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어는 항구에 있어. 얼른 퇴치해 줘.<end>"

## 1430093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカを倒すまで<line-break>この砦からは出さないぜ！<end>"

KO: "대왕오징어를 쓰러뜨릴 때까지 이 요새에서 못 나간다!<end>"

## 1430094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、頼んだよ、<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "All right, I'm counting on you, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1430095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やった！<line-break>これでまた海に出られるぜ！<end>"
EN: "We did it! We can finally get back out to sea!<end>"
KO: "해냈다! 이걸로 다시 바다로 나갈 수 있어!<end>"

## 1430096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の目に狂いはなかった。<line-break>さすがだね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I knew I hadn't misjudged you. Nicely done, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "내 눈은 틀리지 않았어. 역시 대단하네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1430097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、早いとこ船を港に戻しな。<end>"

## 1430098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へい、おかしら！<end>"
EN: "Right, boss!<end>"
KO: "예, 두목!<end>"

## 1430099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助かったわ、<value:$28>。<line-break>おかげで、また船を出せる。<line-break>秘宝探しに出られるわ。<end>"
EN: "You really helped us out, <value:$28>. Thanks to you, we can set sail again and get back to searching for hidden treasure.<end>"
KO: "살았어, <value:$28>. 덕분에 다시 배를 띄울 수 있어. 비보를 찾으러 갈 수 있겠네.<end>"

## 1430100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私らは、海賊って言っても<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてる人間から<line-break>金を巻き上げたりはしてないわ。<end>"
EN: "We may be pirates, but we don't go around shaking down honest folk.<end>"
KO: "우리는 해적이라고 해도 정직하게 일하는 사람들에게 돈을 뜯지는 않아.<end>"

## 1430101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言ってみれば、海の冒険者ね。<line-break>人知れず眠っている宝を求めて<line-break>島々をめぐっているの。<line-break>かつて海賊が隠した<line-break>財宝とかをさ…。<end>"
EN: "You might call us seafaring adventurers. We travel from island to island in search of treasure no one knows is there―things like hoards hidden by pirates long ago.<end>"
KO: "말하자면 바다의 모험자지. 아무도 모르게 잠든 보물을 찾아 섬들을 돌아다녀. 옛 해적이 숨긴 보물 같은 걸 말이야….<end>"

## 1430102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束どおり、これからは、<line-break>あんたたちのためにも<line-break>船を出してあげるわ。<line-break>埠頭にいる船番に頼んでちょうだい。<line-break>いつでも好きな場所へ<line-break>連れていってあげる。<end>"
EN: "As promised, from now on we'll give you passage too. Just speak to the shipkeeper at the wharf. We'll take you wherever you like, whenever you like.<end>"
KO: "약속대로 이제부터는 너희를 위해서도 배를 띄워 줄게. 부두의 뱃사공에게 부탁해. 언제든 원하는 곳으로 데려다줄 거야.<end>"

## 1430103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しぶきの群島、古城、<line-break>闇の門の島について知りたければ、<line-break>また、私の所に来て。<line-break>今度、話してあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "If you want to know about the Spray Archipelago, the Old Castle, or the Island of the Dark Gate, come see me again. I'll tell you about them next time.<end>"
KO: "물보라 군도, 고성, 어둠의 문 섬에 대해 알고 싶으면 다시 나를 찾아와. 다음에 이야기해 줄게.<end>"

## 1430104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>じゃない。<line-break>私に何か用？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't <value:$28>. Do you need something from me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>잖아. 나한테 무슨 볼일이야?<end>"

## 1430105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の門の島とは？<end>"
EN: "What is the Island of the Dark Gate?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 문 섬이란?<end>"

## 1430106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "古城とは？<end>"
EN: "What is the Old Castle?<end>"
KO: "고성이란?<end>"

## 1430107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しぶきの群島とは？<end>"
EN: "What is the Spray Archipelago?<end>"
KO: "물보라 군도란?<end>"

## 1430108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また今度<end>"
EN: "Maybe next time.<end>"
KO: "다음에<end>"

## 1430109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "島の真ん中に、隕石が落ちたような<line-break>大きな穴があるの。<line-break>穴の底は深くて、まったく見えないわ。<line-break>きっとそれが、<line-break>闇の門という名の由来ね。<end>"
EN: "There's a huge crater in the middle of the island, as if a meteor struck it. It's so deep you can't see the bottom at all. That must be where the name Dark Gate comes from.<end>"
KO: "섬 한가운데에 운석이 떨어진 것 같은 큰 구멍이 있어. 바닥은 깊어서 전혀 안 보여. 아마 그게 어둠의 문이라는 이름의 유래겠지.<end>"

## 1430110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつだったか、<line-break>あの穴へ降りてみようと思ったけど<line-break>そばまで行って、あきらめたわ。<line-break>あそこには、人の進入を拒む<line-break>何かがあるのよ。<end>"
EN: "I once thought about climbing down into that crater, but I gave up when I got close. There's something there that refuses to let people enter.<end>"
KO: "언젠가 그 구멍에 내려가 보려 했지만 근처까지 가고 포기했어. 저기에는 사람의 침입을 거부하는 무언가가 있어.<end>"

## 1430111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その名のとおり古い城よ。<line-break>気味の悪い城で、<line-break>私もまだ中へ入ったことはないわ。<end>"
EN: "It's an old castle, just as the name suggests. It's eerie, and I haven't been inside yet either.<end>"
KO: "이름 그대로 오래된 성이야. 으스스한 성이라 나도 아직 안에 들어가 본 적은 없어.<end>"

## 1430113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "目の前にイイ女がいるのに…。<line-break>たまには、他のことを聞いたらどう？<end>"
EN: "You've got a fine woman standing right in front of you... Why not ask about something else for a change?<end>"
KO: "눈앞에 좋은 여자가 있는데…. 가끔은 다른 것도 물어보는 게 어때?<end>"

## 1430114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう…。今さら遅いわよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>どうせ、賊を仕切ってる私なんて、<line-break>女には見えやしないだろうしね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, honestly... It's too late now. I suppose a woman who leads a band of outlaws doesn't look much like a woman to you anyway.<end>"
KO: "정말…. 이제 와선 늦었어. 어차피 도적을 이끄는 나 같은 여자는 여자로도 안 보이겠지.<end>"

## 1430115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのよ。<line-break>これが私の使命なんだから。<end>"
EN: "It's all right. This is my mission, after all.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 이게 내 사명이니까.<end>"

## 1430116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい。<line-break>突っかかったりして。<line-break>また、来てちょうだいね。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry for snapping at you. Come and see me again, will you?<end>"
KO: "미안해. 괜히 시비 걸어서. 또 와 줘.<end>"

## 1430117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。待ってるわ。<line-break><value:$28>とは<line-break>いい友だちになれそうね。<end>"
EN: "Yes. I'll be waiting. I think <value:$28> and I could become good friends.<end>"
KO: "응. 기다릴게. <value:$28>와는 좋은 친구가 될 수 있을 것 같아.<end>"

## 1430118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく来たのに、もう帰るって？<line-break>待ってる女でもいるの？<end>"
EN: "You came all this way, and now you're leaving already? Is there a woman waiting for you?<end>"
KO: "모처럼 왔는데 벌써 돌아가겠다고? 기다리는 여자라도 있어?<end>"

## 1430119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、そう。<line-break>それじゃ、好きにしな。<line-break>ほら、さっさと帰りなよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I see. Do whatever you want, then. Go on, get out of here.<end>"
KO: "아, 그래. 그럼 마음대로 해. 자, 얼른 돌아가.<end>"

## 1430120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "憎らしい男だね！<end>"
EN: "You awful man!<end>"
KO: "얄미운 남자네!<end>"

## 1430121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。<line-break>また顔を見せてちょうだい。<line-break>　<line-break>むさ苦しい男どもの相手ばかりじゃ<line-break>気がめいるわ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Come and see me again. Having no one but scruffy men for company gets depressing.<end>"
KO: "그래. 또 얼굴 보여 줘. 칙칙한 남자들만 상대하면 기분이 가라앉거든.<end>"

## 1430122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "元気そうで安心したわ。<line-break>また顔を見せてちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "I'm relieved to see you're doing well. Come and see me again.<end>"
KO: "건강해 보여서 안심했어. 또 얼굴 보여 줘.<end>"

## 1430123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "約束通り、いつでも船は出すよ。<line-break>必要なら船番に話しかけてちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "As promised, I'll send the ship out whenever you need it. Just speak to whoever's tending it.<end>"
KO: "약속대로 언제든 배를 띄워 줄게. 필요하면 선원에게 말해.<end>"

## 1430124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気をつけてね。<end>"
EN: "Take care.<end>"
KO: "조심해.<end>"

## 1430125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカ退治、見事だったよ。<line-break>今度は俺たちが体をはる番だ。<line-break>出航の準備はできてるがどうする？<end>"
EN: "Fine work taking down that giant squid. Now it's our turn to risk our necks. We're ready to set sail. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어 퇴치, 훌륭했어. 이번엔 우리가 몸을 던질 차례다. 출항 준비는 끝났는데 어떻게 할래?<end>"

## 1430126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしく頼む<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "잘 부탁한다<end>"

## 1430127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また今度<end>"
EN: "Maybe next time.<end>"
KO: "다음에<end>"

## 1430128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大イカが港を荒らすもんで、<line-break>船は安全な場所へ移してある。<line-break>　<line-break>まったく…、<line-break>船に乗れない海賊なんて<line-break>さえないものだな。<end>"
EN: "With that giant squid tearing up the harbor, the ship's been moved somewhere safe. Damn. A pirate who can't take to sea is a sorry sight.<end>"
KO: "대왕오징어가 항구를 망쳐서 배는 안전한 곳으로 옮겨 뒀어. 정말… 배를 못 타는 해적이라니 한심하군.<end>"

## 1430129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec143-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、お仲間以外に<line-break>余計なお連れさんがいるんじゃ　　　　<line-break>船には乗せられないぜ。<line-break>素性のわからない人間は<line-break>船に乗せないってのがおきてだからな。<line-break>悪いな。<end>"
EN: "Hold on. If you've got someone tagging along besides your usual companions, I can't let you aboard. We have a rule against taking anyone whose identity we don't know. Sorry.<end>"
KO: "잠깐, 동료 말고 수상한 일행이 하나 더 있으면 배에는 못 태워. 신원 모르는 사람은 태우지 않는 게 규칙이거든. 미안해.<end>"

## 1440001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、俺は究極の生物を作り上げた。<line-break>俺は、奴を生み出すためだけに<line-break>生まれたのかもしれない。<line-break>思えば、すべてが意味がなかった。<line-break>…孤独な人生も、金も…。<line-break>金には、あれほど執着したのにな。<end>"
EN: "Now I have created the Ultimate Life-Form. Perhaps I was born solely to bring it into being. Looking back, it was all meaningless: my lonely life, money... And to think how obsessed I was with money.<end>"
KO: "지금 나는 궁극의 생물을 만들어냈다. 나는 그 녀석을 탄생시키기 위해 태어난 것인지도 몰라. 돌이켜보면 모든 게 의미 없었다. …고독한 인생도, 돈도…. 돈에는 그렇게 집착했는데 말이지.<end>"

## 1440002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それも、結局は<line-break>何にすがればよいかわからないために、<line-break>とりあえず選んだ無難な選択肢だった。<line-break>戦闘モンスター事業の成功も、<line-break>親友ロティに対する反発も、<line-break>リベルダムの陥落も、<end>"
EN: "That too was merely a safe choice I made for want of knowing what else to cling to. The success of my combat-monster business, my rebellion against my best friend Lottie, the fall of Liberdam―<end>"
KO: "그것도 결국 무엇에 의지해야 할지 몰라서 우선 고른 무난한 선택지였어. 전투 몬스터 사업의 성공도, 절친 로티에 대한 반발도, 리벨덤의 함락도,<end>"

## 1440003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人の奴隷になったのも、<line-break>すべてはここに至るまでの<line-break>道標にすぎなかったのだ。<line-break>この俺の小さな身体は、仮の姿。<line-break>お前こそが<line-break>本当の俺だったのかもしれないな。<end>"
EN: "Even becoming the demon's slave―all of it was merely a series of signposts leading me here. This small body of mine is only a temporary form. Perhaps you were my true self all along.<end>"
KO: "마인의 노예가 된 것도 모두 여기까지 오기 위한 이정표에 지나지 않았어. 이 작은 몸은 임시 모습. 어쩌면 네가 진짜 나였는지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1440004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は運命を成し遂げた。<line-break>満足だ。醜く、強大なお前、<line-break>いや本当の自分に会えて…。<end>"
EN: "I have fulfilled my destiny. I am content to have met you, hideous and mighty―or rather, to have met my true self...<end>"
KO: "나는 운명을 이루었다. 만족한다. 추하고 강대한 너, 아니 진짜 나 자신을 만나서….<end>"

## 1440005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いろいろあったが、<line-break>これで俺の苦労も報われる。<end>"
EN: "A lot has happened, but now all my struggles will have been worthwhile.<end>"
KO: "여러 일이 있었지만, 이걸로 내 고생도 보답받겠군.<end>"

## 1440006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今こそ披露しよう、<line-break>これが究極生物エクリプスだ！<end>"
EN: "Now I shall unveil it: Eclipse, the Ultimate Life-Form!<end>"
KO: "지금이야말로 보여 주지. 이것이 궁극생물 이클립스다!<end>"

## 1440007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec144-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、小さい自分が死に、<line-break>大いなる自分が世界を蹂躙する<line-break>クックックッ、ハハハ…！<end>"
EN: "Now my small self dies, and my great self will trample the world underfoot. Heh heh heh... Ha ha ha...!<end>"
KO: "지금 작은 나는 죽고, 위대한 내가 세계를 유린한다. 크크크, 하하하…!<end>"

JP: "グフトク、Ｖｏｌ６メッセージ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "구프트크 볼륨6 메시지 시작<end>"

## 1450001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私が恐い？<end>"
EN: "Are you afraid of me?<end>"
KO: "내가 무서워?<end>"

## 1450002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はワッシャー海賊のおかしらよ。<line-break>裏では、大声では言えないようなことを<line-break>やってるかもしれないわ。<end>"
EN: "I'm the leader of the Washer Pirates. For all you know, I may be doing things behind the scenes that can't be spoken of openly.<end>"
KO: "나는 와셔 해적의 두목이야. 뒤에서는 큰소리로 말하기 힘든 일도 하고 있을지 모르지.<end>"

## 1450003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何がおかしいの！？<end>"
EN: "What's so funny?!<end>"
KO: "뭐가 우스운 거야!?<end>"

## 1450004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いことをしてるなんて思えない<end>"
EN: "I just can't imagine you doing anything bad.<end>"
KO: "나쁜 짓을 하고 있다고는 생각되지 않아.<end>"

## 1450005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません。もう笑いません<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I won't laugh anymore.<end>"
KO: "미안해요. 이제 안 웃을게요.<end>"

## 1450006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…知ったような口、きかないで。<line-break>私のこと、何も知らないくせに…。<end>"
EN: "Don't talk as if you know me. You don't know anything about me...<end>"
KO: "…다 아는 척 말하지 마. 내 이야기는 아무것도 모르면서….<end>"

## 1450007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいわ。<line-break>今日は私の話を聞いてもらう。<line-break>イヤと言っても帰さないから。<end>"
EN: "Fine. Today, you're going to listen to my story. I'm not letting you leave, even if you say no.<end>"
KO: "좋아. 오늘은 내 이야기를 들어 줘야겠어. 싫다고 해도 못 돌아가게 할 거야.<end>"

## 1450008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワッシャー海賊の初代おかしらはね、<line-break>私の父さんだったの。<line-break>名前はカンダカン。<line-break>闇の門の島や古城への海路を<line-break>開いたのも父さんよ。<end>"
EN: "The first leader of the Washer Pirates was my father. His name was Kandakan. He was the one who opened the sea routes to the Island of the Dark Gate and the Old Castle.<end>"
KO: "왓샤 해적의 초대 두목은 내 아버지였어. 이름은 칸다칸. 어둠의 문의 섬과 고성으로 가는 항로를 연 것도 아버지야.<end>"

## 1450009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強くて、勇気があって、<line-break>やさしくて…。<line-break>誰もが父さんを慕っていたわ。　　　<line-break>でも、ある日突然、<line-break>貝の毒にあたって死んでしまったの。<end>"
EN: "He was strong, brave, and kind... Everyone looked up to him. But then, one day, he suddenly died of shellfish poisoning.<end>"
KO: "강하고 용감하고 다정했어…. 모두가 아버지를 따랐지. 그런데 어느 날 갑자기 조개 독에 중독돼 돌아가셨어.<end>"

## 1450010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでひとり娘だった私が<line-break>第２代おかしらに推されたわけ。<line-break>　<line-break>父さんの命、ワッシャー海賊を<line-break>つぶすわけにはいかない。<line-break>だから、やるしかなかったわ。<end>"
EN: "So, as his only daughter, I was chosen to be the second leader. The Washer Pirates were my father's life. I couldn't let them fall apart. I had to do it.<end>"
KO: "그래서 외동딸이던 내가 2대 두목으로 추대된 거야. 아버지의 목숨과도 같던 와셔 해적단을 없앨 순 없었어. 그러니 할 수밖에 없었지.<end>"

## 1450011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな話、退屈？<end>"
EN: "Is this boring you?<end>"
KO: "이런 얘기, 지루해?<end>"

## 1450012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退屈<end>"
EN: "It is boring.<end>"
KO: "지루함<end>"

## 1450013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうでもない<end>"
EN: "Not really.<end>"
KO: "그렇지도 않아<end>"

## 1450014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、終わりにしてあげる。<line-break>だから最後にひとつだけ約束して。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll stop. But first, promise me one thing.<end>"
KO: "그럼 끝내 줄게. 그러니 마지막으로 한 가지만 약속해 줘.<end>"

## 1450015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私と一緒に船に乗ってちょうだい。<line-break>あなたなら、父さんの代わりが務まるわ。<line-break>私より立派なおかしらになれる。<line-break>今すぐでなくてもいいの。<line-break>あなたの冒険に区切りがついたら、<line-break>私と一緒に海を旅してちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "Come aboard my ship. You could take my father's place. You'd make a better leader than I ever could. It doesn't have to be now. Once you reach a stopping point in your adventure, come sail the seas with me.<end>"
KO: "나와 함께 배에 타 줘. 너라면 아버지 대신 충분히 해낼 수 있어. 나보다 훌륭한 선장이 될 거야. 당장 아니어도 괜찮아. 네 모험이 한 고비 끝나면 나와 함께 바다를 여행해 줘.<end>"

## 1450016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ほんと言うとね、<line-break>私、疲れちゃったの。<line-break>海賊なんて向いていないのね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. To tell you the truth, I'm tired. I suppose I'm just not cut out to be a pirate.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 사실은 말이야, 나 지쳤어. 해적 같은 건 나한테 안 맞나 봐.<end>"

## 1450017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、私がワッシャー海賊を　　　　<line-break>辞めたら父さんが悲しむわ。<line-break>だから、それはできない。<line-break>ただ、父さんみたいな人が、<line-break>そばにいてくれたら…、<line-break>そう思うの。<end>"
EN: "But if I left the Washer Pirates, my father would be sad. I can't do that. I just wish I had someone like him by my side...<end>"
KO: "하지만 내가 와셔 해적단을 그만두면 아버지가 슬퍼할 거야. 그래서 그건 못 해. 다만 아버지 같은 사람이 곁에 있어 준다면… 그렇게 생각해.<end>"

## 1450018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ときには私をしかったり、<line-break>励ましたりしてくれる人が…。<end>"
EN: "Someone who would scold me sometimes, and encourage me...<end>"
KO: "때로는 저를 꾸짖고, 격려해 주는 사람이….<end>"

## 1450019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>わからない？<line-break>あなたのこと言ってるのよ。　　　<line-break>今すぐでなくてもいい。<line-break>あなたの冒険に区切りがついたら、<line-break>私と海を旅してほしいの。<end>"
EN: "Don't you understand, <value:$28>? I'm talking about you. It doesn't have to be now. Once you reach a stopping point in your adventure, I want you to sail the seas with me.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>. 모르겠어? 네 이야기야. 지금 당장이 아니어도 돼. 네 모험이 일단락되면 나와 바다를 여행해 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1450020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。いつか、きっと…<end>"
EN: "All right. Someday, for sure...<end>"
KO: "알겠어. 언젠가 꼭….<end>"

## 1450021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなことはできない<end>"
EN: "I can't do that.<end>"
KO: "그럴 수는 없어.<end>"

## 1450022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワッシャー海賊に入ってくれるの？<line-break>ずっと一緒にいてくれるの？<end>"
EN: "Will you join the Washer Pirates? Will you stay for good?<end>"
KO: "왓샤 해적단에 들어와 줄 거야? 계속 같이 있어 줄 거야?<end>"

## 1450023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありがとう。<line-break>ウソでもうれしい。<end>"
EN: "...Thank you. Even if you don't mean it, I'm glad you said it.<end>"
KO: "…고마워. 거짓말이라도 기뻐.<end>"

## 1450024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日はこのくらいで<line-break>勘弁してあげる。<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、行って。<line-break>行ってちょうだい。<end>"
EN: "I'll let you off for today. Now go. Please, just go.<end>"
KO: "오늘은 이 정도로 봐줄게. 자, 가. 가 줘.<end>"

## 1450025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "父さん…、<line-break><value:$28>は冒険を終えて、<line-break>ここへ戻ってくると思う？<line-break>…そうよね。<line-break>きっと帰ってきてくれるわね。<end>"
EN: "Dad... do you think <value:$28> will come back here when the adventure is over? ...Yes, of course. I'm sure of it.<end>"
KO: "아버지… <value:$28>은 모험을 끝내고 여기로 돌아올까? …그렇지. 분명 돌아와 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1450026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、帰すわけにはいかない。<line-break>いい答えをくれるまではね。<line-break>　<line-break>もう一度聞くわ。<line-break>私と一緒に海を旅してくれる？<end>"
EN: "Then I can't let you leave. Not until you give me the answer I want. Let me ask you again. Will you sail the seas with me?<end>"
KO: "그럼 돌려보낼 수 없지. 좋은 대답을 들을 때까지 말이야. 다시 물을게. 나와 함께 바다를 여행해 줄래?<end>"

## 1450027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、私が恐いんじゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>いいわよ、帰してあげる。<line-break>ほら、行きなさいよ。<end>"
EN: "I knew it. You are afraid of me. Fine, I'll let you go. Go on, then.<end>"
KO: "역시 내가 무서운 건 아니구나. 좋아, 돌려보내 줄게. 자, 가.<end>"

## 1450028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、俺は究極の生物を作り上げた。<line-break>俺は奴を生み出すためだけに<line-break>生まれたのかもしれない。<line-break>戦闘モンスター事業の成功も、<line-break>親友ロティに対する反発も、<line-break>リベルダムの陥落も、<end>"
EN: "Now I have created the ultimate life-form. Perhaps I was born solely to bring it into being. The success of my combat-monster business, my rebellion against my best friend Lottie, the fall of Liberdam,<end>"
KO: "지금 나는 궁극의 생물을 만들어 냈다. 나는 녀석을 만들어 내기 위해 태어난 것일지도 몰라. 전투 몬스터 사업의 성공도, 절친 로티에 대한 반발도, 리벨덤의 함락도,<end>"

## 1450029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人の奴隷になったのも、<line-break>すべてはここに至るまでの<line-break>通過点でしかなかったのだ。　　　<line-break>この俺の小さな身体は仮の姿。<line-break>お前こそが<line-break>本当の俺だったのかもしれないな。<end>"
EN: "Even becoming a demon's slave―everything was merely another step on the road to this moment. This small body of mine is only a temporary form. Perhaps you were my true self all along.<end>"
KO: "마인의 노예가 된 것도 모두 여기까지 오기 위한 과정에 불과했던 거야. 이 작은 몸은 임시 모습. 어쩌면 네가 진짜 나였을지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1450030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は運命を成し遂げた。<line-break>満足だ。醜く、強大なお前、<line-break>いや、本当の自分に会えて…。<end>"
EN: "I have fulfilled my destiny. I am content to have met you, hideous and mighty... no, to have met my true self.<end>"
KO: "나는 운명을 이루었다. 만족한다. 추하고 강대한 너, 아니 진짜 자신을 만나서….<end>"

## 1450031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いろいろあったが、<line-break>これで俺の苦労も報われる。<end>"
EN: "A lot has happened, but now all my struggles will have been worth it.<end>"
KO: "여러 일이 있었지만, 이걸로 내 고생도 보답받겠군.<end>"

## 1450032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今こそ披露しよう、<line-break>これが究極生物エクリプスだ！<end>"
EN: "Now I shall unveil it: Eclipse, the Ultimate Life-Form!<end>"
KO: "지금이야말로 보여 주지. 이것이 궁극생물 이클립스다!<end>"

## 1450033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、エクリプスよ！<line-break>絶対的な強さを持つ、本当の俺よ！<line-break>その力で世界をなぎ倒すのだ！！<end>"
EN: "Now, Eclipse! My true self, possessed of absolute strength! Use that power to lay waste to the world!<end>"
KO: "자, 이클립스여! 절대적인 힘을 지닌 진정한 나여! 그 힘으로 세계를 쓸어 버려라!!<end>"

## 1450034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…禁断の聖杯だわ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail...<end>"
KO: "…금단의 성배야.<end>"

## 1450035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついに…<line-break>ついに取り戻したのね。<line-break>　<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "At last... You finally got it back. ...<end>"
KO: "드디어… 드디어 되찾았구나. ……………….<end>"

<end><if><value:$01><equal>21孤独…か…。<line-break>俺も、あの男のようになる<line-break>ところだったのかもしれぬ。　　　<line-break>ほら、禁断の聖杯だ。<line-break>ずっと、探し求めていたのだろう？ <end><if><value:$01><equal>23闇の神器、禁断の聖杯か…。<line-break><value:$28>、受け取れ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>27これが、<line-break>…禁断の聖杯。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>26ほれ、<value:$28>、<line-break>禁断の聖杯じゃ。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>24これが闇の神器、<line-break>禁断の聖杯ですね。<line-break><value:$28>様。<end><if><value:$01><equal>25これが探してた禁断の聖杯ね。<end><if><value:$01><equal>192これが禁断の聖杯ですか。<line-break>どうぞ。<line-break>私には必要のないものです。<end><if><value:$01><equal>193…こ、これが<line-break>闇の神器・禁断の聖杯なんですね…。<line-break>…ごくり。<line-break>…これを使えば、料理ベタな私でも<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>おいしいお料理を作れちゃうんだ…。<line-break>もともとロストールにあったものだし<line-break>生まれも育ちもロストールの私が<line-break>ちょ、ちょっとくらい使ったって…。<line-break>…や、やだなぁ。冗談ですよ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>194闇の神器、禁断の聖杯だ。<line-break>知識は与えてくれるけど、<line-break>知識に溺れない方法は教えてくれない。<line-break>ほら、<value:$28>。<end><if><value:$01><equal>191闇の神器、禁断の聖杯よ。<line-break>…やっぱり闇の神器って恐ろしいわ。<end><if><value:$01><equal>190あったぞ、禁断の聖杯だ。<line-break>ほら、<value:$28>。<line-break>ずっと探していたんだろ？<line-break>あの男も、こんなものさえなければ<line-break>哀しい結末にはならなかったかもな…。<end>あーっ！　あったよぉ！<line-break>闇の神器、禁断の聖杯！ <end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>3What is this...? Is this the Forbidden Grail, the Dark Artifact for creating the Ultimate Life-Form?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4The Forbidden Grail... It gives knowledge, but it can never give true wisdom. That's a little sad, maybe. So that's why it's a Dark Artifact. Here, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5So this is the Forbidden Grail... I don't wanna get all clever or anything.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20The Forbidden Grail... I have no need of such a thing.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6So this is the Forbidden Grail... It's been a very long chase, hasn't it?<end><if><value:$01><equal>7So this is the Forbidden Grail. One of the Dark Artifacts...<end><if><value:$01><equal>9So this is the Forbidden Grail. This guy seriously meant to create an Ultimate Life-Form. Someone that capable might have made a good minion back then. Nah, kidding.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11The Forbidden Grail... Antino had it after all.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12So this is the Forbidden Grail... It's been a long road, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13So this is... the Forbidden Grail.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10So this is the Forbidden Grail. Think this could make me smarter? Kidding! It was a joke! Don't hit me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Just as I thought, Antino had it. Here, <value:$28>. The Forbidden Grail.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Hey, here's the Grail. It finally turned up, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Here it is: the Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail. Utterly inferior creatures don't live long, so they want something to prove they were ever here. Honestly, what a nuisance!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>! I found it! I found it! The Forbidden Grail!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21Loneliness... I might have ended up like that man myself. Here, the Forbidden Grail. You've been searching for it all this time, haven't you?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23The Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail... Take it, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27So this is... the Forbidden Grail.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Here, <value:$28>. The Forbidden Grail.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24This is the Dark Artifact known as the Forbidden Grail, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25Here's the Forbidden Grail at last.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192So this is the Forbidden Grail. Please, take it. I have no need of it.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193S-so this is the Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail... Gulp. If I used this, even a bad cook like me could make delicious food for <value:$28>... It was originally in Rostorl, and I was born and raised in Rostorl, so if I used it j-just a little... O-oh, come on. I'm joking.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194The Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail. It gives knowledge, but it doesn't teach you how to keep from drowning in it. Here, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191The Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail... Dark Artifacts really are terrifying.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Found it―the Forbidden Grail. Here, <value:$28>. You've been searching for it all this time, haven't you? Maybe that man wouldn't have met such a sad end if it weren't for this thing...<end>Ah! I found it! The Dark Artifact, the Forbidden Grail!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>3이건…? 이게 궁극생물을 만들기 위한 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배인가?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4금단의 성배…. 지식은 주지만 진정한 지혜는 주지 않아. 조금 슬프네. 그래서 어둠의 신기인가 봐. 자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>5이게 금단의 성배야…? 난 똑똑한 애 따윈 되고 싶지 않아.<end><if><value:$01><equal>20…금단의 성배인가. 내게는 필요 없다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>6이게 금단의 성배…. 정말 오래 쫓아다녔지.<end><if><value:$01><equal>7이게 금단의 성배네. 어둠의 신기 중 하나….<end><if><value:$01><equal>9이게 금단의 성배구나. 진짜 궁극생물을 만들 생각이었네. 이 정도 능력이면 그때 부하로 삼아도 됐을까. …농담이야.<end><if><value:$01><equal>11금단의 성배…. 역시 안티노가 가지고 있었네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>12이게 금단의 성배인가…. 길었군, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>13이게… 금단의 성배군.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10이게 금단의 성배인가. …이게 있으면 나도 머리가 좋아질까? 농담이야! 때리지 마!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14역시 녀석이 갖고 있었군. 자, <value:$28>, 금단의 성배다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>15있잖아, 성배가 있었어. 드디어 찾았네, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>17봐, 있었어. 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배. 최하등생물은 수명이 짧으니 살아 있었다는 증거를 원하는 거지. 정말 민폐야!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19<value:$28>! 찾았다 찾았어! 금단의 성배!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21고독…인가…. 나도 저 남자처럼 될 뻔했을지 모른다. 자, 금단의 성배다. 계속 찾고 있었지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>23어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배인가…. <value:$28>, 받아라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>27이게… 금단의 성배.<end><if><value:$01><equal>26자, <value:$28>, 금단의 성배다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>24이게 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배군요. <value:$28> 님.<end><if><value:$01><equal>25이게 찾던 금단의 성배네.<end><if><value:$01><equal>192이게 금단의 성배입니까. 받으십시오. 제게는 필요 없습니다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>193…이, 이게 어둠의 신기·금단의 성배군요…. 꿀꺽. …이걸 쓰면 요리 못하는 저도 <value:$28> 씨에게 맛있는 요리를…. 원래 로스톨에 있던 거니 저도 조금쯤…. …노, 농담이에요.<end><if><value:$01><equal>194어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배다. 지식은 주지만 지식에 빠지지 않는 방법은 알려 주지 않아. 자, <value:$28>.<end><if><value:$01><equal>191어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배야. …역시 어둠의 신기는 무서워.<end><if><value:$01><equal>190찾았다, 금단의 성배다. 자, <value:$28>. 계속 찾고 있었지? 저 남자도 이런 게 없었다면 슬픈 결말은 아니었을지도….<end>아! 찾았어어! 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배!<end>"

## 1450037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec145-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器、<line-break>禁断の聖杯を手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "Obtained the Forbidden Grail, a sacred artifact of darkness.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배를 손에 넣었다.<end>"
