## 1460001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！ <line-break>こんな所で会うなんて、<line-break>すごい偶然だね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! What a coincidence, running into you here.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 이런 곳에서 만나다니, 정말 우연이네.<end>"

## 1460002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この場所には前から興味があってね。<line-break>調査してるんだ。<end>"
EN: "I've been interested in this place for some time. I'm investigating it.<end>"
KO: "이곳엔 전부터 관심이 있어서 조사하고 있어.<end>"

## 1460003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回は、闇の神器の調査なんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>古代の謎を解き明かすには、<line-break>古代の遺跡を調べるのが、<line-break>一番の近道だからね。<end>"
EN: "This time, I'm investigating the Dark Artifacts. If you want to unravel the mysteries of antiquity, examining ancient ruins is the best place to start.<end>"
KO: "이번에는 어둠의 신기 조사야. 고대의 수수께끼를 풀려면 고대 유적을 조사하는 게 가장 빠른 길이니까.<end>"

## 1460004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例えば、魔道の塔に残された<line-break>宝とか…<line-break>　<line-break>古代文明を受け継いで栄えた<line-break>魔道王国ラドラスとかね。<end>"
EN: "The treasures left in the Arcane Tower, for example... or the Magic Kingdom Ladras, which flourished as heir to an ancient civilization.<end>"
KO: "예를 들면 마도의 탑에 남겨진 보물이나… 고대 문명을 이어받아 번영한 마도왕국 라드라스 같은 거지.<end>"

## 1460005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、魔道の塔の宝は　　　　<line-break>ほとんど残ってないし、<line-break>　<line-break>ラドラスは、８００年前に<line-break>滅亡して、謎に包まれてる…。<end>"
EN: "But hardly any of the Arcane Tower's treasures remain, and Ladras fell eight hundred years ago. It's still shrouded in mystery.<end>"
KO: "하지만 마도의 탑 보물은 거의 남아 있지 않고, 라드라스는 800년 전에 멸망해서 수수께끼에 싸여 있어….<end>"

## 1460006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、そこから先人類の謎に<line-break>たどり着くのは、難しいと思う。<end>"
EN: "So I doubt those avenues will lead to the mysteries of the people who came before humanity.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 거기서 선인류의 수수께끼까지 도달하기는 어려울 것 같아.<end>"

## 1460007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、調べる価値があるものは<line-break>なんでも調べてみるつもりさ。<line-break>僕の興味は尽きないからね。<line-break>そして、近いうちに<line-break>古代の魔法文明の秘密を<line-break>解き明かしてみせる。<end>"
EN: "Still, I intend to investigate anything worth investigating. My curiosity is endless. Before long, I'll uncover the secrets of ancient magical civilization.<end>"
KO: "하지만 조사할 가치가 있는 건 뭐든 조사해 볼 생각이야. 내 호기심은 끝이 없거든. 그리고 머지않아 고대 마법 문명의 비밀을 밝혀내 보이겠어.<end>"

## 1460008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人類の未来は明るいよ。<line-break>想像もできないような、<line-break>豊かな未来が待っているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Humanity has a bright future ahead. A prosperous future beyond anything we can imagine.<end>"
KO: "인류의 미래는 밝아. 상상도 못 할 만큼 풍요로운 미래가 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 1460009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現実を見ろよ<end>"
EN: "Face reality.<end>"
KO: "현실을 봐<end>"

## 1460010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなるといいね<end>"
EN: "I hope you're right.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 되면 좋겠네<end>"

## 1460011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいさ。<line-break>僕の研究がうまくいけば、<line-break>君にもわかる日がくるよ。<end>"
EN: "That's all right. If my research succeeds, the day will come when you understand too.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아. 내 연구가 잘되면 너도 알게 되는 날이 올 거야.<end>"

## 1460012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その日まで、<line-break>僕はあきらめないから。<end>"
EN: "And until that day, I won't give up.<end>"
KO: "그날까지 난 포기하지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 1460013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>僕の話を聞くと、みんな笑うんだ。<line-break>でも、君は違うね。<line-break>君はみんなと違うよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. Everyone laughs when I tell them about my work. But not you. You're different from the rest.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 내 이야기를 들으면 다들 웃어. 하지만 넌 다르네. 넌 다른 사람들과 달라.<end>"

## 1460014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄さんのおかげで、<line-break>僕はこうして考古学に熱中できる。<line-break>感謝してるよ。<line-break>でもね、兄さんが僕を好きなように<line-break>させておくのは…<end>"
EN: "It's thanks to my brother that I can devote myself to archaeology like this, and I'm grateful. But the reason he lets me do as I please is...<end>"
KO: "형 덕분에 나는 이렇게 고고학에 몰두할 수 있어. 감사하고 있어. 하지만 형이 날 마음대로 하게 두는 건….<end>"

## 1460015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕に仕事をじゃまされたくない　　<line-break>からなんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>そりゃ、兄さんは<line-break>王宮の貴族たちみたいに、<line-break>僕の研究をバカにしたりはしない。<end>"
EN: "because he doesn't want me interfering with his work. Of course, he doesn't ridicule my research like the nobles at court do.<end>"
KO: "내가 일을 방해하지 못하게 하려는 거야. 물론 형은 왕궁 귀족들처럼 내 연구를 비웃지는 않아.<end>"

## 1460016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも僕の研究を認めてるわけでもない。<line-break>そういう意味じゃ、<line-break>兄さんも他のみんなと同じさ。<end>"
EN: "But that doesn't mean he believes in it. In that respect, he's no different from anyone else.<end>"
KO: "하지만 형이 내 연구를 인정하는 것도 아니야. 그런 의미에선 형도 다른 사람들과 똑같아.<end>"

## 1460017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってないんだ。<line-break>魔道文明を人類が手にすれば…<line-break>　<line-break>兄さんを悩ます<line-break>王宮のゴタゴタなんて<line-break>いっぺんに解決するのに。<end>"
EN: "He doesn't understand. If humanity could reclaim magical civilization, all the squabbling at court that troubles him could be settled at once.<end>"
KO: "모르는구나. 마도 문명을 인류가 손에 넣으면… 형을 괴롭히는 왕궁의 난리 따윈 단숨에 해결될 텐데.<end>"

## 1460018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族だろうと、平民だろうと<line-break>楽して豊かな生活ができるのに。<line-break>　<line-break>この研究はね、<line-break>兄さんの…<line-break>いやロストールのためでもある。<end>"
EN: "Nobles and commoners alike could live prosperous lives without hardship. This research isn't just for my brother... No, it's for Rostorl too.<end>"
KO: "귀족이든 평민이든 편하게 풍요롭게 살 수 있는데. 이 연구는 형을… 아니, 로스톨을 위해서이기도 해.<end>"

## 1460020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それは勝手な言い分だ<end>"
EN: "That's a self-serving excuse.<end>"
KO: "그건 제멋대로인 말이야.<end>"

## 1460021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かにそうだ<end>"
EN: "That's true.<end>"
KO: "확실히 그래<end>"

## 1460022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…確かに勝手だよね。<line-break>なんの成果もあげてないんだから。<end>"
EN: "You're right. It is selfish of me to say that when I haven't achieved anything yet.<end>"
KO: "…확실히 제멋대로긴 하지. 아무 성과도 내지 못했으니까.<end>"

## 1460023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、見ててよ。<line-break>きっと役に立ってみせる。<line-break>きっと…。<end>"
EN: "But just watch. I'll prove I can be of use. I will...<end>"
KO: "그래도 지켜봐 줘. 꼭 도움이 되어 보일게. 반드시…<end>"

## 1460024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがと。<line-break>君ならわかってくれると思ってた。<line-break>　<line-break>きっと、古代の謎を解き明かして<line-break>人類の生活を変えてみせるからね。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. I knew you'd understand. I'll unravel the mysteries of antiquity and transform the way humanity lives. Just wait and see.<end>"
KO: "고마워. 너라면 이해해 줄 줄 알았어. 반드시 고대의 수수께끼를 풀어 인간의 생활을 바꿔 보일게.<end>"

## 1460025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険は順調？<end>"
EN: "How are your adventures going?<end>"
KO: "모험은 순조로워?<end>"

## 1460026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、僕の発掘の方も順調だよ。<line-break>でも、今はちょっと横道に<line-break>それてるかな。<line-break>古代文明を知る手掛かりは、<line-break>あちこちに散らばっているんだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, my excavations are going well too. Though I suppose I've taken a bit of a detour. Clues to ancient civilization are scattered everywhere.<end>"
KO: "응, 내 발굴도 순조로워. 다만 지금은 조금 옆길로 샜다고 할까. 고대문명의 단서는 여기저기 흩어져 있거든.<end>"

## 1460027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一見、無関係のようなことでも、<line-break>よく調べると、つながっている。<line-break>古代文明が土台になっているんだよ。<line-break>例えば、竜骨の砂漠。<line-break>あれは、古代の魔法を受け継いだ国<line-break>ラドラス王国が滅亡しかけたとき、<end>"
EN: "Even things that seem unrelated at first are connected when you look closely. Ancient civilization lies at the root of it all. Take the Dragonbone Desert. When the Kingdom of Ladras, heir to ancient magic, stood on the brink of destruction,<end>"
KO: "겉보기엔 무관해 보여도 자세히 조사하면 이어져 있어. 고대 문명이 토대가 된 거지. 예를 들면 용골의 사막. 그건 고대 마법을 계승한 나라 라드라스 왕국이 멸망할 뻔했을 때,<end>"

## 1460028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラドラス王国の巫女が<line-break>造った砂漠なんだ。<line-break>ラドラスの魔法文明を守るためにね。<line-break>砂漠の下には、ラドラスの都市が<line-break>そのままの姿で沈んでいるという。<end>"
EN: "a priestess of Ladras created that desert to protect its magical civilization. They say Ladras itself lies submerged beneath the desert, preserved just as it was.<end>"
KO: "라드라스 왕국의 무녀가 만든 사막이야. 라드라스의 마법 문명을 지키기 위해서지. 사막 아래에는 라드라스 도시가 그대로 가라앉아 있다고 해.<end>"

## 1460029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人が砂漠を造るなんて信じられる？<line-break>人が都市を沈めるなんて信じられる？<line-break>　<line-break>でも、本当なんだ。<line-break>人間には、そんなことだってできる。<line-break>人間の力ってすごいんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Can you believe a person could create a desert? Or sink a city? But it's true. Human beings are capable of even that. The power of humanity is incredible.<end>"
KO: "사람이 사막을 만든다는 걸 믿을 수 있어? 사람이 도시를 가라앉힌다는 걸 믿을 수 있어? 하지만 사실이야. 인간은 그런 일도 할 수 있어. 인간의 힘은 대단해.<end>"

## 1460030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今ある力で十分だ<end>"
EN: "The power we have now is enough.<end>"
KO: "지금 가진 힘이면 충분해<end>"

## 1460031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな力を操りたい<end>"
EN: "I want to wield that kind of power.<end>"
KO: "그런 힘을 다루고 싶다<end>"

## 1460032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、人は進歩しない。<line-break>満足しちゃったら、<line-break>それ以上はないよ。<line-break>僕は、そんなのイヤだ。<line-break>少しでも前へ進みたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Humanity would never progress that way. Once we're satisfied with what we have, we can go no further. I don't want that. I want to keep moving forward, even if only a little at a time.<end>"
KO: "그러면 사람은 발전하지 않아. 만족해 버리면 그 이상은 없잖아. 난 그런 건 싫어. 조금이라도 앞으로 나아가고 싶어.<end>"

## 1460033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見ていて。<line-break>僕がその力を<line-break>人間の手に取り戻してみせる。<end>"
EN: "Just watch. I'll restore that power to human hands.<end>"
KO: "지켜봐. 내가 그 힘을 인간의 손에 되찾아 보이겠어.<end>"

## 1460034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてと。<line-break>僕は調査を続けるよ。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>君も頑張ってね。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'd better get back to my investigation. You keep at it too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자, 난 조사를 계속할게. <value:$28>, 너도 힘내.<end>"

## 1460035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うーん、なるほど…。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... I see...<end>"
KO: "…음, 그렇군….<end>"

## 1460036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また会ったね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We meet again, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "또 만났네, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1460037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだか、君に会えそうな<line-break>気がしてたんだ。<line-break>うれしいよ。<end>"
EN: "Somehow, I had a feeling I'd see you. I'm glad.<end>"
KO: "왠지 너를 만날 수 있을 것 같았어. 기쁘네.<end>"

## 1460038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕ね、君と最初に出会ったとき、<line-break>君がこんな人ならいいな、と思った。<line-break>　<line-break>君は、そのとき僕が思ったとおりの<line-break>人だったよ。<end>"
EN: "When we first met, I remember hoping you'd be this kind of person. And you turned out to be exactly as I'd imagined.<end>"
KO: "처음 널 만났을 때 네가 이런 사람이면 좋겠다고 생각했어. 넌 그때 내가 생각한 그대로의 사람이었어.<end>"

## 1460039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、いつだって僕の話を　　<line-break>真剣に聞いてくれたもの。<line-break>　<line-break>だから君には、僕の研究成果を<line-break>知る権利があるよね。<end>"
EN: "You've always listened to me so earnestly. That means you have a right to know what my research has uncovered.<end>"
KO: "넌 언제나 내 이야기를 진지하게 들어 줬잖아. 그러니 내 연구 성과를 알 권리가 있지.<end>"

## 1460040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はね、僕、ついに突き止めたんだ。<line-break>君に闇の神器を渡したあと、<line-break>いろんな遺跡を研究して、ついにね。<line-break>先人類の秘密が眠る場所…<line-break>先人類の遺跡のありかを。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, I've finally tracked it down. After I gave you the Dark Artifact, I studied all kinds of ruins until at last I found it: the place where the secrets of the Precursor Humans lie sleeping... the site of their ruins.<end>"
KO: "사실 나, 마침내 알아냈어. 네게 어둠의 신기를 넘긴 뒤, 여러 유적을 연구하다가 드디어 말이야. 선인류의 비밀이 잠든 곳…선인류 유적의 위치를.<end>"

## 1460041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…夢幻の湖。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そこに、僕の仮説が、<line-break>夢なんかじゃないってことを<line-break>証明するものが眠っている。<end>"
EN: "...The Lake of Illusions. Something lies sleeping there that will prove my hypothesis was more than just a dream.<end>"
KO: "…몽환의 호수. 거기에 내 가설이 꿈이 아니라는 걸 증명할 무언가가 잠들어 있어.<end>"

## 1460042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これから忙しくなるよ。<line-break>調査も大詰めだからね。<end>"
EN: "I'm going to be busy from now on. The investigation is entering its final stage.<end>"
KO: "이제부터 바빠질 거야. 조사도 막바지니까.<end>"

## 1460043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アドヴェントを習得した。<end>"
EN: "Learned Advent.<end>"
KO: "어드벤트를 습득했다.<end>"

## 1460044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、君か。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>この私に何か用か？<line-break>　<line-break>たまには君から話すがいい。<line-break>今日は、君の話を聞こう。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者の身の上を。<end>"
EN: "Ah, it's you. Do you have some business with me? For once, you may do the talking. Today, I shall hear your story―the story of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "아, 자네인가. 이 나에게 무슨 용무지? 가끔은 자네가 이야기해 보게. 오늘은 자네 이야기를 듣지. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 삶을.<end>"

## 1460045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の故郷は？　ご両親は健在か？<line-break>君の活躍ぶりを、<line-break>さぞや鼻にかけていることだろう。<end>"
EN: "Where are you from? Are your parents still well? They must take great pride in all you've accomplished.<end>"
KO: "고향은 어디지? 부모님은 건강하신가? 네 활약을 꽤 자랑스러워하고 계시겠군.<end>"

## 1460046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…余計なことを言ったようだ。<end>"
EN: "...It seems I spoke out of turn.<end>"
KO: "…쓸데없는 말을 한 것 같군.<end>"

## 1460047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…この私にも両親はいない。<line-break>私を息子と呼ぶ者はいるが、<line-break>奴など親とは思いたくもない。<end>"
EN: "...I have no parents either. Someone calls me a son, but I have no wish to think of that wretch as a parent.<end>"
KO: "…나 역시 부모가 없다. 나를 아들이라 부르는 자는 있지만 녀석 따위를 부모로 생각하고 싶지도 않아.<end>"

## 1460048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フ…私が先に心ない質問をしたのだ。<line-break>愚かな生みの親について<line-break>話さぬわけにもいくまい。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... I was the one who asked such a thoughtless question. I can hardly refuse to speak of my foolish birth parents.<end>"
KO: "후… 내가 먼저 무례한 질문을 했으니 어리석은 친부모에 대해 말하지 않을 수도 없겠군.<end>"

## 1460049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私を生んだ両親は、　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ディンガル家の一族。しかし、<line-break>その地位は低く、生活は貧しかった。<line-break>そこにゾフォル…あの予言者が現れた。<line-break>当時、宰相であったゾフォルは…<end>"
EN: "My birth parents belonged to the Dyneskal family. But their standing was low, and they lived in poverty. Then Zofor appeared―that prophet. At the time, Zofor was Chancellor...<end>"
KO: "나를 낳은 부모는 딩갈 가문의 일족이었다. 하지만 지위는 낮았고 생활은 가난했지. 그곳에 조포르… 그 예언자가 나타났다. 당시 재상이었던 조포르는….<end>"

## 1460050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一夜、肉体を貸せば、<line-break>その後の地位と財産を保証する。<line-break>…そう言ったという。<line-break>父と母は、ゾフォルに連れられて<line-break>魔道の塔へとおもむき、<line-break>シャロームに肉体を貸した。<end>"
EN: "“Lend your bodies for a single night, and your position and wealth afterward will be guaranteed.” That, it is said, was the offer. My father and mother went with Zofor to the Arcane Tower, where they lent their bodies to Shalom.<end>"
KO: "하룻밤 육체를 빌려주면 이후의 지위와 재산을 보장하겠다. …그렇게 말했다고 한다. 아버지와 어머니는 조포르에게 이끌려 마도의 탑으로 가 샬롬에게 육체를 빌려주었다.<end>"

## 1460051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、奴の子を身ごもったのだ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そのとき受けた強力な魔法がたたって、<line-break>間もなくふたりは世を去った。<line-break>シャロームの子ベルゼーヴァを残して。<end>"
EN: "And my mother conceived his child. The powerful magic they were subjected to took its toll, and both died soon afterward, leaving behind Beelzeva, the son of Shalom.<end>"
KO: "그리고 놈의 아이를 임신했다. 그때 받은 강력한 마법의 여파로 얼마 지나지 않아 둘은 세상을 떠났다. 샤롬의 아이 벨제바를 남기고.<end>"

## 1460052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼らは愚かだろうか？<line-break>…彼らは弱かっただけだ。<line-break>あるいはその両方かもしれない。<line-break>なんにせよ、弱さが、<line-break>この世の悲しみを生むのだ。<end>"
EN: "Were they fools? ...They were merely weak. Perhaps they were foolish as well. In any case, it is weakness that gives rise to sorrow in this world.<end>"
KO: "그들은 어리석었을까? …그저 약했을 뿐이다. 아니면 둘 다였을지도 모르지. 어쨌든 약함이 이 세상의 슬픔을 낳는다.<end>"

## 1460053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが、はずしてくれ。<line-break>片付けてしまいたい仕事がある。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but leave me alone. I have some work I want to finish.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 자리를 비켜 줘. 끝내고 싶은 일이 있어.<end>"

## 1460054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ついに発見したよ。<line-break>この先に、古代遺跡があるんだ。　　<line-break>おそらく、魔法が封印されているよ。<line-break>でも、強力な守護者がいてね。<line-break>僕は、近づけそうにない。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. I've finally found it. There are ancient ruins up ahead, and I suspect some magic has been sealed inside. But a powerful Guardian stands watch, and I don't think I can get close.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 드디어 발견했어. 이 앞에 고대 유적이 있어. 아마 마법이 봉인돼 있을 거야. 하지만 강력한 수호자가 있어서 나는 가까이 가기 힘들 것 같아.<end>"

## 1460055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、君なら近づけるかもしれないね。<line-break>僕は、他の場所も<line-break>調べてみたいからここを離れるけど、<line-break><value:$28>は、<line-break>行ってみたらどうだい？<line-break>見るだけでも価値があるよ。<end>"
EN: "You might be able to get close, though. I'm leaving to investigate some other places, but why don't you go and see, <value:$28>? It would be worth it just to have a look.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너라면 가까이 갈 수 있을지도 모르겠네. 난 다른 곳도 조사하고 싶어서 여길 떠나지만, <value:$28>은 가 보는 게 어때? 보는 것만으로도 가치가 있어.<end>"

## 1460056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、またね。<end>"
EN: "Well, see you later.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 또 봐.<end>"

## 1460057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごい。あの守護者を倒したんだね。<line-break>これで古代の偉大な力のひとつが<line-break>復活したんだ。<line-break>ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>これで研究が進められるよ。<end>"
EN: "Amazing. You defeated that Guardian. One of the great powers of antiquity has been restored. Thank you, <value:$28>. Now I can move forward with my research.<end>"
KO: "대단해. 그 수호자를 쓰러뜨렸구나. 이걸로 고대의 위대한 힘 하나가 부활했어. 고마워, <value:$28>. 이제 연구를 진행할 수 있겠어.<end>"

## 1460059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね。<line-break>兄さんなら、大丈夫だよね。<end>"
EN: "You're right. My brother will be all right, won't he?<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 오빠라면 괜찮을 거야.<end>"

## 1460060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec146-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、僕は、一度戻って、<line-break>中を調べる準備をしてくるよ。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll head back and prepare to investigate the interior.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 한번 돌아가서 안을 조사할 준비를 해 올게.<end>"

## 1470001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、来たな、　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>そんじゃ、ギルドに行くとしようか。<line-break>ついてきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "There you are, <value:$28>. All right, let's head to the Guild. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "오, 왔군, <value:$28>. 그럼 길드로 가자. 따라와.<end>"

## 1470002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさんじゃないか。<line-break>最近、顔を見せなかったが、<line-break>どこをほっつき歩いていたんだい？<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't Xenetes. Haven't seen you around lately. Where've you been wandering off to?<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨잖아. 요즘 얼굴을 안 보이더니 어디를 돌아다니고 있었어?<end>"

## 1470003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いろんなとこだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>で、さっそくだが、こいつのギルド<line-break>登録をしてやってくれないか？<line-break>俺の新しい相棒なんでな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, here and there. Anyway, could you register this one with the Guild? My new partner.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 여러 곳이지. 그보다 바로 본론인데, 이 녀석을 길드에 등록해 주지 않겠어? 내 새 파트너거든.<end>"

## 1470004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へえ。あんたかい。<line-break>ゼネテスさんが目を付けたんだ。<line-break>将来有望なんだろうね。<end>"
EN: "Oh? So you're the one. If Xenetes has his eye on you, you must have real potential.<end>"
KO: "오. 네가 그 사람이야? 제네테스 씨가 눈여겨봤다니 장래가 유망한가 보네.<end>"

## 1470005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "犯罪登録なし。幻術計振れておらず。　　　<line-break>…はい、あなたは今日から<line-break>冒険者として登録されました。<line-break>頑張って仕事してくれよ。<line-break>あんたが有名になったら、そのギルド登録を<line-break>したのは俺だって鼻が高いからね。<end>"
EN: "No criminal record. No reading on the illusion gauge... All right. As of today, you're officially registered as an adventurer. Work hard out there. If you become famous, I'll be proud to say I was the one who registered you with the Guild.<end>"
KO: "범죄 등록 없음. 환술계도 이상 없음. …좋습니다, 오늘부터 모험자로 등록됐습니다. 열심히 일해 주세요. 당신이 유명해지면 그 길드 등록을 내가 했다고 자랑할 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 1470006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、ゼネテスさん。<line-break>せっかく来たんだから、<line-break>ひとつ仕事を頼まれてみないかい？<end>"
EN: "Now then, Xenetes. Since you're here, how about taking on a job?<end>"
KO: "그래서 제네테스 씨. 모처럼 왔으니 일 하나 맡아 보지 않을래?<end>"

## 1470007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　そうだな、<line-break>初心者向けのだったら、<line-break>聞いてみてもいいぜ？<end>"
EN: "Hm? All right. If it's fit for a beginner, I'll hear you out.<end>"
KO: "응? 그래, 초보자용이라면 물어봐도 돼.<end>"

## 1470008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "初心者向けとは言えないだろうが、<line-break>あんたが一緒だったら<line-break>大丈夫なんじゃないかな。<end>"
EN: "I wouldn't call it a beginner's job, but with you along, it should be all right.<end>"
KO: "초보자용이라고는 못 하겠지만 네가 함께라면 괜찮지 않을까.<end>"

## 1470010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、子供の誘拐事件が<line-break>続発しているのは知っているだろう？<line-break>　<line-break>その強盗団のアジトから子供たちを<line-break>助け出してほしいって依頼だ。<end>"
EN: "You know about the recent wave of child abductions, don't you? The request is to rescue the children from the bandits' hideout.<end>"
KO: "최근 아이 납치 사건이 잇따르는 건 알고 있지? 그 강도단의 아지트에서 아이들을 구해 달라는 의뢰야.<end>"

## 1470011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ。てことは、アジトの場所は<line-break>つきとめてあるのかい？<end>"
EN: "Oh? So you've tracked down their hideout?<end>"
KO: "헤에. 그럼 아지트 위치는 알아낸 거야?<end>"

## 1470012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。このロストールから<line-break>２日ほど南西に行ったところにある<line-break>洞窟だってわかったんだ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. We found out it's a cave about two days' journey southwest of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "그래. 이 로스톨에서 이틀 정도 남서쪽으로 간 곳에 있는 동굴이라는 걸 알아냈어.<end>"

## 1470013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅん。<line-break>あの飢えた者の迷宮って<line-break>呼ばれてる洞窟だな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. That cave they call the Labyrinth of the Hungry.<end>"
KO: "흐음. 그 굶주린 자의 미궁이라고 불리는 동굴이군.<end>"

## 1470014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールでも被害が増えてきてるんだ。<line-break>ゼネテスさん、頼むよ。<line-break>報酬は５００ギアだ。<end>"
EN: "The number of victims here in Rostorl is rising too. Please, Xenetes. The reward is 500 Gea.<end>"
KO: "로스톨에서도 피해가 늘고 있어. 제네테스 씨, 부탁해. 보수는 500기어야.<end>"

## 1470015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だそうだ。<line-break>どうする、<value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "There you have it. What do you say, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "…그렇다는데. 어떻게 할래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 1470016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何？　フリントを襲った連中が　　　　<line-break>そんなことを言っていたのか？<line-break>　<line-break>ま、かわいい女の子を捕まえて<line-break>お頭へ献上ってのは、<line-break>悪党なら誰でも言いそうなことだが…。<end>"
EN: "What? The men who attacked Flint said that? Well, “capture a pretty girl and present her to the boss” is the sort of thing any villain might say...<end>"
KO: "뭐? 플린트를 습격한 놈들이 그런 말을 했다고? 뭐, 귀여운 여자애를 잡아서 두목에게 바친다는 건 악당이라면 누구나 할 법한 말이지만….<end>"

## 1470017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは、ひょっとすると<line-break>ひょっとするな。そんじゃ、ひとつ<line-break>依頼を受けてみるとするか？<end>"
EN: "There may be something to this after all. So, shall we take the job?<end>"
KO: "이거 어쩌면 정말 뭔가 있겠는데. 그럼 의뢰 하나 받아 볼까?<end>"

## 1470018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやじ、聞いての通りだ。<line-break>その依頼、俺たちが受けるぜ。<end>"
EN: "You heard, old man. We'll take the job.<end>"
KO: "아저씨, 들은 대로야. 그 의뢰, 우리가 맡을게.<end>"

## 1470019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。そうこなくっちゃな。<line-break>それじゃ、頼んだぜ、ゼネテスさん。<line-break>それに、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Good. That's what I wanted to hear. I'm counting on you, Xenetes―and you too, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그래야지. 그럼 부탁한다, 제네테스 씨. 그리고 <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1470020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、依頼も受けたことだし、<line-break>そろそろ宿屋に戻った方がいいな。<line-break>出発は明日だ。<line-break>ルルアンタが作るっていう夕飯、<line-break>俺も同席していいだろう？<end>"
EN: "Now that we've taken the job, we'd better head back to the inn. We leave tomorrow. You won't mind if I join you for the dinner Lulluantha's making, will you?<end>"
KO: "그럼 의뢰도 받았으니 슬슬 여관으로 돌아가는 게 좋겠군. 출발은 내일이야. 루루안타가 만든다는 저녁, 나도 같이 먹어도 되겠지?<end>"

## 1470021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は先に宿屋に戻ってる。<line-break><value:$28>も<line-break>寄り道してないでさっさと戻れよ。<end>"
EN: "I'll go on ahead to the inn. Don't dawdle, <value:$28>. Get back there soon.<end>"
KO: "난 먼저 여관으로 돌아갈게. <value:$28>도 딴 데 들르지 말고 얼른 돌아와.<end>"

## 1470022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、<value:$28>。<line-break>どこに行くんだ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Where are you going?<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>. 어디 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1470023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタが心配するだろう。<line-break>宿屋で待っていてやろうぜ？<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha will be worried. Why don't we wait for her at the inn?<end>"
KO: "루루안타가 걱정할 거야. 여관에서 기다려 주자고?<end>"

## 1470024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "二階も見てきたが、ルルアンタは<line-break>まだ帰ってきてないぜ。<line-break>俺はここで待たせてもらうとするよ。<end>"
EN: "I checked upstairs too, but Lulluantha still isn't back. Think I'll wait here.<end>"
KO: "2층도 보고 왔지만 릴비는 아직 안 돌아왔어. 난 여기서 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 1470025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、待ちきれないのか？<line-break>お前さんを喜ばせようと、いろいろ<line-break>買いこんでて遅くなってるんだろ？<end>"
EN: "What, can't wait? All that shopping to please you must be taking a while, eh?<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 못 기다리겠어? 널 기쁘게 해 주려고 이것저것 사느라 늦는 거겠지?<end>"

## 1470026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ひょっとしたら…<line-break>…まさかな。<end>"
EN: "Maybe... No. Couldn't be.<end>"
KO: "…혹시… …설마.<end>"

## 1470027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒にいた女の子ですか？<line-break>まだ、戻ってきてませんよ。<end>"
EN: "You mean the girl who was with you? She hasn't come back yet.<end>"
KO: "함께 있던 여자아이요? 아직 돌아오지 않았어요.<end>"

## 1470028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、<value:$28>。<line-break>どこに行くんだ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Where are you headed?<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>. 어디 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1470029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタを捜しに行くのか？<line-break>…確かに、ルルアンタが出てから<line-break>だいぶ時間がたつが…。<end>"
EN: "Going to look for Lulluantha? She has been gone quite a while...<end>"
KO: "루루안타를 찾으러 가는 거야? …확실히 루루안타가 나간 뒤 꽤 시간이 지났지만….<end>"

## 1470030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…実は、俺も例の子供を狙った<line-break>誘拐事件のことがひっかかっていた。<line-break>もしかしたら、ルルアンタが…。<line-break>町を捜してみた方がよさそうだな。<line-break>手遅れになるとまずい。<end>"
EN: "Actually, I've had that kidnapping case on my mind too―the one targeting children. Maybe Lulluantha... We'd better search the town. We can't afford to be too late.<end>"
KO: "…사실 나도 그 아이를 노린 유괴 사건이 걸렸어. 어쩌면 루루안타가…. 도시를 찾아보는 게 좋겠군. 늦으면 곤란해.<end>"

## 1470031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "좋아, 그럼 나도 짚이는 데를 알아볼게. <value:$28>도 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1470032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃいませ。<line-break>今日は何になさいますか？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>え？　リルビーの女の子ですか？<line-break>カウンターにいるお客さんでは<line-break>ないんですよね？<end>"

## 1470036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お店には、他にリルビーの方は<line-break>来てないと思いますよ。<line-break>ごめんなさい。<end>"

## 1470037

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、誘拐かはともかく<line-break>ルルアンタに何かあったのは<line-break>間違いなさそうだな。<end>"

## 1470042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えー？　ボクに用があるのかーい？<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Oh? Is it me you wanted?<end>"
KO: "어? 나한테 볼일이 있는 거야?<end>"

## 1470044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、あんたは<line-break>この広場でよく見かけるんでね。<line-break>ちょっと、聞きたいことがあるんだ。<line-break>今さっき、この辺りで<line-break>リルビーの女の子と、男が何か<line-break>言い争ってたりはしなかったか？<end>"
EN: "I see you around this square a lot, so I wanted to ask you something. Did you happen to see a Lilubee girl arguing with a man around here just now?<end>"
KO: "아니, 당신은 이 광장에서 자주 보여서 말이야. 좀 묻고 싶은 게 있어. 방금 전 이 근처에서 릴비 여자아이와 남자가 말다툼하고 있진 않았어?<end>"

## 1470045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、そういえばこの辺りで<line-break>リルビーの女の子が、人相の悪ーい<line-break>男たちと、もめてるのを見たねー。<line-break>でも、しばらくしたら<line-break>女の子は男たちと一緒に町の外に<line-break>行ってしまったみたいだけどねー。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Now that you mention it, I did see a Lilubee girl having words with some rather nasty-looking men around here. But after a while, she seemed to leave town with them.<end>"
KO: "음, 그러고 보니 이 근처에서 릴비 여자아이가 인상 나쁜 남자들이랑 실랑이하는 걸 봤어. 그런데 잠시 뒤엔 여자아이가 그 남자들과 함께 마을 밖으로 간 것 같던데.<end>"

## 1470046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間違いなさそうだな。<line-break>ルルアンタはそいつらに<line-break>連れていかれたんだ。<end>"
EN: "No doubt about it. They're the ones who took Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "틀림없어. 루루안타는 그 녀석들에게 끌려간 거야.<end>"

## 1470047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それで、ボクはもー、<line-break>行ってもいいのかーい？　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "So, may I go now?<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 이제 가도 되는 거지~?<end>"

## 1470048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ひきとめちまって<line-break>悪かったな。助かったぜ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, sorry for holding you up. You've been a big help.<end>"
KO: "아, 붙잡아 둬서 미안했다. 덕분에 살았어.<end>"

## 1470049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかーい。それなら、<line-break>ボクは行かせてもらうよー。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Very well. In that case, I'll be on my way.<end>"
KO: "그래애. 그렇다면 난 가 볼게.<end>"

## 1470050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誘拐のプロにしてはお粗末だが、<line-break>奴らはもう、この町からは離れていると<line-break>考えた方がいいだろうな。<line-break>ギルドの親父の話では、<line-break>奴らのアジトは飢えた者の迷宮だ。<line-break>今から追うぞ。<end>"
EN: "Sloppy work for professional kidnappers, but we'd better assume they've already left town. According to the old man at the Guild, their hideout is the Labyrinth of the Hungry. We're going after them now.<end>"
KO: "유괴 전문가치곤 허술하지만 놈들은 이미 이 마을을 떠났다고 보는 게 좋겠군. 길드 주인 말로는 놈들의 아지트가 굶주린 자의 미궁이래. 지금부터 쫓는다.<end>"

## 1470051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐに出発しよう。<line-break>うまくいけば、途中で<line-break>捕まえられるかもしれん。<line-break>俺は門で待っている。<line-break>準備をすませてきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Let's leave right away. With any luck, we might catch them on the way. I'll wait at the gate. Go get ready.<end>"
KO: "바로 출발하자. 잘하면 가는 길에 붙잡을 수 있을지도 몰라. 나는 문에서 기다릴 테니 준비하고 와.<end>"

## 1470052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者ってのは、危険な仕事だからな。<line-break>装備を調え、いざって時のために薬を<line-break>用意しておくのは、基本中の基本だ。<line-break>薬は道具屋、装備は鍛冶屋だ。<line-break>こいつをとっときな。<end>"
EN: "Being an adventurer is dangerous work. Getting your gear in order and keeping medicine on hand for emergencies are the bare essentials. You get medicine at the item shop and equipment at the smithy. Here, take this.<end>"
KO: "모험자는 위험한 일이야. 장비를 갖추고 만일을 위해 약을 준비해 두는 건 기본 중의 기본이지. 약은 도구점, 장비는 대장간이다. 이걸 받아 둬.<end>"

## 1470053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気にすんな。<line-break>返したきゃ、出世払いでいいって。<line-break>じゃ、門で待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Don't worry about it. If you want to pay me back, you can do it once you've made it big. I'll be waiting at the gate.<end>"
KO: "신경 쓰지 마. 갚고 싶으면 출세한 뒤에 갚아도 돼. 그럼 문에서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1470054

JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>もう準備はいいんだな？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you're ready to go, right?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 준비는 다 됐지?<end>"

## 1470057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、出かけるとしよう。<line-break>急ごうぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"

## 1470058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタのことが心配で　　　<line-break>気が急ぐのもわかるが、<line-break>冒険の前の準備は肝心だ。<line-break>待っているから、もう一度<line-break>町へ戻って、準備してきてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I know you're worried about Lulluantha and eager to get moving, but it's vital to prepare before an adventure. I'll wait for you, so go back to town and make sure you're ready.<end>"
KO: "루루안타가 걱정돼 마음이 급한 건 알지만, 모험 전 준비는 중요하다. 기다리고 있을 테니 다시 마을에 돌아가 준비하고 와.<end>"

## 1470059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、見覚えがある。<line-break>ノーブルで襲ってきた連中に<line-break>違いない。<line-break>入口がふたつあるな。<line-break>そんじゃ、効率よく<line-break>二手に分かれるか。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, I recognize them. They're the ones who attacked us in Noble. There are two entrances. Let's split up and save some time.<end>"
KO: "아아, 낯이 익어. 노블에서 습격해온 녀석들이 틀림없어. 입구가 둘이군. 그럼 효율적으로 둘로 나누자.<end>"

## 1470060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先に俺が正面から行って、奴らの<line-break>注意を引きつけるから、お前さんは<line-break>右の入口から侵入してくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'll go in through the front and draw their attention. You slip in through the entrance on the right.<end>"
KO: "먼저 내가 정면으로 가서 녀석들의 주의를 끌 테니 너는 오른쪽 입구로 침입해 줘.<end>"

## 1470061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺のことは心配しなさんな。<line-break>それよりも、お前さんだってひとりだ。<line-break>十分に気をつけて行ってくれ。<line-break>確認しておくが、俺たちの目標は<line-break>ルルアンタの救出。それと、<line-break>捕まっている子供たちの解放だ。<end>"
EN: "Don't you worry about me. You'll be on your own too, so watch yourself. Just to be clear, our objectives are to rescue Lulluantha and free the children they're holding captive.<end>"
KO: "내 걱정은 하지 마. 그보다 너도 혼자니까 충분히 조심해서 가. 확인해 두자. 우리 목표는 루루안타 구출과 붙잡혀 있는 아이들의 해방이야.<end>"

## 1470062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、行くとしますか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's get going.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 가볼까.<end>"

## 1470063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、なぁに。ちょっくら<line-break>ここにやっかいになってる子供たちを<line-break>返してもらおうかと思ってね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, nothing much. I just thought I'd ask you to give back the children who've been enjoying your hospitality.<end>"
KO: "아니, 뭐. 잠깐 여기 신세지고 있는 아이들을 돌려받으러 왔을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1470065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470066

## 1470067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml

## 1470068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml

## 1470071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっへ。やろうってのかぁ、俺と？<line-break>そのうち、俺の子分どもが戻ってくる。<line-break>そうなりゃ万事休すだぜ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You really want to take me on? My boys'll be back before long, and then you're finished.<end>"
KO: "헤헤. 나랑 해 보겠다는 거냐? 조금 있으면 내 부하들이 돌아와. 그러면 끝장이야.<end>"

## 1470072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よく見りゃ、お前もなかなか<line-break>いい感じじゃねぇか。俺様直々に<line-break>かわいがってやってもいいんだぜ？　　<end>"
EN: "Now that I get a good look at you, you're not half bad. Maybe I'll give you some personal attention.<end>"
KO: "자세히 보니 너도 꽤 괜찮은데. 나님이 직접 귀여워해 줘도 된다고?<end>"

## 1470073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世の中、そう都合よくは<line-break>いかないと思うがな。<end>"
EN: "I don't think things are going to work out quite that conveniently for you.<end>"
KO: "세상일이 그렇게 뜻대로 되진 않을 텐데.<end>"

## 1470074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、お前たち以外に<line-break>満足に動ける奴は残ってないぜ。<end>"
EN: "You're the only ones left who can still put up a fight.<end>"
KO: "이제 너희 말고 제대로 움직일 수 있는 사람은 남지 않았어.<end>"

## 1470075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、こいつらを叩けば終わりだ。<line-break>さっさとやっちまおうぜ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right. Once we take these guys down, it's over. Let's get it done, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석들만 쓰러뜨리면 끝이야. 빨리 해치우자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1470077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ち、ちくしょう！<line-break>おい、お前ら、ぬかるんじゃねえぞ！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "D-Damn it! Come on, you lot! Don't screw this up!<end>"
KO: "제, 젠장! 어이, 너희들 실수하지 마라!<end>"

## 1470078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、ちくしょう…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>こんなはずじゃ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Tch, damn it... This wasn't supposed to happen...<end>"
KO: "제, 젠장… 이럴 리가…<end>"

## 1470079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやったな、<value:$28>。<line-break>ひとりで行かせて大丈夫かとも<line-break>思っていたが、いらぬ心配だった。<line-break>で、<value:$28>。<line-break>お前さんの方はどうだった？<line-break>ルルアンタは見つかったか？<end>"
EN: "Well done, <value:$28>. I wasn't sure sending you alone was a good idea, but it seems I had nothing to worry about. What about you, <value:$28>? Did you find Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "잘했어, <value:$28>. 혼자 보내도 괜찮을까 생각했지만 쓸데없는 걱정이었군. 그래서 <value:$28>, 네 쪽은 어땠어? 릴비는 찾았나?<end>"

## 1470080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど、エンシャントへね。　　　<line-break>これは厄介なことになってきたな。<line-break>　<line-break>ここの子供たちは俺が救出した<line-break>全員無事だ。…と言っても、<line-break>まだエンシャントのほうが残ってるが。<end>"
EN: "Ancient, then. This is getting complicated. I rescued the children here, and they're all safe. But there are still the ones in Ancient.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군, 엔샨트로 갔단 말이지. 귀찮은 일이 됐군. 여기 아이들은 내가 구출했고 전부 무사해. …그래도 아직 엔샨트 쪽이 남아 있지만.<end>"

## 1470081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、そうだな。とりあえず　　　　<line-break>今回助け出した子供たちを<line-break>送り届けなきゃならんからな。<line-break>子供は俺がロストールまで連れていく。<line-break>お前さんは先にエンシャントへ行って<line-break>さぐりを入れておいてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. First, I need to see the children I rescued safely back. I'll take them to Rostorl. You go on ahead to Ancient and see what you can find out.<end>"
KO: "음, 그렇군. 일단 이번에 구해낸 아이들을 데려다줘야 하니까. 아이들은 내가 로스톨까지 데려갈게. 자네는 먼저 엔샨트로 가서 탐문해 둬.<end>"

## 1470082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントに行ってる奴らは　　<line-break>そんなに多くはないだろうが、<line-break>油断はするなよ。<line-break>俺も急いでエンシャントへ向かう。<line-break>行動を起こすのは、なるべく<line-break>俺が行ってからにしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "There probably aren't many of them in Ancient, but don't let your guard down. I'll make for Ancient as quickly as I can. If possible, wait until I get there before you make a move.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트에 간 놈들은 그렇게 많진 않겠지만 방심하지 마. 나도 서둘러 엔샨트로 갈 테니 행동을 시작하는 건 가능하면 내가 도착한 뒤로 해 줘.<end>"

## 1470083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、俺は子供たちを連れて<line-break>ロストールに向かう。<line-break>エンシャントで会おう。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'll take the children to Rostorl. See you in Ancient.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 아이들을 데리고 로스톨로 갈게. 엔샨트에서 만나자.<end>"

## 1470084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0（はやくルルアンタを捜さないと！<line-break>　ルルアンタはエンシャントにいる。）<end>（はやくルルアンタを捜さないと！<line-break>　ルルアンタは<line-break>　エンシャントにいるわ。）<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0(I have to find Lulluantha, fast! She's in Ancient.)<end>(I have to find Lulluantha, fast! She's in Ancient.)<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0(빨리 루루안타를 찾아야 해! 루루안타는 엔샨트에 있어.)<end>(빨리 루루안타를 찾아야 해! 루루안타는 엔샨트에 있어.)<end>"

## 1470085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、まずったぜ。<line-break>よりによって、あのアンギルダンに<line-break>見つかっちまうなんてよ。<end>"
EN: "Damn, I screwed up. Of all people, Angeerdan had to spot me.<end>"
KO: "쳇, 실수했군. 하필 그 앙길단에게 들키다니.<end>"

## 1470086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこのお主、冒険者だな！<line-break>その男を捕まえてくれ！<line-break>人さらいじゃ！<end>"
EN: "You there―you're an adventurer, aren't you? Catch that man! He's a kidnapper!<end>"
KO: "거기 자네, 모험자군! 그 남자를 잡아 줘! 유괴범이다!<end>"

## 1470087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだ貴様！？　　　　　　　　　<line-break>邪魔すんじゃねぇ！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "W-who the hell are you?! Stay out of this!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐냐 네놈!? 방해하지 마!<end>"

## 1470088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、捕まえてくれたか。<line-break>助かったわ。<line-break>わしはアンギルダン、冒険者じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you caught him. Much obliged. I'm Angeerdan, an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "오, 잡아 줬나. 살았군. 나는 앙길단, 모험자다.<end>"

## 1470089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>その名、覚えておこう。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>最近、ロストール付近で子供たちを<line-break>ねらった誘拐事件が増えていてな。<line-break>こやつは、その一味なんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it? I'll remember that name. There's been a rash of children being abducted around Rostorl lately. This scoundrel is one of the kidnappers.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 그 이름, 기억해 두지. 최근 로스톨 부근에서 아이들을 노린 유괴 사건이 늘고 있다. 이 녀석은 그 일당이야.<end>"

## 1470090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんと、お主もその事件を<line-break>追っておったのか。すでにアジトも<line-break>叩いているとは、大したものじゃ。<line-break>わしの方でも、エンシャントへ<line-break>連れてこられた子供たちは<line-break>助け出しておいた。<end>"
EN: "What, you've been investigating the kidnappings too? And you've already raided their hideout? Impressive. For my part, I rescued the children they'd brought to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "아니, 자네도 그 사건을 쫓고 있었나. 이미 아지트까지 친 걸 보니 대단하군. 내 쪽에서도 엔샨트로 끌려온 아이들은 구해 냈다네.<end>"

## 1470091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃが、お主の言うルルアンタらしい<line-break>リルビーの娘は見あたらなかったぞ。<end>"
EN: "But I saw no sign of the Lilubee girl you say is called Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "하지만 자네가 말한 루루안타라는 릴비 여자아이는 보이지 않았어.<end>"

## 1470092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、お主。話は聞いていたな？<line-break>リルビーの娘はどうしたのじゃ？<line-break>知らぬとは言わさんぞ。<end>"
EN: "You there. You heard all that, didn't you? What happened to the Lilubee girl? Don't tell me you don't know.<end>"
KO: "이봐, 자네. 이야기는 들었겠지? 릴비 소녀는 어떻게 됐나? 모른다고 하진 못할 게야.<end>"

## 1470093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ。今度は賢者の森かい？<end>"

## 1470095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1470096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ワハハ、死んでたまるか。<end>"

## 1470097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の相棒の<value:$28>が<line-break>世話になったようだな。礼を言う。<end>"

KO: "내 동료 <value:$28>이 신세를 진 모양이군. 고맙다.<end>"

## 1470098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "No, <value:$28> lent me a hand. I'm the one who should be giving thanks.<end>"

## 1470099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇ、赤い巨星アンギルダンに<line-break>恩を売るとは、<value:$28>も<line-break>なかなかやるじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Getting the Red Giant Angeerdan in your debt? Not bad, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "헤에, 붉은 거성 앙길단에게 은혜를 베풀다니, <value:$28>도 제법이잖아.<end>"

## 1470100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、アンギルダン。あんた、<line-break>帝国から追われている身だろ？<line-break>こんな所にいていいのかい？<end>"
EN: "So, Angeerdan―you're wanted by the Empire, aren't you? Is it wise to be standing around here?<end>"
KO: "그런데 앙길단. 당신 제국에 쫓기는 몸이잖아? 이런 데 있어도 되는 거야?<end>"

## 1470101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。その通りじゃ。　　　　<line-break>道中、こ奴らを追ってきたら<line-break>帝都まで来てしまってな。<line-break>じゃが、お主の言うとおり<line-break>ここでのんびりとしていられる<line-break>身分ではない。<end>"
EN: "Aye, that I am. I followed these rogues along the road and found myself all the way in the imperial capital. But you're right. I'm in no position to linger here.<end>"
KO: "음, 그렇다. 길에서 이 녀석들을 쫓아오다 보니 제도까지 와 버렸어. 하지만 네 말대로 여기서 느긋하게 있을 처지는 아니지.<end>"

## 1470102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスよ、わしが助け出しておいた<line-break>子供たちをお主に預けたい。<line-break>一緒に来てくれぬか？<end>"
EN: "Xenetes, I'd like to entrust the children I rescued to you. Will you come with me?<end>"
KO: "제네테스여, 내가 구해 둔 아이들을 자네에게 맡기고 싶네. 함께 가 주겠나?<end>"

## 1470103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつは構わないが…。<end>"
EN: "I don't mind, but...<end>"
KO: "그건 상관없지만….<end>"

## 1470104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主らの手柄にしておいてくれ。<line-break>わしは道すがら、泣き叫んでいた<line-break>子供たちを助けたにすぎん。<end>"
EN: "Let the credit go to you two. I merely rescued some wailing children I came upon along the road.<end>"
KO: "너희 공적으로 해 두게. 나는 길에서 울고 있던 아이들을 구했을 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1470105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、<line-break>そういうことにしておくか。<line-break>あとで一杯おごらせてもらう。<end>"
EN: "All right, we'll leave it at that. Let me buy you a drink later.<end>"
KO: "그럼 그렇게 해 두지. 나중에 한잔 살게.<end>"

## 1470106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、そうさせてもらおう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, I'll hold you to that.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그렇게 하지.<end>"

## 1470107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ。必ず、<line-break>ルルアンタを助けてやるのじゃぞ。<line-break>では、さらばじゃ。<end>"

KO: "<value:$28>여. 반드시 루루안타를 구해 주거라. 그럼 잘 가거라.<end>"

## 1470108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>俺はアンギルダンのとっつぁんが<line-break>助けた子供たちを引き取ってくる。<line-break>これで、俺たちが引き受けた<line-break>ギルドの仕事はほとんど終了だ。<line-break>あとは、ルルアンタを追わんとな。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>, I'm going to take charge of the children old Angeerdan rescued. That should just about wrap up the Guild job we took. Now we only need to go after Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "그럼, <value:$28>. 난 앙길단 영감이 구해 낸 아이들을 데려올게. 이걸로 우리가 맡은 길드 일은 거의 끝났어. 이제 루루안타를 쫓아가야지.<end>"

## 1470109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　なんでとっつぁんが<line-break>追われてるのかって？<line-break>ハハ、別に悪さしたってんじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Hm? You want to know why the old man's wanted? Ha, it's not as if he did anything wicked.<end>"
KO: "응? 왜 아저씨가 쫓기냐고? 하하, 딱히 나쁜 짓을 해서 그런 건 아니야.<end>"

## 1470110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わけを聞きたいかい？<end>"
EN: "Want to hear the story?<end>"
KO: "이유를 듣고 싶어?<end>"

## 1470111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞きたい<end>"

KO: "듣고 싶어<end>"

## 1470112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml

## 1470113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、ちょっと長いぜ。<line-break>アンギルダンは、かつてバロルのもとで<line-break>ディンガル統一のために戦った将軍で、<line-break>百戦錬磨の名将、<line-break>赤い巨星の通り名を持つ戦士だ。<end>"
EN: "All right, but it's a bit of a long story. Angeerdan was once a general under Balor, fighting to unite Dyneskal. He's a veteran of countless battles, a renowned commander and warrior known as the Red Giant.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 조금 길다. 앙길단은 과거 발로르 밑에서 딘갈 통일을 위해 싸운 장군으로, 백전노장의 명장, 붉은 거성이라는 별명을 가진 전사다.<end>"

## 1470114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、バロルが魔王になると<line-break>バロルとケンカして<line-break>ディンガルを飛び出しちまった。　　<line-break>その後、世界中を傭兵として<line-break>渡り歩いたが、<line-break>結局、どこにも落ち着かずじまい。<end>"
EN: "But when Balor became the Demon King, Angeerdan fell out with him and left Dyneskal. After that, he roamed the world as a mercenary but never settled down anywhere.<end>"
KO: "하지만 발로르가 마왕이 되자 발로르와 싸우고 딘갈을 떠나 버렸어. 그 뒤 세계 각지를 용병으로 떠돌았지만 결국 어느 곳에도 정착하지 못했지.<end>"

## 1470115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後、魔王バロルが、<line-break>勇者ネメアに倒された時、<line-break>再びディンガルの将軍になったんだが、<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクってのが、<line-break>無理難題をネメアに押しつけるのを<line-break>見かねて堂々と批判した。<end>"
EN: "Later, when the Hero Nemea defeated Balor, Demon King, Angeerdan became a general of Dyneskal once more. But when Emperor Elmark started saddling Nemea with impossible demands, Angeerdan couldn't stand by. He openly criticized the emperor.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 마왕 발로르가 용사 네메아에게 쓰러졌을 때 다시 딩갈의 장군이 됐지만, 황제 에류마르크가 네메아에게 무리한 요구를 떠넘기는 걸 보다 못해 대놓고 비판했지.<end>"

## 1470116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが原因で、皇帝に<line-break>処刑されかけるところを脱走。<line-break>今や、お尋ね者のご老体だ。<end>"
EN: "The emperor nearly had him executed for it, but he escaped. Now the old man's a wanted fugitive.<end>"
KO: "그게 원인이 되어 황제에게 처형당할 뻔하다 탈주. 지금은 수배 중인 노인이다.<end>"

## 1470117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、こういう風に長々と語るより、<line-break>落ち着きがなくて、一生を棒に振った<line-break>老傭兵といった方がわかりやすいかな。<end>"
EN: "But instead of going on at length like that, it might be simpler to call him a restless old mercenary who wasted his whole life.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이렇게 길게 이야기하는 것보다, 철이 없어서 인생을 망친 늙은 용병이라고 하는 편이 이해하기 쉽겠군.<end>"

## 1470118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人の過去にはこだわらないって？<line-break>いいね、そういうの好きだぜ。<end>"
EN: "You don't care about a man's past? I like that.<end>"
KO: "남의 과거에는 연연하지 않는다고? 좋네, 그런 거 마음에 들어.<end>"

## 1470119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、エンシャントの<line-break>門で落ち合うとしよう。<line-break>じゃ、行ってくるぜ。<end>"
EN: "All right, let's meet at the gates of Ancient. I'll be off, then.<end>"
KO: "그럼 엔샨트의문에서 만나자. 그럼 다녀올게.<end>"

## 1470120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　<value:$28>か。<end>"
EN: "Hm? <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "응? <value:$28>인가.<end>"

## 1470121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンのとっつぁんが<line-break>助け出していた子供たちのことは<line-break>ここのギルドの親父に頼んでおいた。<line-break>子供たちの話だと、逃げ出したのは<line-break>ルルアンタだけのようだ。<line-break>早く捜さんとな。<end>"
EN: "I've left the children old Angeerdan rescued in the care of the man at the Guild here. From what they said, it sounds like Lulluantha was the only one who escaped. We'd better find her fast.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 영감이 구해 둔 아이들은 여기 길드 주인에게 맡겼어. 아이들 말로는 도망친 건 릴비뿐인 모양이야. 빨리 찾아야겠군.<end>"

## 1470122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、そんじゃ、<line-break>俺は門の所で待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Right, then. I'll wait by the gate.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 난 문 앞에서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1470123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、<value:$28>。<line-break>今は仕事を受けている時じゃ<line-break>ないだろう？<line-break>ルルアンタひとりじゃ危険だ。<line-break>早く捜しだしてやろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. This is no time to be taking on jobs. Lulluantha's in danger out there alone. Come on, let's hurry and find her.<end>"
KO: "야, <value:$28>. 지금 일이나 받을 때가 아니잖아? 루루안타 혼자면 위험해. 빨리 찾아보자.<end>"

## 1470124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、<value:$28>。<line-break>のんびりと宿屋に泊まっていられる<line-break>状況じゃないだろう？<line-break>ルルアンタの身が心配だ。<line-break>早く助けに行ってやろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. This is no time to be lounging around at the inn, is it? I'm worried about Lulluantha. Let's hurry and go help her.<end>"
KO: "이봐, <value:$28>. 느긋하게 여관에 있을 상황이 아니잖아? 루루안타가 걱정돼. 빨리 구하러 가자.<end>"

## 1470125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>もう準備はいいんだな？<end>"
EN: "You're all set, right, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 준비는 다 됐지?<end>"

## 1470126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "準備できた<end>"
EN: "I'm ready.<end>"
KO: "준비됐다<end>"

## 1470127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ準備中<end>"
EN: "I'm still getting ready.<end>"
KO: "아직 준비 중<end>"

## 1470128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは賢者の森だ。<line-break>はやく助けてやろうぜ、<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>おっと、その前に体力を<line-break>回復しておかないとな。<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha is in Sage's Forest. Let's hurry and save her, <value:$28>. But first, we'd better recover our strength.<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 현자의 숲에 있어. 빨리 구하러 가자, <value:$28>. 아, 그 전에 체력부터 회복해야겠군.<end>"

## 1470129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんまりグズグズしていられる<line-break>状況じゃない。準備は肝心だが<line-break>急いでくれ。<end>"
EN: "We can't afford to dawdle. I know it's important to be prepared, but hurry.<end>"
KO: "너무 꾸물거릴 상황이 아니야. 준비는 중요하지만 서둘러 줘.<end>"

## 1470130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここが賢者の森だ。この森の奥には<line-break>運命に選ばれた者しか中に入れない、<line-break>賢者の住む屋敷があるそうだが…<line-break>今はそれどころじゃないな。<line-break>さぁ、さっさとルルアンタを<line-break>捜そうぜ。<end>"
EN: "This is Sage's Forest. They say that deep within it stands a sage's manor, which only those chosen by fate can enter... but we don't have time for that now. Come on, let's hurry up and find Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "여기가 현자의 숲이야. 이 숲 안쪽에는 운명에 선택된 자만 들어갈 수 있는 현자의 저택이 있다지만… 지금은 그럴 때가 아니지. 자, 빨리 릴비를 찾자.<end>"

## 1470131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、そっちは出口だぞ。<line-break>今はのんびりしていられる<line-break>状況じゃないぜ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, that's the way out. This is no time to be taking it easy, you know?<end>"
KO: "어이, 그쪽은 출구야. 지금은 느긋하게 있을 상황이 아니라고?<end>"

## 1470132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ。あのリルビーのガキめ。<line-break>いったい、どこに逃げやがったんだ。　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Damn that Lilubee brat. Where the hell did she run off to?<end>"
KO: "쳇. 그 릴비 꼬맹이. 대체 어디로 도망친 거야.<end>"

## 1470133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカ野郎が。<line-break>しっかり見張っとけと言ったのに、　　<line-break>お前が油断してるからだろうが。<end>"
EN: "You idiot! I told you to keep a close watch. She got away because you let your guard down!<end>"
KO: "이 바보야. 제대로 지켜보랬는데 네가 방심해서 그런 거잖아.<end>"

## 1470134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え？　その…そうでしたっけね。　　　<line-break>へっへ…。<line-break>すいませんです、はい。<line-break>でも、リルビーのガキがひとりで<line-break>こんな森の中で迷ったら、<line-break>モンスターに食われておしまいですぜ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? Uh... did you? Heh heh... Sorry, sir. But if that Lilubee brat gets lost in these woods all alone, she'll wind up monster food.<end>"
KO: "예? 그… 그랬던가요. 헤헤…. 죄송합니다, 예. 하지만 릴비 꼬맹이가 혼자 이런 숲에서 길을 잃으면 몬스터에게 먹혀 끝장이라고요.<end>"

## 1470135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まぁ、いい。エンシャントでの<line-break>仕事をすませて、さっさと<line-break>お頭の所に戻らねばならんからな。　　<end>"
EN: "Never mind. We need to finish the job in Ancient and get back to the boss.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 됐다. 엔샨트에서 일을 끝내고 빨리 두목한테 돌아가야 하니까.<end>"

## 1470136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、その必要はないぜ。<line-break>お頭もエンシャントの子供たちも<line-break>戻ったところで、もういないからな。<end>"
EN: "No need for that. By the time you get back, your boss and the children from Ancient will already be gone.<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 그럴 필요 없어. 두목도 엔샨트의 아이들도 돌아가 봐야 이미 없으니까.<end>"

## 1470137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何者だ、お前ら！<line-break>こんな所をうろついているとは　　　　<line-break>怪しい奴め！<end>"
EN: "Who are you? Anyone prowling around a place like this has to be up to no good!<end>"
KO: "너희는 누구냐! 이런 곳을 어슬렁거리다니 수상한 놈들!<end>"

## 1470138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どうやら俺たちが何者なのか、<line-break>知っているようだな。<line-break>さっきのセリフ。どういう意味だ？　　<end>"
EN: "You seem to know who we are. What did you mean by that?<end>"
KO: "…아무래도 우리가 누군지 알고 있는 것 같군. 아까 그 말, 무슨 뜻이지?<end>"

## 1470139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言葉通りの意味さ。もう、<line-break>あんたらに戻るところはないぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>さぁ、ルルアンタの居場所を<line-break>教えてもらおうか？<end>"
EN: "Exactly what I said. You've got nowhere to go back to now. So, why don't you tell us where Lulluantha is?<end>"
KO: "말 그대로야. 이제 너희에게 돌아갈 곳은 없어. 자, 루루안타가 어디 있는지 말해 주실까?<end>"

## 1470140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど、あのリルビーの娘を　　　<line-break>捜しに来たってわけか。<line-break>そう言えば、見知った顔がいる。<end>"
EN: "So you've come looking for that Lilubee girl. Come to think of it, I recognize one of you.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군, 그 릴비라는 아이를 찾으러 왔다는 거구나. 그러고 보니 낯익은 얼굴도 있네.<end>"

## 1470141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、フリントのガキだな。　　　　<line-break>親父を殺した俺が憎かろう。<line-break>どうだ、俺と勝負してみるか？<line-break>俺に勝てたら、<line-break>フリント暗殺を依頼してきた奴の<line-break>名を教えてやる。知りたいだろう？<end>"
EN: "You're Flint's brat, aren't you? You must hate me for killing your father. How about taking me on? Beat me, and I'll tell you who hired us to assassinate Flint. You want to know, don't you?<end>"
KO: "너, 플린트의 자식이군. 네 아비를 죽인 내가 밉겠지. 어때, 나와 승부해 볼래? 나를 이기면 플린트 암살을 의뢰한 놈의 이름을 알려 주지. 알고 싶잖아?<end>"

## 1470142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめとけ、<value:$28>。<line-break>挑発に乗るんじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Don't do it, <value:$28>. Don't rise to the bait.<end>"
KO: "그만둬, <value:$28>. 도발에 넘어가지 마.<end>"

## 1470143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一対一で勝負する<end>"
EN: "Fight one-on-one<end>"
KO: "일대일로 승부한다<end>"

## 1470144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "挑発には乗らない<end>"
EN: "Ignore the taunt<end>"
KO: "도발에 넘어가지 않는다<end>"

## 1470145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、そうこなくっちゃな。<line-break>見事に親父の仇を取ってみやがれ！　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That's more like it! Let's see you avenge your father!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그래야지. 멋지게 아버지의 원수를 갚아 봐!<end>"

## 1470146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっ、ひぃ～～～～～！<line-break>こいつ、強すぎる～～～～～。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Eek! This one's too strong!<end>"
KO: "히, 히이~~~~! 이 녀석 너무 강해~~~~.<end>"

## 1470147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ、冷や冷やしたが、<line-break>よくやったな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Whew, you had me worried. Well done, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후우, 조마조마했지만 잘했어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1470148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ルルアンタの居場所と<line-break>ついでに、フリント暗殺の黒幕の名前、<line-break>言ってもらおうか？<end>"
EN: "Now tell us where Lulluantha is―and while you're at it, the name of whoever was behind Flint's assassination.<end>"
KO: "자, 루루안타가 어디 있는지랑 덤으로 플린트 암살의 배후가 누군지 말해 줄까?<end>"

## 1470149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ふ、フリントの暗殺を…<line-break>依頼してきたのは…ロストールの…<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンだ…。<end>"
EN: "The one who hired us to assassinate Flint was... Duke Lemghon of Elienai... from Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "…프, 플린트 암살을… 의뢰한 건… 로스톨의… 에리에나이 공 레무온이다….<end>"

## 1470150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公？　話にならんな。<end>"
EN: "The Duke of Elienai? That's absurd.<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공? 말이 안 되는군.<end>"

## 1470151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公の人柄は知っている。<line-break>盗賊に暗殺を頼むような奴じゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>おおかた、暗殺を依頼した奴に、<line-break>何かあったらそう言うように<line-break>命令されたってところだな。<end>"
EN: "I know what the Duke of Elienai is like. The Duke would never hire bandits for an assassination. Whoever really hired you must have ordered you to give that name if anything went wrong.<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공의 사람 됨됨이는 알아. 도적에게 암살을 부탁할 사람이 아니야. 아마 암살을 의뢰한 놈이 무슨 일이 생기면 그렇게 말하라고 명령했겠지.<end>"

## 1470152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…し、信じるかどうかは…　　　　<line-break>貴様らの勝手だぜ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Believe it or not... that's up to you.<end>"
KO: "…미, 믿을지 말지는… 너희 마음대로 해….<end>"

## 1470153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いい。<line-break>で、ルルアンタはどこにやった？<end>"
EN: "Fine. Now, what did you do with Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 됐어. 그래서 루루안타는 어디에 두고 왔어?<end>"

## 1470154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、ビビッちまったか？<line-break>まぁ、いい。知られてるとあっては<line-break>貴様らを生かしておくわけにはいかん。<line-break>おい、野郎ども！<line-break>抜かるんじゃないぜ！<end>"
EN: "What's wrong, lost your nerve? No matter. Now that you've found us out, we can't let any of you live. Come on, boys! Don't let your guard down!<end>"
KO: "왜, 겁먹었냐? 뭐 됐다. 알려진 이상 네놈들을 살려 둘 수는 없지. 이봐, 녀석들! 실수하지 마라!<end>"

## 1470155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>仮にもフリントを倒した相手だ。<line-break>気を引き締めてくれよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, this is the one who defeated Flint. Stay sharp.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 어쨌든 플린트를 쓰러뜨린 상대다. 정신 바짝 차려.<end>"

## 1470156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっ、ひぃ～～～～～！<line-break>こいつ、強すぎる～～～～～。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Eek! This one's too strong!<end>"
KO: "히, 히이~~~~! 이 녀석 너무 강해~~~~.<end>"

## 1470157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ、よくやったな、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Whew. Well done, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후우, 잘했어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1470158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、ルルアンタの居場所を<line-break>言ってもらおうか？<end>"
EN: "Now tell us where Lulluantha is.<end>"
KO: "자, 루루안타가 어디 있는지 말해 주실까?<end>"

## 1470159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あのリルビーの娘なら<line-break>今ごろ…この森に住み着いている　<line-break>モンスターどもの腹の中だぜ…。<end>"
EN: "That Lilubee girl? By now she's in the belly of one of the monsters living in these woods.<end>"
KO: "…그 릴비 여자애라면 지금쯤… 이 숲에 사는 몬스터들 뱃속에 있겠지….<end>"

## 1470160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと！？<end>"
EN: "What!?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고!?<end>"

## 1470161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ウソじゃないぜ。あのガキには　<line-break>逃げられちまったんだよ…。<line-break>　<line-break>チビひとりじゃ…どのみち<line-break>この森からは抜けだせん…。<line-break>ざ、残念だったな…ぐふっ。<end>"
EN: "I'm not lying. The brat got away from us. But a little runt like her could never make it out of these woods alone. Too bad... Guh!<end>"
KO: "…거짓말 아니야. 그 꼬맹이는 놓쳐 버렸어…. 꼬마 혼자선… 어차피 이 숲에서 빠져나갈 수 없어…. 자, 잘됐네… 크흑.<end>"

## 1470162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずいことになったな。　　　<line-break>早くルルアンタを捜そう。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつの言うとおり、<line-break>女の子ひとりで抜け出せるような<line-break>森じゃない。急いで捜すんだ。<end>"
EN: "This is bad. We have to find Lulluantha, fast. Just as he said, this isn't a forest a girl can make it out of alone. Hurry. We have to find her.<end>"
KO: "큰일이군. 빨리 루루안타를 찾자. 이 녀석 말대로 여자아이 혼자 빠져나올 수 있는 숲이 아니야. 서둘러 찾아야 해.<end>"

## 1470163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いが今は海がしけていてな。<line-break>出航を見合わせているんだ。<line-break>しばらくは船を出せそうにないぜ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but the sea's too rough right now. Departures are on hold, and it doesn't look like the ship will be sailing for a while.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 지금은 바다가 거칠어서 출항을 보류 중이야. 한동안은 배를 띄우기 어려울 것 같아.<end>"

## 1470164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、ロストールのギルドで<line-break>受けた仕事が残ってるだろう？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まだ捕まっている子供がいるみたいだ。<line-break>次の仕事を引き受ける前に、<line-break>今の仕事を終わらせてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "You still have a job from the Guild in Rostorl to finish, don't you? Apparently, at least one child is still being held captive. Could you finish that job before taking on another one?<end>"
KO: "너, 로스톨 길드에서 받은 일이 아직 남아 있지? 아직 붙잡혀 있는 아이가 있는 모양이야. 다음 일을 맡기 전에 지금 일을 끝내 주겠어?<end>"

## 1470165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec147-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしたんだ、<value:$28>？<line-break>まだ引き受けた仕事は<line-break>終わってないんだろう？　　　　　<line-break>子供たちの命がかかっているんだ。<line-break>今は、飢えた者の迷宮へ<line-break>急いでくれないか？<end>"
EN: "What's wrong, <value:$28>? You haven't finished the job you took on yet, have you? The children's lives are at stake. Could you hurry to the Labyrinth of the Hungry right away?<end>"
KO: "왜 그래, <value:$28>? 아직 맡은 일을 끝내지 못했잖아? 아이들 목숨이 걸려 있어. 지금은 굶주린 자의 미궁으로 서둘러 주지 않겠어?<end>"
