## 1480001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは…。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>いや、ここがそうなのか…。<line-break>　<line-break>前に来たときは、<line-break>こんな屋敷はなかったはずだが…<end>"
EN: "This place... No, so this is it... I'm sure there was no manor here the last time I came through...<end>"
KO: "여기는…. 아니, 여기가 그곳인가…. 전에 왔을 때는 이런 저택이 없었던 것 같은데….<end>"

## 1480002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ここは結界を張っています。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Heh. This place is protected by a barrier.<end>"
KO: "후후, 여기는 결계를 쳐 두었습니다.<end>"

## 1480003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 1480004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命に選ばれた人だけが、<line-break>結界を抜けてここに入ってくるのです。<line-break>　<line-break>ですから、あなたほどの冒険者でも　　<line-break>ここに来たことがないんですよ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Only those chosen by fate can pass through the barrier and enter. That is why even an adventurer of your stature has never been here before.<end>"
KO: "운명에 선택받은 사람만 결계를 지나 이곳에 들어옵니다. 그래서 당신만 한 모험자도 이곳에 온 적이 없는 거죠.<end>"

## 1480005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。<line-break>お待ちしていましたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>私はオルファウス。<line-break>この猫屋敷の主です。<end>"
EN: "Welcome. I have been expecting you. I am Orphaus, master of Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요. 기다리고 있었습니다. 저는 오르파우스. 이 고양이 저택의 주인입니다.<end>"

## 1480006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ということは、あんたが<line-break>この森に住んでいるという<line-break>賢者なのか？<end>"
EN: "Then you're the sage who's said to live in this forest?<end>"
KO: "…그 말은 당신이 이 숲에 산다는 현자인가?<end>"

## 1480007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ。私は自分で賢者と<line-break>名乗ったことなどありませんが、<line-break>そのように呼ぶ人もいますね。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. I have never called myself a sage, though there are some who call me one.<end>"
KO: "후후. 저는 스스로 현자라고 칭한 적은 없습니다만 그렇게 부르는 사람도 있지요.<end>"

## 1480008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうか、あんたが。<line-break>…おっと、自分のことを名乗るのを<line-break>忘れていたな。<line-break>俺はゼネテス。<line-break>こっちは<value:$28>。<line-break>ふたりとも冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "So you're the one... Ah, I nearly forgot to introduce myself. I'm Xenetes. This is <value:$28>. We're both adventurers.<end>"
KO: "…그런가, 네가. …아차, 내 소개를 잊었군. 난 제네테스. 이쪽은 <value:$28>. 둘 다 모험자다.<end>"

## 1480009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "連れのリルビーの女の子が<line-break>この森に迷い込んでしまってな。<line-break>今、捜している最中なんだが…。<end>"
EN: "A Lilubee girl traveling with us wandered into this forest. We're looking for her now...<end>"
KO: "함께 다니는 릴비 여자아이가 이 숲에서 길을 잃었어. 지금 찾고 있는 중인데….<end>"

## 1480010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。あなた方が捜している<line-break>女の子はルルアンタさんと<line-break>おっしゃいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Would the girl you are seeking happen to be named Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "후후. 당신들이 찾고 있는 여자아이는 루루안타 씨라고 하지 않나요?<end>"

## 1480011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして、ルルアンタのことを<line-break>知っている？<end>"
EN: "How do you know Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 루루안타를 알고 있지?<end>"

## 1480012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタさんなら、先ほど<line-break>森の中でお会いしましてね。<line-break>今は中にいらっしゃいますよ。<end>"
EN: "I met Lulluantha in the forest a short while ago. She is inside now.<end>"
KO: "루루안타 씨라면 조금 전에 숲에서 만났습니다. 지금은 안에 계십니다.<end>"

## 1480013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ここで立ち話もなんですし、<line-break>とりあえず、中に入りませんか？<end>"
EN: "Heh. Rather than stand here talking, why don't we go inside for now?<end>"
KO: "후후, 여기서 서서 이야기하기도 그러니 일단 안으로 들어가지 않을래요?<end>"

## 1480014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ？　あぁ、そうだな。　　　　<line-break>中に入ってみよう。<line-break>　<line-break>よかったな、<value:$28>、<line-break>ルルアンタが無事で。<end>"
EN: "Huh? Oh, right. Let's go inside. Good news, <value:$28>. Lulluantha's safe.<end>"
KO: "어? 아, 그렇군. 안으로 들어가 보자. 잘됐네, <value:$28>. 루루안타가 무사해서.<end>"

## 1480015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんじゃ、行ってみますか。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let's go.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 가보자.<end>"

## 1480016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ…、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "아…, <value:$28>….<end>"

## 1480017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふたりで捜しに来てくれた<line-break>そうですよ。<line-break>よかったですね、ルルアンタさん。<end>"
EN: "They both came looking for you. Isn't that wonderful, Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "두 분이 함께 찾으러 와 주셨대요. 잘됐네요, 루루안타 씨.<end>"

## 1480018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、ルルアンタを追って<line-break>こんな所まで来てくれたんだ…。<line-break>ありがとう…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you came all this way looking for Lulluantha... Thank you...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 루루안타를 쫓아 여기까지 와 줬구나…. 고마워….<end>"

## 1480019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんね、<value:$28>。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>フリントさんが亡くなって、<line-break><value:$28>が一番つらいときに、<line-break>こんなことになっちゃって。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, <value:$28>. Flint died, and now, when <value:$28> is having the hardest time, this had to happen...<end>"
KO: "미안해, <value:$28>. 플린트 씨가 돌아가시고 <value:$28>가 가장 힘들 때 이런 일이 생겨 버려서.<end>"

## 1480020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、お使いに行くときに<line-break>フリントさんを襲ってきた<line-break>人を見つけて…<line-break>それで、あとを付けていったら<line-break>逆に捕まっちゃって…<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha was out on an errand when she saw the person who attacked Flint... So Lulluantha followed, but got caught instead...<end>"
KO: "루루안타, 심부름 가다가 플린트 씨를 습격한 사람을 발견해서… 뒤를 밟다가 거꾸로 붙잡혀 버렸어….<end>"

## 1480021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタ、<value:$28>に<line-break>迷惑かけちゃった…。<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha caused trouble for <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "루루안타, <value:$28>에게 폐를 끼쳐 버렸어….<end>"

## 1480022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひっく、ひっく…<end>"
EN: "Sniff... sniff...<end>"
KO: "흐윽, 흐윽…<end>"

## 1480023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ～ん！！！<line-break><value:$28>～！<line-break>こわかったよぉ～～～！<end>"
EN: "Waaaaah! <value:$28>! I was so scared!<end>"
KO: "으아앙!!! <value:$28>~! 무서웠어~~~!<end>"

## 1480024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたがルルアンタを<line-break>助けてくれたんだな。<line-break>礼を言う。<end>"
EN: "You're the one who saved Lulluantha. You have my thanks.<end>"
KO: "네가 루루안타를 구해줬구나. 고맙다.<end>"

## 1480025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえいえ、森を歩いていたら<line-break>迷子に出会ったものですから。<line-break>助けるのは当然というものですよ。　　<line-break>それより、皆さんお疲れのようですし、<line-break>久々の再会で話すことも<line-break>たくさんあるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Not at all. I happened upon a lost child while walking through the forest. Helping her was only natural. More importantly, you all look tired, and after so long apart, you must have much to talk about.<end>"
KO: "아닙니다. 숲을 걷다가 길 잃은 아이를 만났으니 돕는 건 당연한 일이지요. 그보다 여러분 모두 지쳐 보이시고, 오랜만의 재회라 나눌 이야기도 많으시겠군요.<end>"

## 1480026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "腕によりをかけて<line-break>晩御飯を作りますから、<line-break>ゆっくり休んでいってください。<end>"
EN: "I shall prepare a splendid dinner, so please stay and rest awhile.<end>"
KO: "솜씨를 발휘해서 저녁을 만들 테니 푹 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 1480027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまない。<line-break>そうさせてもらえると助かる。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. We'd appreciate that.<end>"
KO: "미안해. 그렇게 해 주면 고맙겠어.<end>"

## 1480028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん、<line-break>おはようございます！！<end>"
EN: "Good morning, Orphaus!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 좋은 아침입니다!!<end>"

## 1480029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ。<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタは本当に元気ですね。<end>"
EN: "Good morning, <value:$28>, Lulluantha. You certainly are full of energy, Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, <value:$28>, 루루안타. 루루안타는 정말 활기차네요.<end>"

## 1480030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。<line-break>ありがとうございます！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Thank you!<end>"
KO: "후후. 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1480032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ寝てるよぉ。<line-break>ほんと、お寝坊さんなんだからぁ！<end>"
EN: "He's still asleep. What a sleepyhead!<end>"
KO: "아직 자고 있어. 정말 늦잠꾸러기라니까!<end>"

## 1480033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朝からうるせーぞ！<line-break>このガキ！<end>"
EN: "Quit making such a racket this early, you brat!<end>"
KO: "아침부터 시끄러워! 이 꼬맹이!<end>"

## 1480034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいてててって…。う～。<end>"
EN: "Ow, ow, ow... Ugh.<end>"
KO: "아야야야…. 으~.<end>"

## 1480035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、ブサイクな猫がしゃべった！<end>"
EN: "Whoa! That ugly cat just talked!<end>"
KO: "와, 못생긴 고양이가 말했어!<end>"

## 1480036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は猫じゃない、ネモだ。<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士ネモ！　っててて…。<end>"
EN: "I'm not a cat. I'm Nemo―Nemo, Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction! Ow, ow...<end>"
KO: "난 고양이가 아니야, 네모다. 파괴신의 원탁기사 네모! 아야야….<end>"

## 1480037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、すごいすごーい！！<end>"
EN: "Wow! That's amazing!<end>"
KO: "와, 대단해 대단해ー!!<end>"

## 1480038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "騒ぐなって。頭に響く。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>オルファウスとゼネテスと<line-break>朝まで飲んでて、ふつか酔いなんだよ。<line-break>いてててって…。<end>"
EN: "Keep it down. You're making my head pound. I was drinking with Orphaus and Xenetes until dawn, and now I'm hungover. Ow, ow, ow...<end>"
KO: "떠들지 마. 머리에 울려. 오르파우스와 제네테스와 아침까지 마셔서 숙취라고. 아야야….<end>"

## 1480039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士とは言っても、<line-break>昔の話です。<line-break>心配はいりませんよ。<line-break>昔、勇者ネメアに倒されてからは<line-break>猫の姿に封印されて、<line-break>今はなんの力も残っていませんから。<end>"
EN: "He may have been a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction, but that was long ago. There is no need to worry. Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated him, he has been sealed in the form of a cat. He has no power left now.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사라고 해도 옛날 이야기입니다. 걱정하지 마세요. 옛날 용사 네메아에게 쓰러진 뒤 고양이 모습으로 봉인되어 지금은 아무 힘도 남아 있지 않으니까요.<end>"

## 1480040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、さすがは破壊神の円卓騎士　<line-break>ですよね。猫の姿になってなお<line-break>人語を操るんですから。<end>"
EN: "Still, he is a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction. Even in the form of a cat, he can speak the human tongue.<end>"
KO: "그래도 역시 파괴신의 원탁기사답네요. 고양이 모습이 되고도 사람 말을 하니까요.<end>"

## 1480042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、これ何？<line-break>これ何？<end>"
EN: "Oh! What's this? What is it?<end>"
KO: "앗, 이거 뭐야? 이거 뭐야?<end>"

## 1480043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。<line-break>この子は転送機っていうんです。<line-break>使ってみましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. This little device is called a teleporter. Shall we try it?<end>"
KO: "후후. 이 아이는 전송기라고 해요. 한번 써 볼까요.<end>"

## 1480044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ！　ゼネテスが出てきたぁ！<line-break>どうなってるのぉ？<end>"
EN: "Whoa! Xenetes came out! How did that happen?<end>"
KO: "와! 제네테스가 나왔어! 어떻게 된 거야?<end>"

## 1480045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ。この子を使えば<line-break>このように、遠くにいる人を<line-break>呼び寄せたりできるんですよ。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. With this device, you can summon someone from far away, just as you saw.<end>"
KO: "우후후. 이 아이를 쓰면 이렇게 멀리 있는 사람을 불러올 수도 있답니다.<end>"

## 1480046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へぇー。すごいんですねぇ。<line-break>オルファウスさんのお家って<line-break>不思議なものがいっぱい…。<end>"
EN: "Wow, that's amazing. Your house is full of mysterious things, Orphaus...<end>"
KO: "헤에~ 대단하네요. 오르파우스 씨 집에는 신기한 게 잔뜩….<end>"

## 1480047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、ゼネテスったらぁ。<line-break>みっともないんだからぁ。<end>"
EN: "Honestly, Xenetes. What a disgraceful sight.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 제네테스도 참. 꼴사납다니까.<end>"

## 1480048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれだけ飲めば、仕方ないですかね。<line-break>私ですら多少厳しいんですから…。<line-break>　<line-break>そうそう、それと<line-break>この子は逆に、ここにいる人を<line-break>遠くに送り出すこともできますよ。<end>"
EN: "Given how much we drank, I suppose it was inevitable. Even I found it a little taxing... Ah, yes. This device can also send someone here to a distant place.<end>"
KO: "그만큼 마셨으면 어쩔 수 없겠네요. 저조차 조금 버거우니까요…. 아, 그리고 이 아이는 반대로 여기 있는 사람을 먼 곳으로 보내는 것도 가능합니다.<end>"

## 1480049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいすごーい！<end>"
EN: "Amazing!<end>"
KO: "대단해, 대단해~!<end>"

## 1480051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後はパーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから<line-break>こんな無様なことはありませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, from now on, I'll obtain your party members' consent before summoning them. You won't see such an undignified spectacle again.<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버의 허락을 받은 뒤 불러낼 테니 이런 꼴사나운 일은 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、ゼネテス！<end>"
EN: "Good morning, Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, 제네테스!<end>"

## 1480053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはようございます。<line-break>気分はどうですか？<end>"
EN: "Good morning. How are you feeling?<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침입니다. 기분은 어떠세요?<end>"

## 1480054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…最悪だ。<line-break>朝まで飲んで寝てるところを<line-break>実験台に使われたんだからな…。<end>"
EN: "Awful. I drank until dawn, fell asleep, and then got used as a test subject...<end>"
KO: "…최악이야. 아침까지 마시고 자고 있는 걸실험 대상으로 써 버렸으니까….<end>"

## 1480055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、以後は<line-break>そんなことはありませんから。<end>"
EN: "Hee. I assure you, it won't happen again.<end>"
KO: "후후, 앞으로는 그런 일 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、信じてるぜ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I'll hold you to that.<end>"
KO: "후후, 믿고 있어.<end>"

## 1480057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、すっかり<line-break>長居しちまったからな。<line-break>そろそろ、おいとまするとしようか？<end>"
EN: "Well, we've stayed more than long enough. Shall we be on our way?<end>"
KO: "자, 너무 오래 있었군. 슬슬 실례하도록 할까?<end>"

## 1480058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、もうお帰りですか？<line-break>あなたたちに、ひとつ仕事をお願い<line-break>しようかと思っていたんですが…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, are you leaving already? I was hoping to ask you to take on a job for me...<end>"
KO: "어라, 벌써 돌아가시나요? 여러분께 한 가지 일을 부탁할까 했는데….<end>"

## 1480059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん。<line-break>最終的な判断は　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>に任せるが、<line-break>そろそろ<value:$28>に、<line-break>ロストールのお偉いさん方から<line-break>なんらかの接触があると思うぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. The final decision is yours, <value:$28>, but I expect the important people in Rostorl will be contacting <value:$28> before long.<end>"
KO: "음. 최종 판단은 <value:$28>에게 맡기겠지만 슬슬 로스톨의 높은 분들이 <value:$28>에게 어떤 식으로든 접촉해 올 것 같아.<end>"

## 1480060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんはエリスの密使の子だ。　　　<line-break>だから、ロストール王家の秘密を<line-break>聞かされてると思われてるだろう。<line-break>エリス側か、あるいはエリスに<line-break>敵対する側かが、お前さんに<line-break>働きかけてくるのはまず間違いない。<end>"
EN: "You're the child of Eris's secret envoy. They'll assume you've been told the secrets of the Rostorl Royal House. Either Eris's faction or its enemies are almost certain to approach you.<end>"
KO: "너는 에리스의 밀사의 자식이다. 그러니 로스톨 왕가의 비밀을 들었다고 여길 거다. 에리스 측이든 에리스와 적대하는 측이든 너에게 접근해 올 가능성이 높아.<end>"

## 1480061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんは何も知らんようだが、<line-break>残念ながら、お前さんが知ってるか<line-break>どうかは問題じゃない。<line-break>お前さんが知っていると、<line-break>向こうが思いこんでいることが<line-break>問題なんだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't seem to know anything, but unfortunately, whether you actually know is beside the point. What matters is that they believe you do.<end>"
KO: "자네는 아무것도 모르는 모양이지만, 안타깝게도 자네가 알고 있느냐가 문제는 아니네. 상대가 자네가 알고 있다고 믿고 있는 게 문제지.<end>"

## 1480062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さーてと。<line-break>俺はそろそろロストールに戻るか。<line-break>こう見えてもけっこう忙しいんでね。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it's time I went back to Rostorl. Believe it or not, I'm a busy man.<end>"
KO: "자, 그럼. 난 슬슬 로스톨로 돌아갈까. 이래 보여도 꽤 바쁘거든.<end>"

## 1480063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えー？<line-break>ゼネテス、もう行っちゃうのぉ？<end>"
EN: "What? Xenetes, you're leaving already?<end>"
KO: "에에? 제네테스, 벌써 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1480064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ。ルルアンタがいれば、<line-break><value:$28>は大丈夫だろ？<end>"
EN: "Yeah. With Lulluantha around, <value:$28> should be all right, don't you think?<end>"
KO: "그래. 루루안타가 있으면 <value:$28>은 괜찮겠지?<end>"

## 1480065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>。お前はギルドで<line-break>受けた誘拐事件の依頼も達成している。<line-break>もう、立派な冒険者だ。<line-break>ルルアンタと一緒に、<line-break>お前の冒険を続けるんだ。<line-break>仕事の報酬はその行動資金にしてくれ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you have fulfilled the Guild's request to solve the kidnapping case. You are already a fine adventurer. Continue your adventure with Lulluantha. Use the reward from this job to fund your journey.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>. 너는 길드에서 받은 유괴 사건 의뢰도 해결했다. 이제 훌륭한 모험가야. 릴비와 함께 네 모험을 계속해라. 일의 보수는 그 활동 자금으로 써.<end>"

## 1480066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁに、一生の別れというんじゃない。　<line-break>俺の力が必要になったら、ロストールの<line-break>スラムを訪ねてきてくれればいい。<line-break>じゃあ、またな、<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ。<line-break>オルファウスさんにも世話になったな。<end>"
EN: "Come on, it's not as if we'll never meet again. If you need my help, come find me in the Slums of Rostorl. Until next time, <value:$28>, Lulluantha. And thank you for everything, Orphaus.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 평생 헤어지는 것도 아니잖아. 내 힘이 필요해지면 로스톨 빈민가로 찾아오면 돼. 그럼 또 보자, <value:$28>, 루루안타. 오르파우스 씨에게도 신세 많이 졌네.<end>"

## 1480067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、行っちゃったね…。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes is gone...<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 가 버렸네….<end>"

## 1480068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもでもぉ、ルルアンタは<line-break>いつまでも<value:$28>と<line-break>一緒にいるからね！<end>"
EN: "But Lulluantha will stay with <value:$28> forever!<end>"
KO: "그래도 루루안타는 계속 <value:$28>이랑 함께 있을 거야!<end>"

## 1480069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ゼネテスの話では<line-break>あなたはロストール王家に<line-break>因縁があるようですからね。　<line-break>無理強いはしませんが、ひとつ<line-break>私の仕事の依頼を聞いては<line-break>もらえませんか？<end>"
EN: "Now, from what Xenetes said, it seems you have some connection to the Rostorl Royal House. I won't force you, but would you at least hear about a job I'd like you to undertake?<end>"
KO: "자, 제네테스의 말로는 당신은 로스톨 왕가와 인연이 있는 모양이군요. 억지로 시키진 않겠지만 제 일 의뢰 하나 들어 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1480070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう質問はありません<end>"
EN: "I don't have any more questions.<end>"
KO: "이제 질문은 없습니다.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"

## 1480076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<end>"
EN: "This device will allow you to summon companions you meet on your travels. If you ever wish to change who travels with you, come to the Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1480077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러내거나, 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 1480078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　<line-break>口は悪いですけれど<line-break>無害ですよ。<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれていましたが<line-break>地上に落ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "That would be the ugly cat right here. He's foul-mouthed, but harmless. He was once a spirit of the Old Man Star and was also known as the Sage of the Heavens, but then he fell to earth and became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 입은 험하지만 해는 없어요. 원래는 노인성의 정령으로 천공의 현자라 불렸지만, 지상에 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 1480079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで、<line-break>名前を知るものの異名を持ちます。<end>"
EN: "One of the God of Destruction's powerful demons and one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, known by the epithet “He Who Knows the Name.”<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 열두 원탁기사 중 한 명이며 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명을 지녔습니다.<end>"

## 1480081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れないように<line-break>覚えておかなくちゃ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'd better remember that so I don't forget.<end>"
KO: "잊지 않도록 기억해 둬야겠어.<end>"

## 1480082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ？　なんだって？<line-break>…あいてててって。う～。<line-break>話はオルファウスに聞いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Huh? What did you say? Ow, ow, ow... Ugh. Ask Orphaus about it.<end>"
KO: "아앙? 뭐라고? …아야야야. 으으. 이야기는 올파우스한테 들어.<end>"

## 1480083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>もう、行っちゃうの？<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Are you leaving already?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 벌써 가는 거야?<end>"

## 1480084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、オルファウスさんに<line-break>ちゃんとお礼を言ってからね。<end>"
EN: "Then make sure you thank Orphaus properly before we go.<end>"
KO: "그럼 오르파우스 씨에게 제대로 감사 인사를 한 다음에.<end>"

## 1480085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん。<line-break>本当にお世話になりましたぁ。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus, thank you so much for everything.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 정말 신세 많이 졌어요.<end>"

## 1480086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いえいえ、お礼を言われるほどの<line-break>ことはしていませんよ。<line-break>私もふたりに会えてよかったです。<end>"
EN: "Not at all. I haven't done anything worth thanking me for. I'm glad I got to meet you both, too.<end>"
KO: "아니에요, 감사받을 만큼 대단한 일은 하지 않았습니다. 저도 두 분을 만나서 좋았어요.<end>"

## 1480087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん、<line-break>それじゃあ、行ってきまーす！<end>"
EN: "Well, Orphaus, we're off!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 그럼 다녀올게요!<end>"

## 1480089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あなたの<line-break>ソウルは無限の可能性を秘めている…<line-break>いわば、無限のソウルです。<line-break>世界を巡って人に出会い、<line-break>仲間を作って、<line-break>そのソウルを高めてください。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, your Soul holds limitless potential... It is, in a word, an Infinite Soul. Travel the world, meet people, find companions, and nurture that Soul.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 당신의 소울은 무한한 가능성을 품고 있습니다… 말하자면 무한의 소울입니다. 세계를 돌며 사람을 만나고 동료를 만들며 그 소울을 키워 주세요.<end>"

## 1480090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その中で世界は<line-break>様々な顔を見せてくれるでしょう。<line-break>自由な旅を！<end>"
EN: "Along the way, the world will show you many different faces. Travel freely!<end>"
KO: "그 안에서 세계는 여러 모습을 보여 줄 겁니다. 자유로운 여행을!<end>"

## 1480091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ゼネテスが言ってたんだけどぉ、　<line-break>この猫屋敷に入ってこれるのは、<line-break>運命に選ばれた人だけなんだって。<line-break>それでね、ゼネテスはこれまで<line-break>ここに入れなかったんだって。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Xenetes told me only people chosen by fate can enter Cat Manor. And Xenetes had never been able to get in before.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>. 제네테스가 그러는데 이 고양이 저택에 들어올 수 있는 건 운명에 선택받은 사람뿐이래. 그리고 제네테스는 지금까지 여기에 들어오지 못했대.<end>"

## 1480093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、<value:$28>は<line-break><value:$28>だよねぇ？<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ルルアンタの知ってる、<line-break><value:$28>だよねぇ？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28> is really <value:$28>, right? The <value:$28> Lulluantha knows, right?<end>"
KO: "있지, <value:$28>은 <value:$28> 맞지? 루루안타가 아는 <value:$28> 맞지?<end>"

## 1480094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよね！　よかった…。　　<line-break>ルルアンタ、これからも<line-break><value:$28>と一緒だよ！<line-break>それじゃぁ、<value:$28>。<line-break>出発進行！<end>"
EN: "That's right! Thank goodness... Lulluantha will stay with <value:$28> from now on! All right, <value:$28>. Full speed ahead!<end>"
KO: "그렇지! 다행이다…. 루루안타, 앞으로도 <value:$28>과 함께할 거야! 그럼 <value:$28>, 출발!<end>"

## 1480095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break>それでは、お話ししましょう。　　　<line-break>ことは魔人アーギルシャイアによって<line-break>ミイス村が焼き払われたことから<line-break>始まります。<end>"
EN: "I see. Thank you. Then let me explain. It began when the demon Argyleshire burned Miis Village to the ground.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까. 감사합니다. 그럼 말씀드리죠. 일은 마인 아르길샤이어가 미이스 마을을 불태운 것에서 시작됩니다.<end>"

## 1480096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイア？<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어?<end>"

## 1480097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアは<line-break>破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士と呼ばれる　<line-break>強力な魔人のひとりです。<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器をひとつ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire is one of the powerful demons known as the Knights of the Round Table serving Ulugh, the God of Destruction. She is a seductive beauty, cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground to obtain a Dark Artifact.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어는 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사라 불리는 강력한 마인 중 하나입니다. 요염한 미녀이며 잔혹하고 변덕스럽습니다. 어둠의 신기를 하나 손에 넣으려고 미이스를 불태워 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 1480098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器？<end>"
EN: "A Dark Artifact?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기?<end>"

## 1480099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器とは強大で凶々しい力を持つ<line-break>１２の魔道器のことで、破壊神ウルグの<line-break>復活に使われたと言われています。<line-break>ただ、彼女はその本来の使い方<line-break>以外のことで使おうとしています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The Dark Artifacts are twelve magical artifacts of immense and sinister power. They are said to have been used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. But Argyleshire intends to use one for something other than its original purpose.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기란 강대하고 흉악한 힘을 지닌 12개의 마도기로, 파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰였다고 합니다. 다만 그녀는 본래의 용도와 다른 일에 쓰려 하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1480100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り、<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を<line-break>破壊しようと考えているようです。<line-break>その合成怪物を作るための知識を<line-break>闇の神器のひとつ、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようとしているみたいですね。<end>"
EN: "It seems she plans to create the ultimate hybrid monster and use its power to destroy the world for amusement. She hopes to obtain the knowledge needed to create it from one of the Dark Artifacts: the Forbidden Grail.<end>"
KO: "그녀는 궁극의 합성괴물을 만들고 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴할 생각인 듯합니다. 그 합성괴물을 만들 지식을 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려는 모양이죠.<end>"

## 1480101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その禁断の聖杯が<line-break>アーギルシャイアの手に落ちる前に<line-break>取ってきてほしいというわけです。<end>"
EN: "That is why I want you to retrieve the Forbidden Grail before it falls into Argyleshire's hands.<end>"
KO: "그 금단의 성배가 아르길샤이어의 손에 넘어가기 전에 가져와 달라는 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかったら、早くエンシャントへ<line-break>行くんだ！　…って、うぅ<line-break>頭が…。<end>"
EN: "Now that you understand, hurry to Ancient! Ugh... my head...<end>"
KO: "알았으면 빨리 엔샨트로 가! …으윽, 머리가….<end>"

## 1480103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "現在、聖杯はゴブゴブ団と名乗る<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが盗み持っています。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、それを盗み出させたのは<line-break>魔人アーギルシャイアではなく、<line-break>このネモなのです。<end>"
EN: "The Grail is currently held by three Goblins calling themselves the Gob-Gob Gang, who stole it. But it was not the demon Argyleshire who had them steal it. It was Nemo.<end>"
KO: "현재 성배는 고브고브단을 자칭하는 고블린 세 마리가 훔쳐 가지고 있습니다. 하지만 그것을 훔쳐내게 한 건 마인 아르길샤이어가 아니라 이 네모입니다.<end>"

## 1480104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もともと聖杯は彼のものです。<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は　<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<line-break>神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと、<line-break>３匹のゴブリンたちを使って神器を<line-break>ロストールから盗み出させたのです。<end>"
EN: "The Grail originally belonged to him. When Nemo learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he resolved not to let her seize his artifact. Hoping to use its power to regain his true form, he had the three Goblins steal it from Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "원래 성배는 그의 것입니다. 아르길샤이어의 음모를 안 그는 자신의 신기를 빼앗기고 싶지 않았고, 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가기 위해 세 고블린에게 로스톨에서 신기를 훔쳐 오게 한 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、禁断の聖杯によって<line-break>知恵を身につけたゴブリンたちは　　　<line-break>ネモを見限ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "But the Forbidden Grail granted the Goblins wisdom, and they abandoned Nemo.<end>"
KO: "하지만 금단의 성배로 지혜를 얻은 고블린들은 네모를 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 1480106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ミイスの時のように<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンがいる町ごと<line-break>焼き払ってしまうかもしれないのです。<line-break>ですから、あなたに急いで聖杯を<line-break>取り戻してもらいたいのです。<end>"
EN: "As she did at Miis, Argyleshire may burn down the entire town where the Goblins holding the Grail are staying. That is why I need you to recover it quickly.<end>"
KO: "미이스 때처럼 성배를 가진 고블린이 있는 마을째 불태워 버릴지도 모릅니다. 그래서 당신이 서둘러 성배를 되찾아 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1480107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ。ありがとうございます。<line-break>それでは、聖杯のことお願いしますよ。<line-break>　<line-break>このようなことは、ソウルに巨大な<line-break>可能性を秘めている…無限のソウルを<line-break>持つ者にしか頼めませんから。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Thank you. Then I leave the Grail in your hands. This is something I could ask only of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul, whose Soul holds such tremendous potential.<end>"
KO: "후후. 감사합니다. 그럼 성배 일 부탁드립니다. 이런 일은 소울에 거대한 가능성을 품은… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자에게밖에 부탁할 수 없으니까요.<end>"

## 1480108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモの話だと、そのゴブリンたちは<line-break>エンシャントにいるという話でしたが、<line-break>急がないと移動してしまうでしょう。<end>"
EN: "According to Nemo, the Goblins are in Ancient, but they may move on if you don't hurry.<end>"
KO: "네모의 말로는 그 고블린들이 엔샨트에 있다고 했지만, 서두르지 않으면 이동해 버릴 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアは、<line-break>今も聖杯を手に入れようと<line-break>ねらっているはずです。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire must still be seeking the Grail.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어는 지금도 성배를 손에 넣으려 노리고 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他に何か、聞きたいことがあれば、<line-break>私にできる範囲でお答えしますよ。<end>"
EN: "If there is anything else you wish to ask, I'll answer as best I can.<end>"
KO: "그 밖에 궁금한 게 있다면 제가 할 수 있는 범위에서 답해 드릴게요.<end>"

## 1480111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か質問ですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you have a question?<end>"
KO: "뭔가 질문 있으신가요?<end>"

## 1480112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 1480113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 1480114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 1480115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、なかなか耳ざといですね。<line-break>フフ、今はまだ秘密です。<line-break>　<line-break>おいおいわかりますよ。<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you don't miss much, do you? Heh. For now, it remains a secret. You'll understand in time. For the moment, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "오, 귀가 밝군요. 후후, 지금은 아직 비밀입니다. 차차 알게 될 겁니다. 일단 길드에서 일을 해 나가세요.<end>"

## 1480116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで、<line-break>魂を成長させていけば<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。　　<end>"
EN: "As you work for others and nurture your soul, the answer will become clear in time.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시켜 가면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 1480117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられた強力な魔道器です。　<line-break>　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<end>"
EN: "It is a powerful magical artifact used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are said to be twelve such artifacts, and the Forbidden Grail is one of them.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 1480118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団という<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえずは、<line-break>エンシャントを探してみると<line-break>彼らに会えると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "At present, the Forbidden Grail is in the hands of three Goblins called the Gob-Gob Gang. For now, try looking in Ancient. I believe you'll find them there.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배라면 지금 고브고브단이라는 고블린 셋이 가지고 있습니다. 일단 엔샨트를 찾아보면 그들을 만날 수 있을 거예요.<end>"

## 1480119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 1480120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士のひとりです。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "She is one of the Knights of the Round Table who serve Ulugh, the God of Destruction, and takes the form of a bewitchingly beautiful woman. She is extraordinarily capricious and cruel. She has already burned Miis Village to the ground in pursuit of a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 하나입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 매우 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 손에 넣기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 1480121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から　　<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She seeks the Grail, hoping to learn from the divine artifact known as the Forbidden Grail how to create an Ultimate Life-Form. The other Knights of the Round Table include our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고, 그 제조법을 신기, 금단의 성배에서 얻기 위해 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 저희 네모 같은 이들이 있죠.<end>"

## 1480122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1480123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that about covers it. Does that make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 느낌입니다. 이해하셨나요?<end>"

## 1480124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未使用<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "미사용<end>"

## 1480125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ついたよぉ、<value:$28>。<line-break>これから、どこに行くのぉ？<end>"
EN: "We're here, <value:$28>! Where are we going now?<end>"
KO: "도착했어, <value:$28>. 이제 어디로 갈 거야?<end>"

## 1480126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子、<line-break><value:$28>ですね？<end>"
EN: "You're Flint's child, <value:$28>, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "플린트의 아이, <value:$28> 맞죠?<end>"

## 1480127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お姉ちゃんたちは誰？<line-break>どうして、フリントさんのこと<line-break>知ってるのぉ？<end>"
EN: "Who are you two? How do you know Flint?<end>"
KO: "언니들은 누구야? 왜 플린트를 알고 있어?<end>"

## 1480128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちはロストールのエリス様に<line-break>お仕えする者。<line-break>私がヴァイライラ。<end>"
EN: "We serve Lady Eris of Rostorl. I am Vailaila.<end>"
KO: "우리는 로스톨의 에리스 님을 섬기는 자들이다. 내가 바일라이라다.<end>"

## 1480129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はヴィアリアリ。<end>"
EN: "And I am Vialiali.<end>"
KO: "나는 비아리아리.<end>"

## 1480130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふ～ん、お姉ちゃんたち<line-break>顔がそっくりだねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Huh. You two look exactly alike.<end>"
KO: "흐음, 언니들 얼굴이 똑같이 생겼네.<end>"

## 1480131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私たちは双子なのよ。<line-break>だからね。<end>"
EN: "We're twins. That's why.<end>"
KO: "우린 쌍둥이야. 그러니까.<end>"

## 1480132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>エリス様がお呼びよ。<line-break>すぐにロストールまで来て。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Lady Eris has summoned you. Come to Rostorl at once.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 에리스 님이 부르셔. 바로 로스톨로 와.<end>"

## 1480133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス様はそのことだけ<line-break>伝えればよいと仰せでしたが、<line-break>どうです？　すぐに来てもらえますか？<end>"
EN: "Lady Eris instructed us only to deliver that message, but what do you say? Will you come at once?<end>"
KO: "에리스 님께서는 그것만 전하면 된다고 하셨습니다만, 어떻습니까? 바로 와 주시겠습니까?<end>"

## 1480134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐに行く<end>"
EN: "I'll go right away.<end>"
KO: "바로 간다<end>"

## 1480135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が向いたら行く<end>"
EN: "I'll go if I feel like it.<end>"
KO: "마음이 내키면 간다<end>"

## 1480136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。それはよかった。<line-break>私たちは先に戻ってエリス様に<line-break>そのことをお伝えするわ。<end>"
EN: "Good. We'll return ahead of you and tell Lady Eris.<end>"
KO: "그래. 잘됐네. 우리는 먼저 돌아가 에리스 님께 그 일을 전할게.<end>"

## 1480137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、<line-break>すぐにロストールの城を<line-break>訪ねて来てください。<end>"
EN: "Then please come to Rostorl Castle without delay.<end>"
KO: "그럼 곧바로 로스톨 성을 찾아와 주세요.<end>"

## 1480138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>ゼネテスの言ってたこと、<line-break>本当になっちゃったね。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what Xenetes said really did come true.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 제네테스가 한 말이 정말 현실이 됐네.<end>"

## 1480139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあじゃあ、用意ができたら　　<line-break>早くロストールに行こうよ。<line-break>ルルアンタも準備してくるから。<line-break>町を出るときに合流するからね。<line-break>ロストールにはゼネテスがいるから<line-break>また会えるね。楽しみだなぁ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's get ready and hurry to Rostorl! Lulluantha will go get ready too. We'll meet up when we leave town, all right? Xenetes is in Rostorl, so we'll get to see him again. I can't wait!<end>"
KO: "그럼 준비되면 빨리 로스톨로 가자. 룰루안타도 준비하고 올게. 마을을 나갈 때 합류할 테니까. 로스톨에는 제네테스가 있으니 또 만날 수 있겠네. 기대된다아.<end>"

## 1480140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう。<value:$28>は<line-break>ギルドで受けた仕事を達成してるから<line-break>ギルドに行けば報酬がもらえるよぉ。<line-break>だから、時間があったら<line-break>ロストールのギルドに行った方が<line-break>いいよ。じゃあ、またあとでねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right! You've finished that job you took from the Guild, <value:$28>, so you can collect your reward there. If there's time, you should stop by the Guild in Rostorl. See you later!<end>"
KO: "맞다 맞다. <value:$28>은 길드에서 받은 일을 끝냈으니까 길드에 가면 보수를 받을 수 있어어. 그러니까 시간 있으면 로스톨 길드에 가는 게 좋아. 그럼 나중에 봐아.<end>"

## 1480141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…仕方ないわ。<line-break>エリス様は無理に連れてくる必要は<line-break>ないとおっしゃっていたから。<end>"
EN: "I see. Very well. Lady Eris did say there was no need to bring you by force.<end>"
KO: "그래… 어쩔 수 없네. 에리스 님도 억지로 데려올 필요는 없다고 하셨으니까.<end>"

## 1480142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、エリス様よりの伝言、<line-break>確かに伝えましたよ。遠からず、<line-break>ロストールの城まで来てください。<end>"
EN: "Then Lady Eris's message has been duly delivered. Please come to Rostorl Castle before too long.<end>"
KO: "그럼 에리스 님의 전언은 확실히 전했습니다. 머지않아 로스톨 성으로 와 주십시오.<end>"

## 1480143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあじゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>次の旅の準備してくるから。<line-break>町を出るときに合流するからね。<end>"
EN: "All right, Lulluantha's going to get ready for the next journey. We'll meet up when we leave town!<end>"
KO: "그럼 루루안타는 다음 여행 준비하고 올게. 도시를 나갈 때 합류할게.<end>"

## 1480144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう。<value:$28>は<line-break>ギルドで受けた仕事を達成してるから<line-break>ギルドに行けば報酬がもらえるよぉ。<line-break>だから、ロストールに行くんだったら<line-break>ギルドに行った方がいいよ。<line-break>じゃあ、またあとでねぇ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, right! You've finished that job you took from the Guild, <value:$28>, so you can collect your reward there. If you're going to Rostorl, you should stop by the Guild. See you later!<end>"
KO: "맞다 맞다. <value:$28>은 길드에서 받은 일을 끝냈으니까 길드에 가면 보수를 받을 수 있어어. 그러니까 로스톨에 갈 거면 길드에도 들르는 게 좋아. 그럼 나중에 봐아.<end>"

## 1480145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　なんだ、お前は？<line-break>お城に何か用があるのか？　　　　<end>"
EN: "Hm? Who are you? Do you have business at the castle?<end>"
KO: "응? 뭐냐, 너는? 성에 무슨 볼일이라도 있나?<end>"

## 1480146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子だと？<line-break>…そう言えば、<line-break>お前の顔には見覚えがあるな。　　<line-break>お前が訪ねてくるという話は<line-break>聞いている。エリス様がお待ちだ。<line-break>ついてこい。<end>"
EN: "Flint's child? Come to think of it, I do recognize your face. I was told you'd be coming. Lady Eris is waiting. Follow me.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 자식이라고?…그러고 보니, 네 얼굴은 본 기억이 있군. 네가 찾아올 거라는 얘기는들었다. 에리스 님이 기다리신다. 따라와.<end>"

## 1480147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0よく来た、フリントの息子<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>もはや知っていようが…<line-break>そなたの父フリントは<line-break>ただの旅の商人ではない。<line-break>我が配下の諜報員だった。<end>よく来た、フリントの娘<line-break><value:$28>よ。<line-break>もはや知っていようが…<line-break>そなたの父フリントは<line-break>ただの旅の商人ではない。<line-break>我が配下の諜報員だった。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Welcome, <value:$28>, son of Flint. You may already know this, but your father was no mere traveling merchant. He was a spy in my service.<end>Welcome, <value:$28>, daughter of Flint. You may already know this, but your father was no mere traveling merchant. He was a spy in my service.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0잘 왔다, 플린트의 아들 <value:$28>여. 이미 알고 있겠지만… 네 아버지 플린트는 평범한 여행 상인이 아니다. 내 휘하의 첩보원이었다.<end>잘 왔다, 플린트의 딸 <value:$28>여. 이미 알고 있겠지만… 네 아버지 플린트는 평범한 여행 상인이 아니다. 내 휘하의 첩보원이었다.<end>"

## 1480148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回は、ノーブルの代官ボルボラから<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンの出生の秘密に<line-break>関わる手紙を運んでくる途中だった。<line-break>そのことを私の口から伝えておくのが<line-break>筋だと思い、こうして呼んだのだ。<line-break>そなたの父には世話になったゆえな。<end>"
EN: "He had been on his way here carrying a letter from Bolbora, the governor of Noble, concerning the secret surrounding the birth of Lemghon, Duke of Elienai. I summoned you because I thought it only right to tell you myself. I owed your father a great deal.<end>"
KO: "이번에는 노블의 대관 볼보라에게서 엘리에나이 공 레무온의 출생 비밀과 관련된 편지를 옮겨 오는 중이었다. 그 사실을 내 입으로 전하는 게 도리라 생각해 이렇게 부른 것이다. 그대의 아버지에게 신세를 졌으니 말이다.<end>"

## 1480149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて、テジャワの変で<line-break>救ってくれて以来、久しく<line-break>フリントは私によく仕えてくれた。<line-break>フリントのことは<line-break>心から残念に思っている。<line-break>そなたには気を落とすなとも言えぬ。<end>"
EN: "Flint served me faithfully for many years, ever since he saved me during the Tejava Incident. What happened to Flint pains me deeply. I can hardly tell you to keep your spirits up.<end>"
KO: "예전에 데자와의 변에서 나를 구해 준 뒤로 플린트는 오랫동안 나를 잘 섬겨 주었다. 플린트의 일은 진심으로 안타깝게 생각한다. 네게 낙담하지 말라고도 할 수 없겠구나.<end>"

## 1480150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたは目の輝きが他人とは違う。<line-break>目の輝きは魂の輝きを示す。<line-break>そなたはよき魂を持っているようだ。<line-break>その魂を、今回のことで<line-break>曇らせぬようにしてほしい。<line-break>ただ、それを願うばかりだ。<end>"
EN: "The light in your eyes is unlike anyone else's. The light in one's eyes reveals the light of the soul. Yours seems to be a good soul. I hope you will not let what has happened cloud it. That is my only wish.<end>"
KO: "그대는 눈빛이 남들과 다르다. 눈빛은 영혼의 빛을 드러낸다. 그대는 좋은 영혼을 지닌 것 같구나. 이번 일로 그 영혼이 흐려지지 않기를 바란다. 그저 그것만을 바라노라.<end>"

## 1480151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わざわざ、すまなかったな。<line-break>そなたの元気な姿を見ることができて<line-break>安心した。<line-break>さがってよい。<line-break>フリントの子<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "Forgive me for summoning you all this way. I am relieved to see you well. You may go, <value:$28>, child of Flint.<end>"
KO: "일부러 와 줘서 고맙다. 네 건강한 모습을 보니 안심이 되는구나. 물러가도 좋다. 플린트의 아이 <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 1480152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子<value:$28>よ。<line-break>父のように私に仕えろとは言わぬ。<line-break>だが、時には私の所に来るとよい。<line-break>門衛には、そなたのことは<line-break>申し伝えておく。<line-break>門衛に話をすれば通れるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, child of Flint. I will not ask you to serve me as your father did. But you should come to see me from time to time. I will leave word at the gate. Speak to a guard there, and you should be admitted.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 아이 <value:$28>여. 아버지처럼 나를 섬기라고 하진 않겠다. 하지만 가끔은 내게 오도록 해라. 문지기에게 네 이야기는 해 두겠다. 문지기에게 말하면 통과할 수 있을 것이다.<end>"

## 1480153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、あんた、ゼネテスさんの連れの<line-break>…たしか、<value:$28>だったな。<line-break>　<line-break>子供たちが戻ってきたって、依頼主が<line-break>言っていたよ。初仕事で見事に<line-break>事件を解決してくれたみたいだな。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you're the one traveling with Xenetes... <value:$28>, wasn't it? The client told me the children are back. Sounds like you did a fine job sorting everything out on your very first assignment.<end>"
KO: "오, 자네 제네테스 씨 일행의… 분명 <value:$28>였지. 아이들이 돌아왔다고 의뢰인이 말하더군. 첫 일에서 멋지게 사건을 해결한 모양이야.<end>"

## 1480154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは報酬の５００ギアだ。<line-break>ゼネテスさんから、全部あんたに　　<line-break>渡すように言われている。<line-break>受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Here's your reward: 500 Gea. Xenetes told me to give the whole amount to you. Go on, take it.<end>"
KO: "이건 보수 500기어다. 제네테스 씨가 전부 당신에게 주라고 했어. 받아 줘.<end>"

## 1480155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec148-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>次の仕事も用意しておくから<line-break>また今度も頼んだぞ。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. I'll have another job lined up for you, so I'll be counting on you again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>. 다음 일도 마련해 둘 테니 다음에도 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1490001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…富める者はこう言う。　　　　　　<line-break>自分には金があるから大丈夫だ。<line-break>金は他人が自分を敬うようにする。<line-break>金が自分を守る護衛を雇ってくれる。　<line-break>今日飢えないのも金があればこそだ。<line-break>今日凍えないのも金があればこそだ。<end>"
EN: "The wealthy say this: “I have money, so I have nothing to fear. Money makes others respect me. Money hires guards to protect me. Because I have money, I will not go hungry today. Because I have money, I will not freeze today.”<end>"
KO: "…부유한 자는 이렇게 말한다. 내게는 돈이 있으니 괜찮다. 돈은 남들이 나를 존경하게 한다. 돈은 나를 지킬 호위를 고용해 준다. 오늘 굶지 않는 것도 돈 덕분이고, 오늘 얼어 죽지 않는 것도 돈 덕분이다.<end>"

## 1490002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その者は神の存在を忘れている。　　　<line-break>神がその者を敬わせ、危険や飢えや<line-break>凍えから守っていることを忘れている。<line-break>思い上がり、神を忘れ、すべての加護を<line-break>金と、金を持つ己の力のおかげだと<line-break>傲慢にも考えている。<end>"
EN: "Such a person has forgotten that God exists―that it is God who makes others show respect and protects that person from danger, hunger, and cold. Swollen with pride and forgetful of God, such a person arrogantly credits every blessing to money and to the power of possessing it.<end>"
KO: "그자는 신의 존재를 잊고 있다. 신이 다른 이로 하여금 그자를 존경하게 하고, 위험과 굶주림과 추위에서 지켜 주고 있다는 사실을 잊고 있다. 교만해져 신을 잊고, 모든 가호가 돈과 돈을 가진 자기 힘 덕분이라고 오만하게 생각한다.<end>"

## 1490003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神はその傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<line-break>傲慢を捨てよ。すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>ただ、真の神にのみ、すがるがよい。<end>"
EN: "God shall shatter that pride. Cast off your arrogance. Cast aside everything. Place your faith in the one true God alone.<end>"
KO: "신은 그 오만을 부술 것이다. 오만을 버려라. 모든 것을 내던져라. 오직 진정한 신에게만 의지하라.<end>"

## 1490004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "非市民どもの集会は禁じられておる。<line-break>散れ！<end>"
EN: "Gatherings of noncitizens are forbidden. Disperse!<end>"
KO: "비시민들의 집회는 금지되어 있다. 흩어져라!<end>"

## 1490005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは神懸かった神聖王サラミスが　　<line-break>商業を禁止したときに、<line-break>追放された商人たちが建てた町だ。<line-break>だから、このリベルダムの市民は<line-break>お前のような神懸かりを見ると<line-break>ムカムカするのだ。さっさと出て行け！<end>"
EN: "This town was built by merchants banished when the fanatical Holy King Salamis outlawed trade. That's why the citizens of Liberdam can't stand the sight of fanatics like you. Now get out!<end>"
KO: "여긴 신들린 성왕 살라미스가 상업을 금지했을 때 추방된 상인들이 세운 도시다. 그래서 이 리벨덤 시민들은 너 같은 신들린 자를 보면 짜증이 나는 거다. 얼른 나가!<end>"

## 1490006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな…。<line-break>己が頭上に災いが降りかかろうと<line-break>しているのに。<end>"
EN: "Foolish... with disaster about to come crashing down overhead.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나…. 자기 머리 위로 재앙이 떨어지려 하는데도.<end>"

## 1490007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天なる神は、このリベルダムの混沌に<line-break>心を痛めておられる。<line-break>…神はリベルダムを破壊されるだろう。<line-break>この町の者の命はすべて<line-break>死神の鎌を持った者に<line-break>ゆだねられることだろう。<end>"
EN: "God in heaven grieves at the chaos in Liberdam. God shall destroy Liberdam. Every life in this town shall be left in the hands of the bearer of Death's scythe.<end>"
KO: "하늘의 신께서는 이 리벨덤의 혼돈을 안타까워하고 계신다. …신께서는 리벨덤을 파괴하실 것이다. 이 도시 사람들의 목숨은 모두 사신의 낫을 든 자에게 맡겨질 것이다.<end>"

## 1490008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん！<line-break>寝言は寝てから言うのだな！<end>"
EN: "Hmph! Save that nonsense for your sleep!<end>"
KO: "흥! 잠꼬대는 자면서 해라!<end>"

## 1490009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつを逮捕しろ！<line-break>非市民のクズどもを煽動して、<line-break>市民の財産を脅かした罪に当たる！<end>"
EN: "Arrest this one! Inciting noncitizen scum and putting citizens' property at risk is a crime!<end>"
KO: "이놈을 체포해라! 비시민 쓰레기들을 선동해 시민의 재산을 위협한 죄다!<end>"

## 1490010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助ける<end>"
EN: "Help<end>"
KO: "돕는다<end>"

## 1490011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けない<end>"
EN: "Don't help<end>"
KO: "안 도와<end>"

## 1490012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、貴様？<line-break>ええい、こいつもぶちのめせ！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you? Damn it, beat this one up too!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 네놈? 에잇, 이 녀석도 때려눕혀라!<end>"

## 1490013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ。非市民のクズが！<end>"
EN: "Ngh. Noncitizen scum!<end>"
KO: "큭. 비시민 쓰레기가!<end>"

## 1490014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、君か。<line-break>まだ、自分の力を信じてるのか。<line-break>傲慢だな。<end>"
EN: "You again. Do you still believe in your own strength? How arrogant.<end>"
KO: "또 너인가. 아직도 자신의 힘을 믿는 건가. 오만하군.<end>"

## 1490015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くい改めることだ。<line-break>力を求めても、そんなものは<line-break>さらなる力の前には無意味だ。<end>"
EN: "Repent. Whatever power you seek, it will mean nothing before a greater power.<end>"
KO: "회개하라. 힘을 구해도 그런 것은 더 큰 힘 앞에서는 무의미하다.<end>"

## 1490016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…他でもない僕が言うんだ。<line-break>間違いない。<end>"
EN: "Take it from me. There can be no doubt.<end>"
KO: "…다른 누구도 아닌 내가 하는 말이야. 틀림없어.<end>"

## 1490017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世話になったな。<end>"
EN: "I owe you for that.<end>"
KO: "신세 졌군.<end>"

## 1490018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…大きなお世話だ。<line-break>放っておいてくれても<line-break>大丈夫だった。<end>"
EN: "You needn't have bothered. I would have been fine on my own.<end>"
KO: "…쓸데없는 참견이야. 내버려뒀어도 괜찮았어.<end>"

## 1490019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傲慢だな、君は。<line-break>自分の力を信じている。<line-break>金を信じているこの町のゲスと一緒だ。<end>"
EN: "You're arrogant. You believe in your own strength. You're just like the scum in this town who put their faith in money.<end>"
KO: "오만하군, 너는. 자신의 힘을 믿고 있어. 돈을 믿는 이 도시의 천한 자들과 똑같아.<end>"

## 1490020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の名前？<line-break>それが君に何の関係がある？<end>"
EN: "My name? What does that have to do with you?<end>"
KO: "내 이름? 그게 너랑 무슨 상관이지?<end>"

## 1490021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスだ。<line-break>救世主エルファス。<end>"
EN: "Elfas. Elfas the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스다. 구세주 엘파스.<end>"

## 1490022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>それが君の名か？<line-break>そんなこと僕に教えてどうするんだ？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Is that your name? What do you expect me to do with that?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그게 네 이름인가? 그걸 나한테 알려서 어쩌려고?<end>"

## 1490023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫か？<end>"
EN: "Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "괜찮아?<end>"

## 1490024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ、自分の力を信じてるのか。<line-break>しかも、やられているんだから、<line-break>愚かと言うべきか。<end>"
EN: "Do you still believe in your own strength? After being beaten like this, perhaps I should call you foolish instead.<end>"
KO: "아직도 자기 힘을 믿는 건가. 게다가 이미 당하고 있으니 어리석다고 해야겠군.<end>"

## 1490025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かであることは傲慢であるよりは<line-break>救われる。ほめているんだ。<end>"
EN: "There is more hope for a fool than for the arrogant. That was a compliment.<end>"
KO: "어리석은 건 오만한 것보다는 구원받을 여지가 있지. 칭찬하는 거다.<end>"

## 1490026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらのことなら、片は付いた。<line-break>神の力が、君たちも救ってくれた。<line-break>　<line-break>力を信じても無駄だ。<line-break>力は、より大きな力に<line-break>叩き伏せられるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "That lot has been dealt with. God's power saved you as well. Putting your faith in strength is futile. Strength is only ever crushed by greater strength.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 처리했다. 신의 힘이 너희도 구해 줬지. 힘을 믿어 봐야 소용없어. 힘은 더 큰 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が言うんだから間違いはない。　<line-break>ただ至上なるものは<line-break>神の力のみだ。<end>"
EN: "Take it from me. There can be no doubt. The power of God alone is supreme.<end>"
KO: "내가 말하는 거니 틀림없다. 오직 지고한 것은 신의 힘뿐이다.<end>"

## 1490028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気がついたか？<line-break>放っておいてくれて大丈夫だったのに、<line-break>…大きなお世話だ。<end>"
EN: "You're awake? I would have been fine on my own. You needn't have bothered.<end>"
KO: "정신이 들었나? 그냥 내버려 둬도 괜찮았는데, …쓸데없는 참견이군.<end>"

## 1490029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傲慢だな、君は。<line-break>自分の力を信じている。<line-break>金を信じているこの町のゲスと一緒だ。<end>"
EN: "You're arrogant. You believe in your own strength. You're just like the scum in this town who put their faith in money.<end>"
KO: "오만하군, 너는. 자신의 힘을 믿고 있어. 돈을 믿는 이 도시의 천한 자들과 똑같아.<end>"

## 1490030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の名前？<line-break>それが君になんの関係がある？<end>"
EN: "My name? What does that have to do with you?<end>"
KO: "내 이름? 그게 너랑 무슨 상관이지?<end>"

## 1490031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスだ。<line-break>救世主エルファス。<end>"
EN: "Elfas. Elfas the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스다. 구세주 엘파스.<end>"

## 1490032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>それが君の名？<line-break>そんなこと僕に知らせてどうする？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... That's your name? Why tell me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그게 네 이름이야? 그런 걸 내게 알려서 어쩌겠다는 거지?<end>"

## 1490033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神が私を遣わして、お前たちに<line-break>警告しているというのに、<line-break>お前たちには神の慈悲も通じないのか？<end>"
EN: "God sent me to warn you, yet even His mercy cannot reach you?<end>"
KO: "신께서 나를 보내 너희에게 경고하고 계신데도 너희에겐 신의 자비조차 통하지 않는가?<end>"

## 1490034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわっ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa!<end>"
KO: "우왓!<end>"

## 1490035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破滅の日は近い。ただ祈るがいい。<line-break>神が遣わした私を信じるがいい。<line-break>さもなくば、この世界は滅ぶ。<end>"
EN: "The day of ruin draws near. Pray. Believe in me, whom God has sent. Otherwise, this world shall perish.<end>"
KO: "파멸의 날은 가깝다. 그저 기도하라. 신께서 보내신 나를 믿어라. 그렇지 않으면 이 세계는 멸망한다.<end>"

## 1490036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだ？　どこへ行ったのだ？<end>"

## 1490037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアは愚か者だ。<line-break>ただ己の力を信じている。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>彼は神の存在を忘れている。<line-break>神を忘れ、すべての加護を<line-break>己の力だと思い上がっている。<end>"
EN: "Emperor Nemea is a fool. He believes only in his own strength. He has forgotten that God exists. He has forgotten God and, in his arrogance, mistakes every blessing granted him for power of his own.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아는 어리석은 자다. 오직 자신의 힘만 믿고 있지. 그는 신의 존재를 잊었다. 신을 잊고 모든 가호를 자신의 힘이라 착각하고 있어.<end>"

## 1490038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知るがいい。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだということを。<line-break>神は彼の傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<line-break>破滅の日に神の力が<line-break>すべての他の力を打ち砕く。<end>"
EN: "Know this: power can only be crushed by greater power. God will shatter his arrogance. On the Day of Ruin, the power of God will crush all others.<end>"
KO: "알아 두어라. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 의해 짓눌릴 뿐이라는 것을. 신은 그의 오만을 부술 것이다. 파멸의 날, 신의 힘이 다른 모든 힘을 부숴 버릴 것이다.<end>"

## 1490039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傲慢を捨てよ。すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>真の神にのみすがれ。<line-break>神の他に力はない。<end>"
EN: "Cast off your arrogance. Cast everything aside and cling only to the true God. There is no power but God's.<end>"
KO: "오만을 버려라. 모든 것을 내던져라. 참된 신만을 의지하라. 신 외에는 힘이 없다.<end>"

## 1490040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア様を侮辱することは許さん！<line-break>逮捕しろ！<end>"
EN: "I will not allow you to insult Lord Nemea! Arrest him!<end>"
KO: "네메아 님을 모욕하는 건 용서 못 한다! 체포해!<end>"

## 1490041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのゲスの名を二度と口にするな。<line-break>耳が汚れる。<end>"
EN: "Never speak that scum's name again. It fouls my ears.<end>"
KO: "그 천한 자의 이름을 두 번 다시 입에 올리지 마라. 귀가 더러워진다.<end>"

## 1490042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっ、小僧！<line-break>自分が何を言っているのか<line-break>思い知らせてやる！<end>"
EN: "Y-you little brat! I'll make you understand just what you're saying!<end>"
KO: "이, 꼬마! 네가 무슨 말을 하는지 똑똑히 알게 해 주마!<end>"

## 1490046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が告死天使の長だと知っても、<line-break>システィーナの伝道師の一員だと<line-break>知っても、君は僕を助けるのか…？<line-break>度し難い愚か者だ。<line-break>いや、むしろ警戒すべきなのか。<line-break>意図が見えなさすぎる。<end>"
EN: "Even knowing that I am the Leader of the Angels of Death, that I am one of Sistina's missionaries, you would still help me...? You are a hopeless fool. Or perhaps I should be wary of you. Your intentions are utterly inscrutable.<end>"
KO: "내가 고사천사의 수장인 걸 알고, 시스티나 전도사인 것도 알면서도 나를 구하겠다고…? 이해할 수 없는 바보군. 아니, 오히려 경계해야 하나. 의도가 너무 안 보여.<end>"

## 1490047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアの悪口を言って気が晴れたか？<end>"
EN: "Did insulting Nemea make you feel better?<end>"
KO: "네메아 욕을 하니 속이 풀렸나?<end>"

## 1490048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜネメアのことを悪く言う？<end>"
EN: "Why do you speak ill of Nemea?<end>"
KO: "왜 네메아를 나쁘게 말하지?<end>"

## 1490049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "晴れるわけがない！<end>"
EN: "Of course I don't feel better!<end>"
KO: "후련해질 리가 없어!<end>"

## 1490050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴のことを思い出すたび、<line-break>全身がくやしさに貫かれる！　<line-break>　<line-break>奴の力に恐怖したのは、<line-break>死の羽音だけじゃない。<line-break>僕だってそうだったんだ！<end>"
EN: "Every time I think of him, bitter frustration tears through me! The Wings of Death weren't the only ones terrified of his power. I was too!<end>"
KO: "그 녀석을 떠올릴 때마다 온몸이 분함에 휩싸여! 그 힘을 두려워한 건 죽음의 날갯소리만이 아니야. 나도 그랬어!<end>"

## 1490051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さんが連れ去られるのを、僕は<line-break>黙って見てることしかできなかった。<line-break>　<line-break>自分で奴を殺しに行くことも<line-break>できなかったんだ！<line-break>お前に、お前なんかに何がわかる！<end>"
EN: "I could do nothing but stand by and watch as my sister was taken away. I couldn't even go and kill him myself! What would you know? Someone like you could never understand!<end>"
KO: "누나가 끌려가는 걸 난 그저 보고만 있을 수밖에 없었어. 직접 놈을 죽이러 갈 수도 없었다고! 네가, 너 따위가 뭘 알아!<end>"

## 1490053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "当たり前だ！<end>"
EN: "Of course!<end>"
KO: "당연하지!<end>"

## 1490058

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490069

## 1490074

## 1490075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490076

## 1490077

## 1490078

## 1490079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かであることは傲慢であるよりは<line-break>救われる。ほめているんだ。<end>"

KO: "어리석은 건 오만한 것보다는 구원받을 여지가 있지. 칭찬하는 거다.<end>"

## 1490086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらのことなら、片は付いた。<line-break>神の力が、君たちも救ってくれた。<line-break>　<line-break>力を信じても無駄だ。<line-break>力は、より大きな力に<line-break>叩き伏せられるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "As for those men, they've been dealt with. God's power saved you as well. Faith in strength is futile. Power is only ever crushed by greater power.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 처리했다. 신의 힘이 너희도 구해 줬지. 힘을 믿어 봐야 소용없어. 힘은 더 큰 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が言うんだから間違いはない。　<line-break>至上なるものは神の力のみだ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Take it from me. There is no power supreme but God's.<end>"
KO: "내가 말하는 거니 틀림없어. 지고한 것은 신의 힘뿐이다.<end>"

## 1490088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
