KO: "정신이 들었나? 그냥 내버려 둬도 괜찮았는데, …쓸데없는 참견이군.<end>"

## 1490089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "傲慢だな、君は。<line-break>自分の力を信じている。<line-break>ネメアのゲスと一緒だ<end>"
EN: "How arrogant you are. You believe in your own strength, just like that scum Nemea.<end>"
KO: "오만하군, 자네는. 자기 힘을 믿고 있어. 네메아 그 쓰레기와 똑같아.<end>"

## 1490090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の名前？<line-break>それが君になんの関係がある？<end>"
EN: "My name? What does that have to do with you?<end>"
KO: "내 이름? 그게 너랑 무슨 상관이지?<end>"

## 1490091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスだ。<line-break>救世主エルファス。<end>"
EN: "Elfas. Elfas the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스다. 구세주 엘파스.<end>"

## 1490092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>それが君の名？<line-break>そんなこと僕に知らせてどうする？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Is that your name? Why tell me?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그게 네 이름이야? 그런 걸 내게 알려서 어쩌겠다는 거지?<end>"

## 1490093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神が私を遣わして、お前たちに<line-break>警告しているというのに、<line-break>お前たちには神の慈悲も通じないのか？<end>"
EN: "God sent me to warn you, yet even His mercy cannot reach you?<end>"
KO: "신께서 나를 보내 너희에게 경고하고 계신데도 너희에겐 신의 자비조차 통하지 않는가?<end>"

## 1490094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわっ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa!<end>"
KO: "우왓!<end>"

## 1490095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破滅の日は近い。ただ祈るがいい。<line-break>神が遣わした私を信じるがいい。<line-break>さもなくば、この世界は滅ぶ。<end>"
EN: "The Day of Ruin draws near. Pray, and believe in me, whom God has sent. Otherwise, this world will perish.<end>"
KO: "파멸의 날은 가깝다. 그저 기도하라. 신께서 보내신 나를 믿어라. 그렇지 않으면 이 세계는 멸망한다.<end>"

## 1490096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだ？　どこへ行ったのだ？<end>"
EN: "W-what? Where did he go?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐지? 어디로 간 거야?<end>"

## 1490097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "世界が滅ぶ日は近い。<line-break>すべてを投げだし、ただ、天なる神と<line-break>その遣わしたる我に祈るのだ…。<line-break>その日、天に魔女は怒り、<line-break>地よりは闇の巨人が吼え、<line-break>世界のすべての町、村を襲うだろう。<end>"
EN: "The end of the world is near. Cast everything aside and pray only to the God in heaven and to me, the emissary God has sent. On that day, the witch in the heavens will rage, the giant of darkness will roar from the earth, and every town and village in the world will be assailed.<end>"
KO: "세계가 멸망할 날은 가깝다. 모든 것을 내던지고 오직 하늘의 신과 그 신이 보낸 나에게 기도하라…. 그날 하늘에서는 마녀가 분노하고 땅에서는 어둠의 거인이 울부짖으며 세계의 모든 도시와 마을을 덮칠 것이다.<end>"

## 1490098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは神の下したもう罰。<line-break>すべてを投げだし、天なる神と<line-break>我に祈る者のみが救われる。<end>"
EN: "This is the punishment God has ordained. Cast everything aside. Only those who pray to the God in heaven and to me shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "이것은 신께서 내리신 벌이다. 모든 것을 내던지고 하늘의 신과 나에게 기도하는 자만이 구원받는다.<end>"

## 1490099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな日は来ない。なぜなら<line-break>そんな日が来ないように<line-break>竜王様が守ってくださるからだ。<end>"
EN: "That day will never come. The Dragon King will protect us and see that it does not.<end>"
KO: "그런 날은 오지 않는다. 그런 날이 오지 않도록 용왕님께서 지켜 주시기 때문이다.<end>"

## 1490100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "堕落した竜王に仕える神官か。<line-break>エルフの竜教神官とは珍しいな…。<end>"
EN: "A priest serving the fallen Dragon King? An Elf priest of the Dragon Faith is a rare sight.<end>"
KO: "타락한 용왕을 섬기는 신관인가. 엘프 용교 신관이라니 드물군….<end>"

## 1490101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神ノトゥーン系の偽予言者か？<line-break>世界の滅亡を予言し、不安をあおり<line-break>ひともうけしようという手合いだな。<end>"
EN: "A false prophet of the Sky God Notun, are you? One of those charlatans who prophesy the end of the world, stir up fear, and make a tidy profit from it.<end>"
KO: "천공신 노툰 계열의 가짜 예언자인가? 세계 멸망을 예언하며 불안을 부추겨 한몫 벌려는 부류로군.<end>"

## 1490102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "滅亡の日は真だ。<line-break>やがて破壊神ウルグが復活する。<line-break>貴様がそれを知らぬはずがあるまい。<end>"
EN: "The day of destruction is real. Before long, Ulugh, the God of Destruction, will rise again. You cannot possibly be unaware of that.<end>"
KO: "멸망의 날은 사실이다. 머지않아 파괴신 울그가 부활한다. 네놈이 그것을 모를 리 없겠지.<end>"

## 1490103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…！<line-break>…貴様…何者だ？<end>"
EN: "What...? Who are you?<end>"
KO: "…! …네놈… 누구냐?<end>"

## 1490104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "静まれ！<line-break>こいつは世迷い言を言っているだけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>諸君らロストール人は<line-break>敬虔な竜教徒のはず。<line-break>うろたえるな。竜王様を信じろ。<end>"
EN: "Silence! This charlatan is merely spouting nonsense. You people of Rostorl are devout followers of the Dragon Faith. Do not be shaken. Put your faith in the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "진정해라! 이자는 헛소리를 하고 있을 뿐이다. 너희 로스톨인은 독실한 용교도일 터. 동요하지 마라. 용왕님을 믿어라.<end>"

## 1490105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王は神ではない。<line-break>ただ神の名において<line-break>この世界を監視するのみ。　　<line-break>それが神になりきっているとは<line-break>いつの間にか思い上がり、<line-break>堕落したものだな。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King is no god, but merely watches over this world in God's name. Yet somewhere along the way, the Dragon King grew arrogant enough to assume the mantle of a god and fell into corruption.<end>"
KO: "용왕은 신이 아니다. 그저 신의 이름으로 이 세계를 감시할 뿐. 그것을 스스로 신이 된 것처럼 어느새 자만하고, 타락한 거군.<end>"

## 1490106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、よいだろう。<line-break>その日には、神の遣わされた我自ら、<line-break>竜王に鉄槌を下してやろう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. When that day comes, I, the emissary sent by God, shall personally bring divine judgment down upon the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 좋다. 그날에는 신이 보낸 이 내가 직접 용왕에게 철퇴를 내려 주지.<end>"

## 1490107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この…！　偽予言者が！<end>"
EN: "You... false prophet!<end>"
KO: "이…! 가짜 예언자 놈!<end>"

## 1490108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、愚かな…。<line-break>もう少しで、怒りからダークエルフに<line-break>堕ちてしまうところだった。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者よ、礼を言う。<line-break>また、会うことになるだろう。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Foolish... I nearly let my anger turn me into a Dark Elf. Bearer of the Infinite Soul, you have my thanks. We shall meet again.<end>"
KO: "훗, 어리석은… 조금만 더했으면 분노 때문에 다크엘프로 타락할 뻔했군. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여, 고맙다. 또 만나게 되겠지.<end>"

## 1490109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "諸君、竜王に逆らう者から離れるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Everyone, keep away from the one who defies the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "제군들, 용왕에게 거역하는 자에게서 떨어져라.<end>"

## 1490110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なんだ、また君か。<line-break>余計なことを…。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あのエルフは世界に対する抑圧なのだ。<line-break>うまく術が効いて、<line-break>奴を闇に葬りかけていたのに…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's you again. You had to interfere... That Elf is an oppressive force upon the world. My spell was working; I nearly consigned that Elf to darkness.<end>"
KO: "…뭐야, 또 너냐. 쓸데없는 짓을…. 그 엘프는 세계에 대한 억압이다. 술법이 잘 먹혀 놈을 어둠에 묻어 버리기 직전이었는데…<end>"

## 1490111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのエルフは世界に対する抑圧なのだ。<line-break>うまく術が効いて、奴を闇に葬りかけて<line-break>いたのに…。余計なことをしてくれた。<end>"
EN: "That Elf is an oppressive force upon the world. My spell was working; I nearly consigned that Elf to darkness. And then you had to interfere.<end>"
KO: "그 엘프는 세계에 대한 억압이다. 술법이 잘 먹혀 녀석을 어둠에 묻을 뻔했는데…. 쓸데없는 짓을 했군.<end>"

## 1490112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、いつも、あの口調じゃない。<line-break>当たり前だ。<end>"
EN: "Of course I don't always talk like that.<end>"
KO: "그래, 늘 그런 말투를 쓰는 건 아니야. 당연하지.<end>"

## 1490113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の名前？<line-break>それが君になんの関係がある？<end>"
EN: "My name? What business is that of yours?<end>"
KO: "내 이름? 그게 너랑 무슨 상관이지?<end>"

## 1490114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…エルファスだ。<line-break>救世主エルファス。<end>"
EN: "...Elfas. Elfas the Savior.<end>"
KO: "…엘파스다. 구세주 엘파스.<end>"

## 1490115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>それが君の名？<line-break>そんなこと僕に知らせてどうする？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... That's your name? What am I supposed to do with that?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그게 네 이름이야? 그런 걸 내게 알려서 어쩌겠다는 거지?<end>"

## 1490116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エ、エルファス様！<line-break>救世主のエルファス様でしょ？　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "E-Elfas! You're Elfas the Savior, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "에, 엘파스 님! 구세주 엘파스 님이시죠?<end>"

## 1490117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お願いです。助けてください。　<line-break>僕の友だちが…、<line-break>カメが死んじゃったんです。<line-break>父さんが酔っぱらって<line-break>ふんづけちゃったんだよ。<line-break>たったひとりの親友なのに…。<end>"
EN: "Please, you have to help me! My friend... my turtle died. My dad was drunk and stepped on it. It was my only friend...<end>"
KO: "부탁이에요. 도와주세요. 제 친구가… 거북이가 죽어 버렸어요. 아버지가 술에 취해 밟아 버렸어요. 하나뿐인 친구였는데….<end>"

## 1490118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんなが言ってたよ。<line-break>エルファス様は神の遣いだって、<line-break>奇跡を起こせるって…。<line-break>ボク、これから<line-break>毎日神様にお祈りします。<line-break>だからお願い。生き返らせて！<end>"
EN: "Everyone said you're a messenger from God, and that you can work miracles... I'll pray to God every day from now on. So please, bring my friend back to life!<end>"
KO: "다들 말했어. 엘파스 님은 신의 사자고 기적을 일으킬 수 있다고…. 나 이제부터 매일 신께 기도할게. 그러니까 부탁이야. 되살려 줘!<end>"

## 1490119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら、君！<line-break>その男から離れなさい！　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>その男は救世主でも<line-break>神の遣いでもない。<line-break>大ウソつきのインチキ予言者だ。<end>"
EN: "You there! Get away from that man! He's no Savior or messenger from God. He's a lying fraud of a prophet!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 너! 그 남자에게서 떨어져! 그 남자는 구세주도 신의 사자도 아니야. 새빨간 거짓말쟁이 사기 예언자다.<end>"

## 1490120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐに立ち去るんだ。<line-break>我々も手荒な真似はしたくない。<end>"
EN: "Leave at once. We don't want to use force.<end>"
KO: "당장 떠나라. 우리도 거칠게 하고 싶진 않다.<end>"

## 1490121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少年よ、家で、<line-break>その友と共に待っているがよい。<end>"
EN: "Go home, boy, and wait there with your friend.<end>"
KO: "소년이여, 집에서 그 친구와 함께 기다리고 있어라.<end>"

## 1490122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けてくれるんですね！<line-break>ありがとう、エルファス様！<end>"
EN: "You're going to help? Thank you, Elfas!<end>"
KO: "도와주시는군요! 감사합니다, 엘파스 님!<end>"

## 1490123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら、君！<end>"
EN: "Hey, you!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 자네!<end>"

## 1490124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "子供までたぶらかすつもりか。<line-break>すぐ町から出ていけと言っただろう！<end>"
EN: "Now you're deceiving children too? I told you to leave town at once!<end>"
KO: "아이까지 꾀려는 거냐. 당장 마을에서 나가라고 했을 텐데!<end>"

## 1490125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "驕れる者どもよ…<line-break>やがて神の裁きが下る。そのとき<line-break>汝の魂は引き裂かれるであろう。<end>"
EN: "You who exalt yourselves... God's judgment shall soon fall. When it does, your soul shall be torn asunder.<end>"
KO: "교만한 자들이여… 머지않아 신의 심판이 내린다. 그때 네 영혼은 찢겨 나갈 것이다.<end>"

## 1490126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなざれ言、信じるものか。<line-break>村人はだませても、<line-break>我々には通用しない。<end>"
EN: "You don't expect us to believe such nonsense. You may fool the villagers, but you won't fool us.<end>"
KO: "그런 헛소리를 믿을 것 같으냐. 마을 사람은 속여도 우리에게는 통하지 않는다.<end>"

## 1490127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けよう<end>"
EN: "Help him<end>"
KO: "도와주자<end>"

## 1490128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しばらく様子をみよう<end>"
EN: "Wait and see for now<end>"
KO: "당분간 상황을 지켜보자.<end>"

## 1490129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんだ貴様！<end>"
EN: "W-what the hell do you think you're doing?!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐냐 네놈!<end>"

## 1490130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "刃向かう気か？　容赦せんぞ！<end>"
EN: "You mean to defy us? You'll get no mercy!<end>"
KO: "덤빌 셈이냐? 용서하지 않겠다!<end>"

## 1490131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たかが兵士ふたりを相手に大乱闘か。<line-break>なんのつもりか知らないが、<line-break>やめてほしい。<line-break>騒ぎを大きくされては迷惑なんだ。<end>"
EN: "A full-scale brawl over a mere two soldiers? I don't know what you're playing at, but stop. I don't need this escalating any further.<end>"
KO: "고작 병사 둘을 상대로 대난투라니. 무슨 생각인진 몰라도 그만둬 줘. 소란이 커지면 곤란하다.<end>"

## 1490132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もっとも、僕の邪魔をするつもりなら<line-break>いつでも相手になるが…。<end>"
EN: "Of course, if you mean to stand in my way, I'll face you whenever you like...<end>"
KO: "물론 내 방해를 할 생각이라면 언제든 상대해 주겠지만…<end>"

## 1490133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君はわかっているのかい？<line-break>僕が、君の敵だということを。<end>"
EN: "Do you understand that I am your enemy?<end>"
KO: "너는 알고 있나? 내가 네 적이라는 걸.<end>"

## 1490134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たかが兵士ふたりを相手に大乱闘か。<line-break>なんのつもりか知らないが、<line-break>やめてほしい。<line-break>騒ぎを大きくされては迷惑なんだ。<end>"
EN: "A full-scale brawl over a mere two soldiers? I don't know what you're playing at, but stop. I don't need this escalating any further.<end>"
KO: "고작 병사 둘을 상대로 대난투라니. 무슨 생각인진 몰라도 그만둬 줘. 소란이 커지면 곤란하다.<end>"

## 1490135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けてくれたお礼に忠告しよう。　　<line-break>力を信じるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>いずれ、より大きな力に砕かれる。<line-break>しかし、信ずるにたる力がひとつある。<line-break>神の力だ。<line-break>覚えておくといいよ。<end>"
EN: "In return for your help, let me offer you some advice. It is foolish to put your faith in strength. Sooner or later, a greater strength will crush you. There is only one power worthy of faith: the power of God. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "구해 준 답례로 충고 하나 하지. 힘을 믿는 건 어리석은 일이야. 언젠가 더 큰 힘에 부서지게 돼. 하지만 믿을 만한 힘이 하나 있지. 신의 힘이야. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1490136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たかが兵士ふたりを相手に大乱闘か。<line-break>なんのつもりか知らないが、<line-break>やめてほしい。<end>"
EN: "A full-scale brawl over a mere two soldiers? I don't know what you're playing at, but stop.<end>"
KO: "병사 둘을 상대로 대난투인가. 무슨 생각인지 모르겠지만 그만했으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1490137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？　　<line-break>それならひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>　<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then let me offer you some advice. It is foolish to cling to power. Power is merely beaten down by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주 엘파스. 나의 말은 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1490139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Your name... I'd like to know it.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1490140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1490141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようやく気が付いたようだね。<end>"
EN: "So you're finally awake.<end>"
KO: "이제야 눈치챈 모양이네.<end>"

## 1490142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が追い払った。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君は僕が誰だか知っているはずだ。<line-break>あの程度の奴ら相手に<line-break>不覚をとるとでも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "I drove them off. You should know who I am. Did you really think the likes of them could get the better of me?<end>"
KO: "그놈들이라면 내가 쫓아냈다. 넌 내가 누군지 알고 있을 텐데. 그 정도 녀석들에게 당할 거라 생각했나?<end>"

## 1490143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、君のお節介にも慣れてきた。<line-break>でも、これから僕は忙しくなる。<line-break>そうそう付き合ってもいられないよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I'm getting used to your meddling. But I'm about to become very busy. I won't always have time to indulge you.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 네 참견에도 익숙해졌어. 하지만 앞으로 나는 바빠질 거야. 계속 어울려 줄 수도 없다고.<end>"

## 1490144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が追い払った。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君もやってくれるね。<line-break>たかが兵士ふたりを相手に大乱闘、<line-break>挙げ句に気を失うなんて…。<end>"
EN: "I drove them off. You really are something. A full-scale brawl over a mere two soldiers, only to pass out afterward...<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 쫓아냈어. 자네도 꽤 하는군. 고작 병사 둘을 상대로 대난투를 벌이고 끝내 기절하다니…<end>"

## 1490145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まあ、いい。<line-break>君がここに現れたときから<line-break>こうなるだろうと思っていたよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, never mind. I suspected it would come to this from the moment you appeared.<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 됐어. 네가 여기에 나타났을 때부터 이렇게 될 줄 알았어.<end>"

## 1490146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの手の弾圧には慣れている。<line-break>放っておいてくれればよかったのに。<end>"

KO: "그런 탄압에는 익숙해. 그냥 내버려 뒀으면 됐을 텐데.<end>"

## 1490147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 내가 해치웠어.<end>"

## 1490154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、仕方ない。<line-break>ここは退くぞ！<end>"

KO: "어, 어쩔 수 없군. 여기선 물러난다!<end>"

## 1490157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ神のみを信じよ。<line-break>すべてをなげうち<line-break>神にのみすがれ。<line-break>そして神の遣わしたる我を信じよ。<end>"
EN: "Believe in God alone. Cast everything aside and place your faith in God alone. And believe in me, whom God has sent.<end>"
KO: "오직 신만 믿어라. 모든 것을 버리고 오직 신에게 의지하라. 그리고 신이 보낸 나를 믿어라.<end>"

## 1490158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすれば、近き滅びの日、<line-break>汝は神の力により救われるだろう。<end>"
EN: "Then, on the coming day of destruction, you shall be saved by the power of God.<end>"
KO: "그리하면 다가오는 멸망의 날, 그대는 신의 힘으로 구원받을 것이다.<end>"

## 1490159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら、ここで何やってる！<line-break>やめろ、やめんか！<end>"
EN: "Hey! What are you doing here? Stop that! I said stop!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 여기서 뭐 하는 거야! 그만둬, 그만두라고!<end>"

## 1490160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人心を惑わすエセ予言者め！<line-break>二度とこの街に来るなと言ったはずだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ふん…、こうなったら<line-break>神だ、破滅だと言わぬように<line-break>その舌を引っこ抜いてやる。<end>"
EN: "You false prophet, leading people astray! I told you never to set foot in this town again. Hmph. I'll rip out your tongue so you can never speak of God or destruction again.<end>"
KO: "사람들을 현혹하는 가짜 예언자 녀석! 두 번 다시 이 거리에 오지 말라고 했을 텐데. 흥… 이렇게 된 이상 신이니 멸망이니 떠들지 못하게 그 혀를 뽑아 주마.<end>"

## 1490161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、その手を放さないか！<line-break>この方はノクサの生まれ変わり。<line-break>無礼をはたらいてはいけない！<end>"
EN: "L-let go of him! He is Noxa reborn. You must not treat him with such disrespect!<end>"
KO: "그, 그 손 놓지 못하겠나! 이분은 녹사의 환생이시다. 무례를 범해선 안 된다!<end>"

## 1490162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むっ、邪魔をするな！<line-break>引っ込んでいろ！<end>"
EN: "Gah, stay out of this! Get back!<end>"
KO: "흥, 방해하지 마라! 물러나 있어!<end>"

## 1490163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とめなければ<end>"

## 1490165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様！<line-break>痛い目にあいたいのか！<end>"

KO: "뭐냐 네놈! 호된 꼴을 당하고 싶은 거냐!<end>"

## 1490167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "으윽…. 파, 팔을 다쳤어.<end>"

## 1490169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、傷が治った！<line-break>一瞬で！<line-break>しかも、こんなにきれいに！<line-break>あ、ありがとうございます！<line-break>エルファス様！<end>"
EN: "M-my wound is gone! In an instant! And it healed so cleanly! Th-thank you, Lord Elfas!<end>"
KO: "상, 상처가 나았어! 순식간에! 게다가 이렇게 깨끗하게! 가, 감사합니다! 엘파스 님!<end>"

## 1490170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいわ！<line-break>さすが、エルファス様。<line-break>　<line-break>奇跡だわ！<line-break>エルファス様の奇跡よ！<end>"
EN: "Amazing! That's Lord Elfas for you. It's a miracle! Lord Elfas has worked a miracle!<end>"
KO: "대단해! 역시 엘파스 님이야. 기적이야! 엘파스 님의 기적이야!<end>"

## 1490171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ神を信じよ！<line-break>神の遣わしたる我を信じよ！<end>"
EN: "Believe in God alone! Believe in me, whom God has sent!<end>"
KO: "오직 신을 믿어라! 신이 보내신 나를 믿어라!<end>"

## 1490172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わからないな。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>僕の正体を知っていて、なお、<line-break>僕を助けるとは…。<line-break>まったく、君の意図は理解できない。<line-break>何を考えている…？<end>"
EN: "I don't understand. You knew who I am, and still you helped me... I cannot fathom your intentions. What are you thinking?<end>"
KO: "…모르겠군. 내 정체를 알고도 나를 돕다니…. 정말 네 의도를 이해할 수 없어. 무슨 생각이지…?<end>"

## 1490173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの手の弾圧には慣れている。　　　　<line-break>放っておいてくれれば、<line-break>すんなり片付いたのに…。<line-break>君もあいつらと変わらない。<line-break>…すべてを力で解決しようとしている。<end>"
EN: "I'm accustomed to that sort of persecution. If you had left me alone, I would have dealt with it easily enough. You're no different from them. You try to solve everything through force.<end>"
KO: "그런 탄압에는 익숙하다. 내버려 뒀다면 순조롭게 끝났을 텐데…. 너도 저들과 다를 바 없어. …모든 걸 힘으로 해결하려 하고 있군.<end>"

## 1490174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その力が身を滅ぼす時が<line-break>くるとも知らずに…。<end>"
EN: "Never knowing that one day, that power will be your undoing...<end>"
KO: "그 힘이 언젠가 자신을 멸망시킬 줄도 모르고….<end>"

## 1490175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの手の弾圧には慣れている。<line-break>放っておいてくれれば、<line-break>すんなり片付いたのに…。<end>"
EN: "I'm accustomed to that sort of persecution. If you had left me alone, I would have dealt with it easily enough.<end>"
KO: "그런 탄압에는 익숙해. 내버려 뒀으면 쉽게 끝났을 텐데….<end>"

## 1490176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が礼を言うとでも思ったのか？<line-break>哀れだな…、君は力の亡者だ。<end>"
EN: "Did you expect me to thank you? How pathetic. You're obsessed with power.<end>"
KO: "내가 감사라도 할 줄 알았나? 가엾군… 자네는 힘에 굶주린 자야.<end>"

## 1490177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？　　<line-break>それなら、礼を言う代わりに<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"

## 1490183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、見ただろ？<line-break>傷がすっかり治っちまった。<line-break>魔法でだって、こんなにきれいには治せないよ！<line-break>やっぱり、あの人は神の遣いなんだよ。<line-break>…ってことは、<line-break>…本当に破滅の日が来るのか？<end>"
EN: "Hey, you saw that, right? The wound's completely healed. Even magic couldn't have healed it that cleanly! The one who healed it really is a divine messenger. So... does that mean the day of ruin is really coming?<end>"
KO: "이봐, 봤지? 상처가 완전히 나았어. 마법으로도 이렇게 깨끗하게는 못 고쳐! 역시 그 사람은 신의 사자인가 봐. …그렇다면… 정말 파멸의 날이 오는 건가?<end>"

## 1490184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、エルファス様！<line-break>私はあなたを信じます。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Elfas! I believe in you.<end>"
KO: "아아, 엘파스 님! 저는 당신을 믿습니다.<end>"

## 1490185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わからないな。<line-break>まったく、君の意図は計り知れない。<end>"
EN: "I don't understand. Your intentions are utterly beyond me.<end>"
KO: "…모르겠군. 정말 네 의도는 헤아릴 수 없어.<end>"

## 1490186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの程度の男、何人束になろうと<line-break>僕の相手ではない。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them myself. Men of that caliber would be no match for me, no matter how many came at once.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 처리했어. 그 정도 남자는 몇 명이 덤벼도 내 상대가 안 돼.<end>"

## 1490187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのくらい君にもわかっているはずだ。<line-break>それなのに…。<end>"
EN: "Even you should know that. And yet...<end>"
KO: "그 정도는 너도 알고 있을 텐데. 그런데도….<end>"

## 1490188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それも自分の力を過信するがゆえ、か。<line-break>もはや救いようがない…。<line-break>　<line-break>とにかく、これ以上僕の手を<line-break>わずらわすのは、やめてほしい。<line-break>僕にはすることが山ほどある。<end>"
EN: "So that, too, was because you overestimate your own power. You are beyond saving. In any case, I would rather you caused me no more trouble. I have a great deal to do.<end>"
KO: "그것도 자기 힘을 과신하기 때문인가. 이제 구제할 길이 없군…. 아무튼 더 이상 내 손을 번거롭게 하지 않았으면 해. 나도 할 일이 산더미야.<end>"

## 1490189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと気が付いたか…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの手の弾圧には慣れている。<line-break>放っておいてくれればよかったのに。<end>"
EN: "So you've finally realized. I'm accustomed to that sort of persecution. You should have left me alone.<end>"
KO: "이제야 정신이 들었나…. 그런 탄압에는 익숙해. 그냥 내버려 뒀으면 됐는데.<end>"

## 1490190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君もあいつらと変わらない。<line-break>…すべてを力で解決しようとする。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them myself. You're no different from them. You try to solve everything through force.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 처리했어. 너도 그들과 다르지 않아. …모든 걸 힘으로 해결하려 하지.<end>"

## 1490191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その力が身を滅ぼす時が<line-break>くるとも知らずに…。<end>"
EN: "Never knowing that one day, that power will be your undoing...<end>"
KO: "그 힘이 언젠가 자신을 멸망시킬 줄도 모르고….<end>"

## 1490192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと気が付いたか…。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの手の弾圧には慣れている。<line-break>放っておいてくれればよかったのに。<end>"
EN: "So you've finally realized. I'm accustomed to that sort of persecution. You should have left me alone.<end>"
KO: "이제야 정신이 들었나…. 그런 탄압에는 익숙해. 그냥 내버려 뒀으면 됐는데.<end>"

## 1490193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？　　<line-break>それならひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>　<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"

KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490204

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "통행인에게 방해된다. 이런 곳에서 집회는 허가하지 않았다!<end>"

## 1490216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　お前は…！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前だな？<line-break>世界の破滅を説いて回っている<line-break>というニセ予言者は！<end>"

## 1490217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前のバカげた作り話のせいで、　　<line-break>仕事や財産を投げ出す者まで<line-break>出ているっていうじゃないか。<line-break>だが、この街で勝手な真似は許さんぞ。<end>"
EN: "I hear your ridiculous lies have even driven people to abandon their livelihoods and possessions. But you won't be doing as you please in this town!<end>"
KO: "네 터무니없는 지어낸 이야기 때문에 일이나 재산을 내던지는 자까지 나오고 있다잖아. 하지만 이 도시에서 멋대로 굴게 둘 순 없다.<end>"

## 1490218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml

## 1490219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml

## 1490220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、こいつをどうするか…？<line-break>このまま逃がすのも惜しいな。<end>"

KO: "자, 이 녀석을 어떻게 할까…? 이대로 도망치게 두기도 아깝군.<end>"

## 1490223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "領主様の前に連行してはどうでしょう？<end>"

## 1490224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは<value:$28>様、<line-break>ちょうどいいところへ！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>各地で問題を起こしている<line-break>ニセ予言者を捕らえました。<line-break>今、ご報告にあがろうと思っていたのです。<end>"

KO: "이런, <value:$28> 님! 마침 잘 오셨습니다! 각지에서 문제를 일으키던 가짜 예언자를 붙잡았습니다. 지금 보고드리러 가려던 참입니다.<end>"

## 1490230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…、は、放してやれですと！<line-break>何を言われるのですか！？<line-break>　<line-break>この男は救世主などと偽り、<line-break>ノーブルの民をたぶらかそうと<line-break>していたのですよ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you want us to release him?! What are you saying?! This man has been posing as the Savior and trying to lead the people of Noble astray!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 노, 놓아주라고요!? 무슨 말씀입니까!? 이 남자는 구세주를 사칭하며 노블 주민들을 현혹하려 했습니다!<end>"

## 1490231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうですか。<line-break>で、では、我々はこれで。<end>"
EN: "I... see. Th-then we'll be on our way.<end>"
KO: "…그렇습니까. 그, 그럼 저희는 이만.<end>"

## 1490232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ…、<line-break>手柄だと思ったのに…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I thought we'd earned ourselves some credit...<end>"
KO: "하아… 공을 세운 줄 알았는데….<end>"

## 1490233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯…、君がね…。　　<line-break>告死天使の長を突き出せば<line-break>さらに出世もできるだろうに。<line-break>だが、世界は破滅を待っている。<line-break>権力などなんの意味もない…。<end>"
EN: "The Count of Noble... So it was you. You might have risen even higher by turning in the leader of the Angels of Death. But the world awaits destruction. Power means nothing...<end>"
KO: "노블 백작… 자네였군. 고사천사의 우두머리를 넘기면 더 출세할 수도 있을 텐데. 하지만 세계는 파멸을 기다리고 있다. 권력 따윈 아무 의미도 없어…<end>"

## 1490234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それで？<line-break>僕をどうするつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "Well? What do you intend to do with me?<end>"
KO: "…그래서? 나를 어떻게 할 셈이지?<end>"

## 1490235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>たとえ君がいくたび僕を救おうとも、<line-break>　<line-break>君と僕とは、<line-break>相いれない存在だということ…、<end>"
EN: "No matter how many times you save me, you and I can never be reconciled...<end>"
KO: "………………. 네가 몇 번이나 나를 구하려 해도, 너와 나는 서로 받아들일 수 없는 존재라는 것….<end>"

## 1490236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのことは覚えていて欲しい。<end>"
EN: "I want you to remember that.<end>"
KO: "그건 기억해 줬으면 해.<end>"

## 1490237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "The Count of Noble... So it was you. What do you intend to do with me?<end>"
KO: "노블 백작… 네가 말이지…. 그래서 날 어떻게 할 생각이야?<end>"

## 1490238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕を突き出せば、もっと　　　　　<line-break>出世できるかもしれないのに。<line-break>　<line-break>…だが、世界は破滅を待っている。<line-break>権力などなんの意味もない。<end>"

## 1490239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕をどうするつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "What do you intend to do with me?<end>"

## 1490240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら、僕は行くよ。<end>"
EN: "In that case, I'll be going.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 난 갈게.<end>"

## 1490241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？　　<line-break>それなら、礼を言う代わりに<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then instead of thanking you, let me give you a word of warning. It is folly to depend on power. Power is merely crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 감사 대신 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主、<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주, 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1490243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Tell me your name.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1490244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"

## 1490245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様！<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이 녀석은 우리가 잡았어. 소중한 돈줄을 넘겨줄 수 있나!<end>"

## 1490247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "또 너인가…. 너도 나를 영주 앞에 끌고 가려는 건가?<end>"

## 1490251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう…。<line-break>それなら僕をあいつらから<line-break>救ったつもりか？<line-break>…言っておくが、<line-break>あの程度の人間にやられるほど<line-break>僕は非力ではない。<end>"

## 1490252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕から見れば、<line-break>君の方がよほど危なっかしい。<line-break>　<line-break>自分の愚かさに気づかず、<line-break>ただ破滅へと向かっている…。<end>"
EN: "From where I stand, you're in far greater danger. Blind to your own folly, you are heading straight for destruction...<end>"
KO: "내가 보기엔 네 쪽이 훨씬 위태로워. 자신의 어리석음을 깨닫지 못한 채 파멸로 향하고 있어….<end>"

## 1490260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1490261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕を助けようとしたのか…？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君は僕が誰だか知っているはずだ。<line-break>あの程度の男に<line-break>やられるわけがなかろう。<end>"

## 1490262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "第一、…僕は君の敵だ。<end>"
EN: "Besides... I am your enemy.<end>"
KO: "무엇보다… 나는 네 적이다.<end>"

## 1490263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君みたいな人間は初めてだ。<line-break>君ほど不可解な人間はね。<end>"
EN: "I've never met anyone like you. No one so utterly baffling.<end>"
KO: "너 같은 인간은 처음이야. 너만큼 이해할 수 없는 인간은 말이지.<end>"

## 1490264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml

KO: "…이제야 눈치챈 모양이네.<end>"

## 1490265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<line-break>己の力を過信するから<line-break>こうなるんだ。<line-break>でも、これでわかったろう。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them. This is what comes of overestimating one's own strength. But now you understand.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 처리했다. 자기 힘을 과신하니 이런 꼴이 되는 거지. 이제 알겠지.<end>"

## 1490266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を信じるものは<line-break>さらなる力によって叩きのめされる。<line-break>絶対なる力は、神の力のみだ。<end>"
EN: "Those who put their faith in power will be crushed by a greater power. Only the power of God is absolute.<end>"

## 1490267

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわっ！<line-break>…………。<line-break>い、いなくなった…<end>"

## 1490278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、くそぉ。怪しい術を使いやがって。<end>"
EN: "D-damn it! Using some kind of foul sorcery...<end>"
KO: "크, 젠장. 수상한 술법을 쓰다니.<end>"

## 1490279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まさか、あいつ…ほ、本物の救世主…？<end>"
EN: "No... could that be the r-real Savior...?<end>"
KO: "설마 저 녀석… 지, 진짜 구세주…?<end>"

## 1490280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "昨日、船の上で食った魚が悪かった。<line-break>毒にあたっちまったんだ。<line-break>て、手足がしびれて動かねえ。<end>"
EN: "It was that fish I ate on the ship yesterday. It poisoned me. M-my arms and legs are numb. I can't move.<end>"
KO: "어제 배에서 먹은 생선이 상했어. 독에 당했나 봐. 소, 손발이 저려서 움직일 수가 없어.<end>"

## 1490281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたぁ、しっかりしておくれよ。　　　　<line-break>あんたが船を降りたら<line-break>家族全員、路頭に迷っちまうよ。<line-break>エルファス様、お願いです。<line-break>この人の体をもと通りにしてください。<end>"
EN: "Please, stay with me! If you can't go back to sea, the whole family will be left destitute. Lord Elfas, please. Make him well again.<end>"
KO: "당신, 정신 차려요. 당신이 배에서 내리면 우리 가족 전부 길거리에 나앉아요. 엘파스 님, 부탁드립니다. 이 사람의 몸을 원래대로 돌려 주세요.<end>"

## 1490282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "汝は神を信じるか？<line-break>神にすべてをゆだねることができるか？<end>"
EN: "Dost thou believe in God? Canst thou place all in God's hands?<end>"
KO: "그대는 신을 믿는가? 신에게 모든 것을 맡길 수 있는가?<end>"

## 1490283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ…。海の上じゃ、<line-break>人間の力なんて知れたものだ。<line-break>風ひとつ操れやしねえ。<line-break>俺は生まれてこの方、<line-break>神に祈らなかった日は一日もねえ。<line-break>神に感謝しなかった日は一日もねえよ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah... Out at sea, you learn how little power people really have. We can't control so much as the wind. I've never gone a day without praying to God. Never a day without giving thanks.<end>"
KO: "그래…. 바다 위에서는 인간의 힘 따위 뻔하지. 바람 하나 마음대로 못 다뤄. 나는 태어나서 지금까지 신에게 기도하지 않은 날이 하루도 없어. 신께 감사하지 않은 날도 하루도 없고.<end>"

## 1490284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神は、…汝を救いたもう。<end>"
EN: "God shall... deliver thee.<end>"
KO: "신께서, …그대를 구원하시리라.<end>"

## 1490285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…！！<line-break>しびれが…消えた…。<end>"
EN: "The numbness... It's gone...!<end>"
KO: "…!! 저림이… 사라졌어….<end>"

## 1490286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたぁ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, darling!<end>"
KO: "당신 말이야!<end>"

## 1490287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…奇跡じゃ。<line-break>エルファス様が奇跡を起こされた！<end>"
EN: "A miracle... Lord Elfas has worked a miracle!<end>"
KO: "…기적이다. 엘파스 님께서 기적을 일으키셨다!<end>"

## 1490288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様は救世主だ！<line-break>間違いない！<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas is the Savior! There's no doubt about it!<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님은 구세주다! 틀림없어!<end>"

## 1490289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すべては神のご加護だ。　　　　　<line-break>神に祈るのだ。すべてを捨て<line-break>ただ真の神にのみすがれ。<line-break>滅びの日が近づいている。その日、<line-break>神は人々の傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<end>"
EN: "All this is by the grace of God. Pray to Him. Cast everything aside and cling to the one true God alone. The day of ruin draws near. On that day, God shall crush the arrogance of mankind.<end>"
KO: "모든 것은 신의 가호다. 신에게 기도하라. 모든 것을 버리고 오직 참된 신만 의지하라. 멸망의 날이 가까워지고 있다. 그날 신은 사람들의 오만을 산산이 부술 것이다.<end>"

## 1490290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神に祈る者のみが救われる。<line-break>神の遣わしたる我を信ずる者のみが<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Only those who pray to God shall be saved. Only those who believe in me, whom God has sent, shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "신에게 기도하는 자만이 구원받는다. 신이 보낸 나를 믿는 자만이 구원받는 것이다.<end>"

## 1490291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファスというのは貴様だな。<line-break>これ以上、勝手な真似はさせん。<end>"
EN: "So you're the one called Elfas. Your lawless behavior ends here.<end>"
KO: "엘파스라는 게 네놈이군. 더 이상 제멋대로 굴게 두지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1490292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主様に何しやがる！<line-break>おかしな真似したら<line-break>ただじゃおかねぇぞ！<end>"
EN: "What the hell are you doing to the Savior? Try anything funny and you'll answer to me!<end>"
KO: "구세주님께 무슨 짓이야! 이상한 짓 하면 가만두지 않겠어!<end>"

## 1490293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前、船乗りだな？<line-break>誰の許しで船を出せると思ってる。<line-break>港から締め出されたいのか？　え？<end>"
EN: "What did you say? You're a sailor, aren't you? Just who do you think gives you permission to sail? Would you like to be barred from the harbor? Well?<end>"
KO: "뭐라고? 너 선원이구나? 누구 허락으로 배를 띄울 수 있다고 생각하지. 항구에서 쫓겨나고 싶나? 응?<end>"

## 1490294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ…、<line-break>な、なんて汚い野郎だ！<end>"
EN: "D-damn you! What a filthy bastard!<end>"
KO: "큭… 뭐, 뭐 이런 비겁한 놈이 다 있어!<end>"

## 1490295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、おやめよ！<line-break>船に乗れなくなったら元も子もないだろ。<end>"
EN: "Stop it! If they won't let you sail anymore, we'll lose everything.<end>"
KO: "그만해! 배를 못 타게 되면 다 소용없잖아.<end>"

## 1490296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふん…。<line-break>さぁ、こっちへ来い。ニセ予言者。       <line-break>　<line-break>貴様の作り話のせいで、<line-break>仕事や財産を投げ出す者も出ているんだ。<line-break>国を混乱に陥れた罪は重いぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Come along, false prophet. Your lies have already driven people to abandon their livelihoods and property. The crime of throwing the country into turmoil will cost you dearly.<end>"
KO: "흥…. 자, 이리 와라, 가짜 예언자. 네 지어낸 이야기 때문에 일과 재산을 내던지는 자까지 나오고 있다. 나라를 혼란에 빠뜨린 죄는 무겁다.<end>"

## 1490297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空を拝めるのも、今日が最後だと思え！<end>"
EN: "Take a good look at the sky. It'll be your last!<end>"
KO: "하늘을 보는 것도 오늘이 마지막인 줄 알아라!<end>"

## 1490298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けよう<end>"
EN: "Let's help.<end>"
KO: "도와주자<end>"

## 1490299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておこう<end>"
EN: "Let's stay out of it.<end>"
KO: "내버려 두자<end>"

## 1490300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おら、ヤジ馬はとっとと失せろ！<line-break>さもないと、お前らもしょっぴくぞ。<end>"
EN: "All right, you gawkers, clear out! Or we'll haul you in too.<end>"
KO: "야, 구경꾼들은 당장 꺼져! 안 그러면 너희도 잡아간다.<end>"

## 1490301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ貴様は？<line-break>消えろと言ったのが聞こえなかったか？<line-break>　<line-break>なるほど…、痛い目にあわないと<line-break>わからないようだな！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you? Didn't you hear me tell you to get lost? I see... You won't understand until you've been taught a painful lesson!<end>"
KO: "뭐냐 네놈은? 사라지라고 한 말이 안 들렸나? 그렇군… 아픈 맛을 봐야 알아듣겠나 보군!<end>"

## 1490302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶん手間取ったじゃないか。<line-break>僕が手を降せば一瞬だったのに。<line-break>　<line-break>…そもそも意図がわからない。<line-break>告死天使の長である僕に<line-break>手を貸そうとしたわけでもあるまい。<end>"
EN: "That took you long enough. I could have ended it in an instant. To begin with, I don't understand your intentions. Surely you weren't trying to help me, the leader of the Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "꽤 오래 걸렸네. 내가 손을 썼다면 한순간이었을 텐데. …애초에 의도를 모르겠어. 고사천사의 장인 나를 도우려던 것도 아닐 테고.<end>"

## 1490303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…危険だな。<line-break>意図の読めない人間は、<line-break>大それたことをしかねない。<line-break>まぁ、何をするにしろ、<line-break>人間に残された時間は少ないよ。<end>"
EN: "You're dangerous. A person whose intentions can't be read may attempt something outrageous. Well, whatever you mean to do, humanity has little time left.<end>"
KO: "…위험하군. 의도를 읽을 수 없는 인간은 터무니없는 일을 벌일 수 있어. 뭐, 뭘 하든 인간에게 남은 시간은 적지만.<end>"

## 1490304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶん手間取ったじゃないか。<line-break>僕が手を降せば一瞬だったのに…。<end>"
EN: "That took you long enough. I could have ended it in an instant...<end>"
KO: "꽤 오래 걸렸잖아. 내가 손을 썼다면 한순간이었을 텐데…<end>"

## 1490305
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のために言っているんだ。<line-break>僕に構う前に<line-break>自分の身を案じたらどうだ？<line-break>人間に残された時間は少ないんだ。<line-break>大切にした方がいいよ。<end>"
EN: "I'm saying this for your own sake. Rather than concerning yourself with me, why not worry about your own safety? Humanity has little time left. You should make the most of it.<end>"
KO: "널 위해 하는 말이야. 나한테 신경 쓰기 전에 네 몸부터 걱정하는 게 어때? 인간에게 남은 시간은 얼마 없어. 소중히 쓰는 게 좋아.<end>"

## 1490306
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "得意げな顔はやめてくれ。<line-break>礼を言えとでも言うのか？<end>"
EN: "Spare me that smug look. Do you expect me to thank you?<end>"
KO: "잘난 척하는 얼굴은 그만둬. 감사 인사라도 하라는 건가?<end>"

## 1490307
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が手を降せば一瞬だったのに…。<end>"
EN: "I could have ended it in an instant...<end>"
KO: "내가 손을 썼다면 순식간이었을 텐데….<end>"

## 1490308
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それなら、礼を言う代わりに<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then instead of thanking you, let me give you a word of warning. It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 감사 대신 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490309
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主、<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주, 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1490310
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Your name... I'd like to know it.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1490311
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1490312
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が付いたようだね。<end>"
EN: "You're awake, I see.<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이군.<end>"

## 1490313
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兵士ならもう帰った。<line-break>僕が神の遣いだという証拠を<line-break>見せてやったからね。<end>"
EN: "The soldier has already left. I presented proof that I am God's messenger.<end>"
KO: "병사라면 이미 돌아갔어. 내가 신의 사자라는 증거를 보여 줬거든.<end>"

## 1490314
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し痛い目にあわせてやっただけだ。<line-break>自分の愚かさを教えてやったのさ。<line-break>　<line-break>…それより、君の意図を知りたい。<line-break>告死天使の長である僕に<line-break>手を貸そうとしたわけでもあるまい。<end>"
EN: "I only inflicted a little pain―enough to teach a lesson in foolishness. But more importantly, I want to know your intentions. Surely you weren't trying to help me, the leader of the Angels of Death.<end>"
KO: "조금 아픈 맛을 보여 줬을 뿐이야. 자기 어리석음을 깨닫게 해 준 거지. …그보다 네 의도를 알고 싶어. 고사천사의 수장인 나를 도우려던 건 아닐 테고.<end>"

## 1490315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…危険だな。<line-break>意図の読めない人間は、<line-break>大それたことをやりかねない。<line-break>まぁ、何をするにしろ、<line-break>人間に残された時間は少ないよ。<end>"
EN: "You're dangerous. A person whose intentions can't be read may well attempt something outrageous. Still, whatever you mean to do, humanity has little time left.<end>"
KO: "…위험하군. 의도를 읽을 수 없는 인간은 터무니없는 짓을 저지를 수도 있어. 뭐, 무슨 짓을 하든 인간에게 남은 시간은 얼마 없어.<end>"

## 1490316

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が付いたようだね。<end>"
EN: "You're awake, I see.<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이군.<end>"

## 1490318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し痛い目にあわせてやっただけだ。<line-break>自分の愚かさを教えてやったのさ。<line-break>　<line-break>…僕のことは放っておいてほしい。<line-break>それより君は自分の身を案じるべきだ。<end>"

## 1490321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兵士ならもう帰った。<line-break>僕が神の遣いだという証拠を<line-break>見せてやったからね。<end>"
EN: "The soldier has already left. I presented proof that I am God's messenger.<end>"
KO: "병사라면 이미 돌아갔어. 내가 신의 사자라는 증거를 보여 줬거든.<end>"

## 1490322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し痛い目にあわせてやっただけだ。<line-break>自分の愚かさを教えてやったのさ。<line-break>…それじゃ、僕は行くよ。<end>"
EN: "I only inflicted a little pain―enough to teach a lesson in foolishness. Well then, I'll be going.<end>"
KO: "조금 따끔한 맛을 보여 줬을 뿐이야. 자기 어리석음을 가르쳐 준 거지. …그럼 난 간다.<end>"

## 1490323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それならひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>　<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then let me give you a word of warning. It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主、<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주, 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1490325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Your name... I'd like to know it.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1490326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1490327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、くそぉ。やっぱりガマンならねえ！<end>"
EN: "D-damn it! I can't stand this after all!<end>"
KO: "크, 젠장. 역시 참을 수가 없어!<end>"

## 1490328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生意気な！　こうしてくれる！<end>"
EN: "You insolent cur! Take this!<end>"
KO: "건방진! 이렇게 해 주마!<end>"

## 1490329
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おわっ！<end>"
EN: "Whoa!<end>"
KO: "우왓!<end>"

## 1490330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、何しやがる。<line-break>インチキ呪い師がぁっ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, what are you doing, you charlatan sorcerer?!<end>"
KO: "큭, 무슨 짓이야. 사기 저주술사 놈!<end>"

## 1490331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐわっ！<end>"
EN: "Gwah!<end>"
KO: "크악!<end>"

## 1490332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、妙な術を使いやがって。<line-break>いいか、そこを動くな。<line-break>逃げるんじゃないぞ！<end>"
EN: "Y-you and your strange tricks... Stay right there, you hear? Don't even think of running!<end>"
KO: "이, 이상한 술법을 쓰다니. 잘 들어, 거기서 움직이지 마. 도망치지 말라고!<end>"

## 1490333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "す、すげぇ…。<end>"
EN: "I-incredible...<end>"
KO: "대, 대단해….<end>"

## 1490334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしは、もう長く生きとるが、<line-break>は、初めて見る技じゃ！<line-break>まさに奇跡の技！！<end>"
EN: "I've lived a long life, but never have I seen such a feat! It was nothing short of miraculous!<end>"
KO: "나도 오래 살았지만 이런 기술은 처음 보는구먼! 그야말로 기적의 기술!!<end>"

## 1490335
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神を信じよ。<line-break>神が遣わしたる我を信じよ。<line-break>我を信じるもののみが救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Believe in God. Believe in me, whom God has sent. Only those who believe in me shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "신을 믿어라. 신이 보낸 나를 믿어라. 나를 믿는 자만이 구원받는다.<end>"

## 1490336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これでまた明日から船に乗れる。<line-break>こんなにうれしいことはねぇや。<end>"
EN: "I'll be back aboard ship tomorrow. Nothing could make me happier.<end>"
KO: "이제 내일부터 다시 배를 탈 수 있어. 이렇게 기쁜 일이 또 있을까.<end>"

## 1490337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、よかった。<line-break>この人が船を降りちまったら、<line-break>ただの飲んだくれだよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank goodness. If this one ever gave up sailing, there'd be nothing left but a drunk.<end>"
KO: "아, 다행이다. 이 사람이 배에서 내리기라도 하면 그냥 술주정뱅이일 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1490338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい見たか？<line-break>エルファス様の起こした奇跡を。<line-break>やっぱりあの人は本物の救世主だ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, did you see the miracle Lord Elfas worked? He really is the true Savior.<end>"
KO: "어이, 봤어? 엘파스 님이 일으킨 기적을. 역시 그분은 진짜 구세주야.<end>"

## 1490339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今やどこへ行っても<line-break>エルファス様を知らない者はない。<line-break>誰もが滅びの日を信じているよ。<end>"
EN: "These days, wherever you go, everyone knows of Lord Elfas. Everyone believes in the day of destruction.<end>"
KO: "이제 어디를 가도 엘파스 님을 모르는 사람은 없어. 모두가 멸망의 날을 믿고 있어.<end>"

## 1490340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがたや、ありがたや。<end>"
EN: "Praise be, praise be.<end>"
KO: "감사하옵니다, 감사하옵니다.<end>"

## 1490341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec149-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "静まれ！<line-break>こいつは世迷い言を言っているだけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>諸君らアミラル人は<line-break>敬虔な竜教徒のはず。<line-break>うろたえるな。竜王様を信じろ。<end>"
EN: "Silence! This charlatan is merely spouting nonsense. You people of Amiral are devout followers of the Dragon Faith. Do not be shaken. Put your faith in the Dragon King.<end>"
KO: "진정해라! 이 녀석은 헛소리를 하고 있을 뿐이다. 너희 아미랄인들은 독실한 용교도일 터. 당황하지 마라. 용왕 님을 믿어라.<end>"

## 1500001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、さっさと歩かないか！<line-break>エセ救世主め！<end>"
EN: "Hey, hurry up, you sham savior!<end>"
KO: "야, 빨리 걷지 못해! 가짜 구세주 놈!<end>"

## 1500003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、救世主様に、<line-break>ノクサの生まれ変わりであるお方に<line-break>なんてことを…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, how could you do such a thing to the Savior, the reincarnation of Nokusa...?<end>"
KO: "아아, 구세주님께, 녹사의 환생이신 분께 무슨 짓을…<end>"

## 1500004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "罰当たりな…<line-break>神よ、どうかお許しください。<end>"
EN: "Such sacrilege... God, please forgive us.<end>"
KO: "불경한…. 신이시여, 부디 용서해 주십시오.<end>"

## 1500005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この男は救世主などではないぞ。<line-break>この男の世迷い言など信じるな。<line-break>信じた者は、同罪とみなす。<line-break>頭を冷やせ！<line-break>世界が滅びるものか！<end>"
EN: "This man is no Savior. Don't believe his mad ravings. Anyone who does will be judged equally guilty. Come to your senses! The world is not going to end!<end>"
KO: "이 남자는 구세주 같은 게 아니다. 이자의 헛소리를 믿지 마라. 믿는 자도 동죄로 간주한다. 정신 차려! 세계가 멸망할 리 없다!<end>"

## 1500006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前こそ頭を冷やすがいい。<line-break>滅びの日はそこまで迫っている。<end>"
EN: "You are the one who should come to your senses. The day of destruction is nearly upon us.<end>"
KO: "너야말로 머리를 식혀라. 멸망의 날은 바로 코앞까지 다가왔다.<end>"

## 1500007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ！<line-break>貴様が救世主というのなら<line-break>その証拠を見せろ！<end>"
EN: "Silence! If you're the Savior, then prove it!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐! 네놈이 구세주라면 그 증거를 보여라!<end>"

## 1500008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "剣は貴様の喉元に向いている。<line-break>本当に神の遣いなら、<line-break>素手で剣を受け止めてみろ！<line-break>奇跡を起こしてみろ！<end>"
EN: "My sword is at your throat. If you truly are God's messenger, stop the blade with your bare hands! Show us a miracle!<end>"
KO: "검은 네 목을 겨누고 있다. 정말 신의 사자라면 맨손으로 검을 받아 봐라! 기적을 일으켜 봐라!<end>"

## 1500009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくぞっ！<end>"
EN: "Here I come!<end>"
KO: "간다!<end>"

## 1500010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けよう<end>"
EN: "Help him<end>"
KO: "도와주자<end>"

## 1500011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "放っておこう<end>"
EN: "Leave him be<end>"
KO: "내버려 두자<end>"

## 1500012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、貴様！<line-break>刃向かう気か！<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you? You mean to defy us?<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 네놈! 맞설 셈이냐!<end>"

## 1500013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕の邪魔をしたいなら<line-break>他にやり方があるだろう？<line-break>　<line-break>せっかく、神の遣いである証拠を<line-break>見せてやろうと思ったのに。<end>"
EN: "If you meant to interfere, surely you could have found another way. I was about to show them proof that I am God's messenger.<end>"
KO: "나를 방해하고 싶다면 다른 방법도 있잖아? 모처럼 신의 사자라는 증거를 보여 주려 했는데.<end>"

## 1500014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とにかく、<line-break>今度、今みたいな真似をしたら<line-break>君でも容赦はしない。<end>"
EN: "In any case, if you ever do that again, I won't show mercy―even to you.<end>"
KO: "…어쨌든 다음에 또 지금 같은 짓을 하면 너라도 봐주지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1500015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様！<line-break>おケガはありませんか？<end>"
EN: "Elfas! Are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님! 다치신 곳은 없습니까?<end>"

## 1500016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主様！<end>"
EN: "Savior!<end>"
KO: "구세주님!<end>"

## 1500017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく、神の遣いである証拠を<line-break>見せてやろうと思ったのに。<line-break>　<line-break>また余計なことをしてくれたね。<end>"
EN: "I was about to show them proof that I am God's messenger. You've interfered needlessly yet again.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 신의 사자라는 증거를 보여 주려 했는데. 또 쓸데없는 짓을 해 줬구나.<end>"

## 1500018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様！<line-break>おケガはありませんか？<end>"
EN: "Elfas! Are you hurt?<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님! 다치신 곳은 없습니까?<end>"

## 1500019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主様！<end>"
EN: "Savior!<end>"
KO: "구세주님!<end>"

## 1500020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく、神の遣いである証拠を<line-break>見せてやろうと思ったのに…。<line-break>　<line-break>余計なことをしてくれたな。<end>"
EN: "I was about to show them proof that I am God's messenger... You had no need to interfere.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 신의 사자라는 증거를 보여 주려 했는데…. 쓸데없는 짓을 했군.<end>"

## 1500021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が礼を言うとでも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "Did you think I would thank you?<end>"
KO: "내가 감사라도 할 줄 알았나?<end>"

## 1500022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それならひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then let me offer you one piece of advice. It is foolish to rely on strength. Strength can only be beaten down by greater strength.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1500023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主、<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주, 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1500024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Your name... I'd like to know it.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1500025

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1500026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様！<end>"
EN: "Elfas!<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님!<end>"

## 1500027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主様！<end>"
EN: "Savior!<end>"
KO: "구세주님!<end>"

## 1500028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようやくお目覚めか…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>君は僕の正体を知っているはずだ。<line-break>この僕があいつらに不覚をとるなど<line-break>ありえない…、違うか？<end>"
EN: "Finally awake...? You know who I truly am. Surely you don't believe that rabble could ever get the better of me... do you?<end>"
KO: "드디어 깨어났나… 자네는 내 정체를 알고 있을 텐데. 이 내가 그런 녀석들에게 당한다는 건 있을 수 없어… 그렇지?<end>"

## 1500029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかっているなら、<line-break>邪魔をするのはやめてくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>僕に挑もうというのなら話は別だが。<end>"
EN: "If you understand that, then stop interfering. Unless you mean to challenge me yourself. That would be another matter.<end>"
KO: "알고 있다면 방해하지 마. 나에게 도전하겠다면 얘기가 다르지만.<end>"

## 1500030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようやくお目覚めか…。<end>"
EN: "Finally awake...?<end>"
KO: "이제야 눈을 떴나….<end>"

## 1500031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらのことなら片付いた。<line-break>神の力が、君をも救ってくれた。<line-break>　<line-break>…言ったはずだよ。<end>"
EN: "Those men have been dealt with. God's power saved you as well. I told you, didn't I?<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들 일은 끝났어. 신의 힘이 너도 구해 줬지. …말했잖아.<end>"

## 1500032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力を信じても無駄だ。<line-break>力は、より大きな力に<line-break>叩き伏せられるだけだ。<end>"
EN: "There is no point in putting your faith in strength. Strength is merely beaten down by greater strength.<end>"
KO: "힘을 믿어 봐야 소용없다. 힘은 더 큰 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1500033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようやくお目覚めか…。<end>"
EN: "Finally awake...?<end>"
KO: "이제야 눈을 떴나….<end>"

## 1500034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらのことなら片付いた。<line-break>神の力が、君をも救ってくれた。<line-break>　<line-break>もう、あんな真似はやめてくれ。<line-break>迷惑だ…。<end>"
EN: "Those men have been dealt with. God's power saved you as well. Don't do anything like that again. It's a nuisance...<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 정리됐어. 신의 힘이 너도 구해 줬다. 이제 그런 짓은 그만해. 민폐니까….<end>"

## 1500035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それなら、ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>　<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then let me offer you one piece of advice. It is foolish to rely on strength. Strength can only be beaten down by greater strength.<end>"
KO: "자네, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이야. 힘은 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1500036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주 엘파스. 나의 말은 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1500037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml

JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "Your name... I'd like to know it.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1500038

JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1500039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "きゃー、エルファス様！<end>"
EN: "Aah! Elfas!<end>"
KO: "꺄~ 엘파스 님!<end>"

## 1500040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んでしまう！　やめてくれ。<end>"
EN: "You'll kill him! Stop!<end>"
KO: "죽어 버려! 그만둬.<end>"

## 1500041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ！　静かにしろ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up! Quiet!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐! 조용히 해!<end>"

## 1500042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにっ！<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐!?<end>"

## 1500043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぐ…、やりやがった…な。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... You... actually did it...<end>"
KO: "으윽…, 해냈구나….<end>"

## 1500044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神は怒っておいでだ。<line-break>この私を試すことは<line-break>神を試すことに他ならない。<line-break>神はその傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<line-break>傲慢を捨てよ。すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>ただ、真の神にのみ、すがるがよい。<end>"
EN: "God is angered. To test me is nothing less than to test God. God shall crush that arrogance. Cast aside your arrogance. Cast aside everything. Cling only to the true God.<end>"
KO: "신께서 노하셨다. 나를 시험하는 건 곧 신을 시험하는 것과 같다. 신은 그 오만을 부술 것이다. 오만을 버려라. 모든 것을 버려라. 오직 참된 신에게만 의지하라.<end>"

## 1500045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様は奇跡を見せてくださった。<line-break>あのお方は救世主だ。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas has shown us a miracle. He is the Savior.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님은 기적을 보여주셨다. 그분은 구세주다.<end>"

## 1500046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、エルファス様。<line-break>私は神にすべてを捧げます。<end>"
EN: "Oh, Lord Elfas. I give my all to God.<end>"
KO: "아아, 엘파스 님. 저는 신께 모든 것을 바치겠습니다.<end>"

## 1500047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様を信じるぞ。<line-break>滅びの日を生き残るために。<end>"
EN: "I'll put my faith in Lord Elfas, so I can survive the day of destruction.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님을 믿겠어. 멸망의 날을 살아남기 위해.<end>"

## 1500048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うう…<end>"
EN: "U-ugh...<end>"
KO: "으, 으으…<end>"

## 1500049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…く、くそぉ、あの野郎。<end>"
EN: "…D-damn it, that bastard.<end>"
KO: "…크, 젠장, 그 자식.<end>"

## 1500050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アキュリュースは聖なる町だ。<line-break>神の守護のもと<line-break>戦乱と破壊を免れてきた。<end>"
EN: "Aqyurius is a holy city. Under God's protection, it has been spared the ravages of war and destruction.<end>"
KO: "아큐류스는 성스러운 도시다. 신의 가호 아래 전란과 파괴를 피하며 살아왔다.<end>"

## 1500051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、人は平和だけでは飽きたらず、<line-break>神の座所を売り物にして人を集め、<line-break>金を取り、私腹を肥やしている。<line-break>神への感謝もなく、祈りもなく、<line-break>ただ己の欲望のために<line-break>神を利用している。<end>"
EN: "But peace alone was not enough for its people. They made God's dwelling place into an attraction, drawing crowds, taking their money, and lining their own pockets. They offer neither thanks nor prayers to God. They use God only to satisfy their own desires.<end>"
KO: "하지만 인간은 평화만으로 만족하지 못하고 신의 거처를 상품으로 삼아 사람을 모으고 돈을 받아 사리사욕을 채우고 있다. 신에 대한 감사도 기도도 없이 오직 자신의 욕망을 위해 신을 이용하고 있다.<end>"

## 1500052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神はその傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<line-break>アキュリュースは滅ぶであろう。<line-break>　<line-break>傲慢を捨てよ。すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>ただ、真の神にのみ、すがるがよい。<end>"
EN: "God shall crush their arrogance. Aqyurius shall perish. Cast aside your pride. Cast aside everything. Cling to the true God alone.<end>"
KO: "신은 그 오만을 부술 것이다. 아큐류스는 멸망하리라. 오만을 버려라. 모든 것을 버려라. 오직 참된 신에게만 의지하라.<end>"

## 1500053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、もう十分でしょう。<line-break>終わったなら帰ってください。<end>"
EN: "That's quite enough. If you're finished, please leave.<end>"
KO: "자, 이제 충분하겠죠. 끝났으면 돌아가 주세요.<end>"

## 1500054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな話が広まったら<line-break>せっかくの観光客が帰ってしまうよ。<end>"
EN: "If talk like that gets around, we'll lose all the tourists we went to such trouble to attract.<end>"
KO: "이런 이야기가 퍼지면 모처럼 온 관광객들이 돌아가 버려.<end>"

## 1500055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、あんたも行った行った！<end>"
EN: "Come on, you too. Off you go!<end>"
KO: "자, 당신도 어서 가!<end>"

## 1500056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "残る<end>"
EN: "Stay<end>"
KO: "남는다<end>"

## 1500057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "去る<end>"
EN: "Leave<end>"
KO: "떠난다<end>"

## 1500058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ…。<line-break>まさか、この男の言葉を信じているのかい？<line-break>　<line-break>こういうのは、たくさんいるんだ。<line-break>簡単に信じちゃダメだよ。<end>"
EN: "Phew... You don't actually believe what this man is saying, do you? There are plenty of people like him around. Don't be so quick to believe such claims.<end>"
KO: "후우…. 설마 이 남자의 말을 믿는 건 아니지? 이런 녀석은 많아. 쉽게 믿으면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1500059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、あんたも。<line-break>誰に頼まれたのか知りませんが、<line-break>いいかげんにしてくださいよ、まったく。<end>"
EN: "And you, too. I don't know who put you up to this, but enough is enough. Honestly.<end>"
KO: "자, 당신도요. 누가 부탁했는지는 모르겠지만 이제 그만 좀 하세요, 정말.<end>"

## 1500060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神の声を解さぬ愚者よ。<line-break>我は神の遣いだ。<line-break>我の言葉を信じよ。<line-break>さもなくば…、<line-break>滅びあるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "Fool, deaf to the voice of God. I am God's messenger. Believe my words. If you do not... only destruction awaits.<end>"
KO: "신의 목소리를 이해하지 못하는 어리석은 자여. 나는 신의 사자다. 내 말을 믿어라. 그렇지 않으면… 멸망뿐이다.<end>"

## 1500062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、今のは…。              <line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの少年に見つめられた瞬間、<line-break>変わり果てた町が見えたんだ。<line-break>アキュリュースの町が…。<end>"
EN: "Wh-what was that...? The moment that boy stared at me, I saw the city changed beyond recognition. The city of Aqyurius...<end>"
KO: "바, 방금 건…. 그 소년과 눈이 마주친 순간 완전히 변해 버린 마을이 보였어. 아큐류스의 마을이….<end>"

## 1500063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、気のせいだ。<line-break>この町が滅びるはずがない。<line-break>ミズチに守られたこの町が…。<end>"
EN: "No, it was just my imagination. This city could never be destroyed. Not a city protected by Mizuchi...<end>"
KO: "아니, 기분 탓이야. 이 마을이 멸망할 리 없어. 미즈치에게 지켜지는 이 마을이….<end>"

## 1500064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec150-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神の遣いか。<line-break>…まさかなぁ。<end>"
EN: "A divine messenger? No... couldn't be.<end>"
KO: "신의 사자인가. …설마.<end>"
