## 1510001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんて娘だ！<line-break>いままでこんな娘を<line-break>屋敷に置いていたとは！　　　　　<end>"
EN: "What a wretched girl! To think I've kept someone like you in my household all this time!<end>"
KO: "이 무슨 아이인가! 지금까지 이런 아이를 저택에 두고 있었다니!<end>"

## 1510002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誤解です！　信じてください。　<line-break>私、だんな様の宝石を<line-break>盗んだりしていません。<end>"
EN: "There's been a mistake! Please believe me. I never stole your jewelry, master.<end>"
KO: "오해입니다! 믿어 주세요. 저는 주인님의 보석을 훔치지 않았습니다.<end>"

## 1510003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だまれ！　お前に決まっているわ！<line-break>私の部屋に出入りできるのは<line-break>お前しかいないんだぞ。<line-break>さぁ、どこに隠した！<line-break>正直にいわんか！<end>"
EN: "Silence! It had to be you! You're the only one with access to my room. Now, where did you hide the jewelry? Tell me the truth!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐! 네놈이 분명하다! 내 방에 드나들 수 있는 건 너뿐이잖아. 자, 어디 숨겼어! 솔직히 말해!<end>"

## 1510004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知りません。<line-break>本当に知らないんです。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I don't know. I truly don't.<end>"
KO: "모릅니다. 정말 모른다고요.<end>"

## 1510005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エ、エルファス様だ！<line-break>救世主様がおいでになったぞ！<end>"
EN: "I-It's Lord Elfas! The Savior has come!<end>"
KO: "에, 엘파스 님이다! 구세주님이 오셨다!<end>"

## 1510006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、エルファス様！<line-break>どうか、お救いください！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私、だんな様の宝石に<line-break>手を触れたことなどありません！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Lord Elfas! Please, save me! I've never so much as touched my master's jewelry!<end>"
KO: "아, 엘파스 님! 부디 구해 주세요! 저는 주인님의 보석에 손댄 적이 없습니다!<end>"

## 1510007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな、誤解です！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "No! That's not true!<end>"
KO: "그런, 오해입니다!<end>"

## 1510008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんじゃ。<line-break>わしは間違っておらんぞ。<line-break>この娘は人の物を盗んだのだ。　　<end>"
EN: "W-What is this? I have made no mistake! This girl stole what belongs to someone else.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐냐. 나는 틀리지 않았어. 이 계집애가 남의 물건을 훔쳤단 말이다.<end>"

## 1510009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この娘に罪はない。<line-break>すぐに知らせが来よう。<line-break>汝は、己が過ちを知ることになる。<end>"
EN: "This girl is innocent. Word will arrive shortly, and you shall know your error.<end>"
KO: "이 아이에게 죄는 없다. 곧 소식이 올 것이다. 너는 자신의 잘못을 알게 되리라.<end>"

## 1510010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、な、な。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Wh-Wh-Wh...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐, 뭐….<end>"

## 1510011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお！<line-break>さすが救世主様だ！　<line-break>すべてお見通しだ！<end>"
EN: "Oh! The Savior truly sees all!<end>"
KO: "오오! 역시 구세주님이시다! 모든 걸 꿰뚫어 보셨다!<end>"

## 1510012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁ…、すごい！<end>"
EN: "Oh... Amazing!<end>"
KO: "와아…, 대단해!<end>"

## 1510013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…神よ、感謝します！　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "...God, thank you!<end>"
KO: "…신이여, 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1510014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この娘は罪に身を染めてはいない。<line-break>神はすべてを見通しておいでだ。<end>"
EN: "This girl is untainted by sin. God sees all.<end>"
KO: "이 소녀는 죄에 물들지 않았다. 신께서는 모든 것을 보고 계신다.<end>"

## 1510015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや！<line-break>この娘が盗んだのだ！　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それをあろうことか、<line-break>宝石をわしのポケットに忍ばせ<line-break>わしの勘違いに仕立てたのだ！<end>"
EN: "No! The girl stole it! Then, brazenly, she slipped the jewelry into my pocket to make it look as though I had made a mistake!<end>"
KO: "아니다! 이 계집이 훔친 거다! 그것도 모자라 보석을 내 주머니에 넣어 내가 착각한 것으로 꾸몄다!<end>"

## 1510016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "愚かな…。<line-break>神は怒っておいでだ。<line-break>　<line-break>人は思い上がり、神を忘れ、<line-break>すべての加護を金と、金を生んだ己の力<line-break>によるものと考えている。<end>"
EN: "Foolish... God is angered. In their conceit, men forget God and imagine that every blessing comes from money―and from their own power to acquire it.<end>"
KO: "어리석구나…. 신께서 노하셨다. 인간은 교만해져 신을 잊고, 모든 가호를 돈과 그 돈을 만든 자신의 힘 덕이라 여기고 있다.<end>"

## 1510017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神はその傲慢を打ち砕くだろう。<line-break>傲慢を捨てよ。すべてを投げ捨てよ。<line-break>ただ、真の神にのみ、すがるがよい。<end>"
EN: "God shall shatter that arrogance. Cast off your pride. Cast everything aside, and cleave only to the true God.<end>"
KO: "신은 그 오만을 부술 것이다. 오만을 버려라. 모든 것을 내던져라. 오직 진정한 신에게만 의지하라.<end>"

## 1510018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、こいつらを逮捕するんだ！　<line-break>この手癖の悪い娘と<line-break>エルファスとかいう予言者を！<end>"
EN: "Arrest them! That light-fingered girl and this so-called prophet Elfas!<end>"
KO: "자, 이 녀석들을 체포해! 손버릇 나쁜 이 여자애와 엘파스라는 예언자를!<end>"

## 1510019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、だんな様。<line-break>宝石、ございました。　　　　<end>"
EN: "M-Master. Your jewelry has been found.<end>"
KO: "주, 주인님. 보석이 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1510020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヤジ馬は消えろ！　見せ物じゃない！<end>"
EN: "Clear out, you gawkers! This isn't a show!<end>"
KO: "구경꾼은 꺼져! 구경거리가 아니야!<end>"

## 1510021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わわわ、わかったよ。<line-break>だから、怒らないで<end>"
EN: "A-All right, I understand! Just don't get angry.<end>"
KO: "아, 알았어. 그러니까 화내지 마.<end>"

## 1510022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーんだ！<line-break>やなおやじ！<end>"
EN: "Hmph! What a nasty man!<end>"
KO: "흥! 싫은 아저씨!<end>"

## 1510023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様か…、破滅を説いて<line-break>世間を騒がしてるっていう予言者は？<end>"
EN: "So you're the prophet who's been stirring up trouble with all this talk of doom?<end>"
KO: "네놈인가… 파멸을 떠들며 세상을 시끄럽게 한다는 예언자는?<end>"

## 1510024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなペテン師を<line-break>野放しにしておくな。<line-break>早いとこ牢にぶち込め！<end>"
EN: "Don't let this fraud roam free. Throw him in a cell at once!<end>"
KO: "이런 사기꾼을 내버려 두지 마. 빨리 감옥에 처넣어!<end>"

## 1510025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、おとなしくしろ！<line-break>抵抗すると痛い目をみるぞ。<end>"
EN: "Come quietly! Resist, and you'll regret it.<end>"
KO: "자, 얌전히 있어라! 저항하면 아픈 꼴을 볼 거다.<end>"

## 1510026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けよう<end>"
EN: "Help<end>"
KO: "도와주자<end>"

## 1510027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しばらく様子を見よう<end>"
EN: "Wait and see for now<end>"
KO: "당분간 상황을 지켜보자.<end>"

## 1510028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様！　何者だ！<end>"
EN: "You there! Who are you!?<end>"
KO: "네놈! 누구냐!<end>"

## 1510029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くせ者だ！　捕らえろ！<end>"
EN: "There's an intruder! Seize the intruder!<end>"
KO: "수상한 자다! 잡아라!<end>"

## 1510030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、こら。わしをおいて逃げるな！<line-break>この町の復興に<line-break>いくら金を出したと思っているんだ！<end>"
EN: "W-Wait! Don't run away and leave me here! Do you have any idea how much money I gave to rebuild this town!?<end>"
KO: "이, 이봐. 날 두고 도망가지 마! 이 마을 부흥에 내가 돈을 얼마나 쏟아부었는지 알아!<end>"

## 1510031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様。<line-break>なんとお礼を言っていいか。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私、エルファス様を信じています。<line-break>神にすべてを捧げます。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas, I cannot thank you enough. I believe in you. I shall offer everything to God.<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님. 뭐라고 감사드려야 할지…. 저는 엘파스 님을 믿습니다. 신께 모든 것을 바치겠습니다.<end>"

## 1510032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、よほど力を<line-break>振りかざしたいらしいな。<line-break>　<line-break>僕を誰だと思っている？<line-break>僕がおとなしく牢に入る<line-break>とでも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "You seem terribly eager to flaunt your strength. Who do you think I am? Did you truly expect me to go quietly to prison?<end>"
KO: "넌 정말 힘을 휘두르고 싶은 모양이군. 내가 누군지 알고 있나? 내가 얌전히 감옥에 들어갈 거라고 생각했어?<end>"

## 1510033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は自分の力を過信している。<end>"
EN: "You place too much faith in your own strength.<end>"
KO: "넌 자기 힘을 과신하고 있어.<end>"

## 1510034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、よほど力を<line-break>振りかざしたいらしいな。<line-break>　<line-break>誰も助けなんて<line-break>呼んでいないのに…。<end>"
EN: "You seem terribly eager to flaunt your strength, though no one called for help...<end>"
KO: "넌 정말 힘을 휘두르고 싶은 모양이군. 아무도 도움을 청하지 않았는데….<end>"

## 1510035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は自分の力を過信している。<end>"
EN: "You place too much faith in your own strength.<end>"
KO: "넌 자기 힘을 과신하고 있어.<end>"

## 1510036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、よほど力を<line-break>振りかざしたいらしいな。<line-break>　<line-break>誰も助けなんて<line-break>呼んでやしないのに…。<end>"
EN: "You seem terribly eager to flaunt your strength, when no one even called for help...<end>"
KO: "너는 정말 힘을 휘두르고 싶은 모양이군. 아무도 도움을 청하지 않았는데….<end>"

## 1510037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が礼を言うとでも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "Did you expect me to thank you?<end>"
KO: "내가 감사라도 할 줄 알았나?<end>"

## 1510038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それなら、礼を言う代わりに<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am...? Then instead of thanking you, let me give you a word of warning. It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 감사 대신 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1510039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主、<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주, 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1510040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "I'd like to know your name.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1510041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1510042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が付いたようだね。<end>"
EN: "So, you are awake.<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이군.<end>"

## 1510043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<line-break>君が気を失っている間にね。<line-break>　<line-break>君は僕が誰だか知っているはずだ。<line-break>この僕がおとなしく牢に入るとでも<line-break>思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them while you were unconscious. You should know who I am. Did you truly expect me to go quietly to prison?<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 내가 처리했다. 네가 정신을 잃은 사이에. 넌 내가 누군지 알 텐데, 이 내가 얌전히 감옥에 들어갈 거라 생각했나?<end>"

## 1510044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ以上、僕の手をわずらわすのは<line-break>やめてほしい。<end>"
EN: "Do not trouble me any further.<end>"
KO: "더는 내 손을 번거롭게 하지 않았으면 좋겠군.<end>"

## 1510045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気が付いたようだね。<end>"
EN: "So, you are awake.<end>"
KO: "정신이 든 모양이군.<end>"

## 1510046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<line-break>君が気を失っている間にね。<line-break>　<line-break>これ以上、僕の手をわずらわすのは<line-break>やめてほしい。<end>"

## 1510047

JP: "気が付いたようだね。<end>"

## 1510048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<line-break>君が気を失っている間にね。<line-break>　<line-break>君の無責任なお節介の礼に<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>…自分の力を過信しないことだ。<end>"

## 1510049

## 1510058

## 1510059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1510060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、我を信じよ。滅びの日は近い。<line-break>その日に我を信じる者のみが<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"

## 1510063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1510064

## 1510067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はタルテュバ・リューガ様だ。<line-break>名家リューガ家の貴族だぞ。<line-break>すぐ俺の館へ来い。俺がお前を雇う。<end>"
EN: "I am Lord Tartuba Ryuga, a noble of the illustrious House of Ryuga. Come to my manor at once. I shall employ you.<end>"
KO: "나는 타르튜바 류가 님이다. 명문 류가 가문의 귀족이라고. 당장 내 저택으로 와라. 내가 너를 고용하겠다.<end>"

## 1510069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はリューガ家の当主になりたい。<line-break>従兄弟のレムオンさえ死ねば、<line-break>奴の弟エストはクズだ。<line-break>レムオンさえ死ねば、<line-break>俺がリューガ家の当主になれる！　<line-break>クックックッ！<end>"
EN: "I want to become head of the House of Ryuga. If only my cousin Lemghon would die... His younger brother Est is worthless. Once Lemghon is dead, I can become head of the house! Heh heh heh!<end>"
KO: "난 류가 가문의 당주가 되고 싶다. 사촌 레무온만 죽으면, 그의 동생 에스트는 쓰레기야. 레무온만 죽으면 내가 류가 가문의 당주가 될 수 있어! 크크크크!<end>"

## 1510071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナと結婚するには<line-break>こっちも釣り合った身分がなければな。<line-break>王になるにふさわしい身分が…。<end>"
EN: "If I'm to marry Tiana, I'll need a station worthy of her. A station befitting a king...<end>"
KO: "티아나와 결혼하려면 나도 그에 걸맞은 신분이 있어야지. 왕이 되기에 합당한 신분이….<end>"

## 1510073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガキがぁああ！<line-break>俺の言うことを聞いてるのか！<line-break>こっちを向け。俺は大事な話をしてる！<end>"
EN: "You little brat! Are you listening to me? Turn around! I'm telling you something important!<end>"
KO: "이 자식아아! 내 말 듣고 있는 거냐! 이쪽을 봐. 중요한 이야기를 하고 있잖아!<end>"

## 1510074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっちを向く必要などない。<line-break>そばで怒鳴られているのだ。<line-break>耳があれば聞こえる。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to face you. You are shouting right beside me. Anyone with ears can hear you.<end>"
KO: "그쪽을 볼 필요도 없어. 옆에서 고함치고 있잖아. 귀가 있으면 들려.<end>"

## 1510075

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "뭐라고!<end>"

## 1510076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、見つめられていた方が、<line-break>少しは、的を射た話し方になるのか？<end>"
EN: "Or would having me stare at you make your words a little more relevant?<end>"
KO: "아니면 계속 바라보고 있어야 조금이라도 핵심을 찌르는 말을 할 수 있나?<end>"

## 1510077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族だろうがなんだろうが、<line-break>神の前では平等だ。話はあとにしろ。<line-break>今はこの危篤の子を治している。<end>"
EN: "Noble or otherwise, all are equal before God. Your business can wait. I am healing this child, who is at death's door.<end>"
KO: "귀족이든 뭐든 신 앞에서는 평등하다. 이야기는 나중에 해. 지금은 이 위독한 아이를 치료하고 있다.<end>"

## 1510078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "젠장! 젠장! 젠장! 귀족을, 나, 나를 우습게 보다니!<end>"

## 1510080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここでガキを手遅れにすることは簡単だ。<line-break>さっさと俺の言うことを聞け。<line-break>さもないと、お前も手遅れになるぞ！<end>"
EN: "It would be easy to make sure the brat is beyond saving. Do as I say, now―or you will be beyond saving too!<end>"
